sidebar:

import { Tabs, TabItem } from '@astrojs/starlight/components';

This is an interesting sutta that documents the Buddha's renunciation and search for liberation from suffering. It would seem to be a late text that serves the purpose of emphasising the Buddha's rejection of the practices of his teachers, and documents his own path.

The sutta is considered late because the accompanying discourse "Heap of traps" (`pāsarāsi`) contains a number of post-sectarian concepts, makes numerous references to Māra, and avoids mentioning any concepts from the early discourses:

* five objects of sensual desire (`kāmaguṇā`)
* four mundane jhānā
* four formless jhānā
* cessation of apperception and feeling processes (`saññāvedayitanirodhaṃ`)
* destruction of defilements (`āsavā`)

It is interesting that the sutta integrates the rejected practices (the bases of nothingness and neither apperception nor non-apperception) back into the path, which suggests the author was trying to reconcile the apparent contradiction of the stories of the Buddha's rejection of Āḷāra Kālāma and Uddaka son of Rāma's (which presumably predated this sutta) into a thinly disguised attempt to legitimise late practices such as the formless `jhānā` and the destruction of the defilements. The suggestion here is that the Buddha was able to transcend both practices into the "cessation of apperception and feeling processes" and thus destroy the defilements. This is of course contrary to the eightfold path which is an active process based on a reflective feedback loop.

## Introduction

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
933\. Thus heard by me —

At one time the Bhagavā was dwelling at Sāvatthī, in Jeta's Grove, Anāthapiṇḍika's Park. Then, in the morning, having dressed and taking his bowl and robes, the Bhagavā entered Sāvatthī for alms. Then several bhikkhū went to where āyasmā Ānanda was; having approached, they said this to āyasmā Ānanda:

> “It has been a long time, Āvuso Ānanda, since we heard a Dhamma talk from the Bhagavā’s own mouth. It would be good, Āvuso Ānanda, if we could hear a Dhamma talk from the Bhagavā’s own mouth.”

> “Then, venerable ones, go to the hermitage of Rammaka the brahmin; perhaps you might get to hear a Dhamma talk from the Bhagavā’s own mouth.”

“Yes, Āvuso,” those bhikkhū replied to āyasmā Ānanda.

934\. Then, after wandering for alms in Sāvatthī and returning from his alms-round after the meal, the Bhagavā addressed āyasmā Ānanda:

> “Come, Ānanda, let us go to the Eastern Park, the Palace of Migāra’s Mother, for the day’s abiding.”

> “Yes, Bhante,” āyasmā Ānanda replied to the Bhagavā. Then the Bhagavā, together with āyasmā Ānanda, went to the Eastern Park, the Palace of Migāra’s Mother, for the day’s abiding. Then, in the evening, having risen from seclusion, the Bhagavā addressed āyasmā Ānanda:

> “Come, Ānanda, let us go to the Eastern Gatehouse to bathe our limbs.”

> “Yes, Bhante,” āyasmā Ānanda replied to the Bhagavā.

935\. Then the Bhagavā, together with āyasmā Ānanda, went to the Eastern Gatehouse to bathe his limbs. Having bathed his limbs at the Eastern Gatehouse, and having come out, he stood there, dressed in a single robe, drying his limbs. Then āyasmā Ānanda said this to the Bhagavā:

> “Bhante, the hermitage of Rammaka the brahmin is not far away. Delightful, Bhante, is the hermitage of Rammaka the brahmin; charming, Bhante, is the hermitage of Rammaka the brahmin. It would be good, Bhante, if the Bhagavā would go to the hermitage of Rammaka the brahmin, out of compassion.”

The Bhagavā consented by silence.

936\. Then the Bhagavā went to the hermitage of Rammaka the brahmin. Now at that time, several bhikkhū were sitting together in Rammaka the brahmin’s hermitage, engaged in a Dhamma talk. Then the Bhagavā stood outside the outer door, waiting for their talk to finish. Then, knowing that their talk had finished, the Bhagavā coughed and tapped on the door bar. Those bhikkhū opened the door for the Bhagavā. Then the Bhagavā entered Rammaka the brahmin’s hermitage and sat down on the prepared seat. Having sat down, the Bhagavā addressed the bhikkhū:

> “Bhikkhave, what talk were you engaged in just now, as you were sitting together? And what was the topic of conversation that was interrupted?”

> “Bhante, our Dhamma talk, which was indeed about the Bhagavā himself, was interrupted, and then the Bhagavā arrived.”

937\. “Excellent, Bhikkhave. It is indeed fitting for you, Bhikkhave, as young men of good families who have gone forth from the home life into homelessness out of faith, that you should sit together engaged in a Dhamma talk. When you are gathered together, Bhikkhave, one of two things should be done: either a Dhamma talk, or the silence of the liberated.”

938\. Bhikkhave, there are these two `pariyesanā` (quests): the quest of the liberated and the quest of the unliberated.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
933\. Evaṃ me sutaṃ—  ekaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jetavane anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme. Atha kho bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi. Atha kho sambahulā bhikkhū yenāyasmā ānando tenupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ etadavocuṃ—  “cirassutā no, āvuso ānanda, bhagavato sammukhā dhammī kathā. Sādhu mayaṃ, āvuso ānanda, labheyyāma bhagavato sammukhā dhammiṃ kathaṃ savanāyā”ti. “Tena hāyasmanto yena rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamo tenupasaṅkamatha; appeva nāma labheyyātha bhagavato sammukhā dhammiṃ kathaṃ savanāyā”ti. “Evamāvuso”ti kho te bhikkhū āyasmato ānandassa paccassosuṃ.

934\. Atha kho bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantesi—  “āyāmānanda, yena pubbārāmo migāramātupāsādo tenupasaṅkamissāma divāvihārāyā”ti. “Evaṃ, bhante”ti kho āyasmā ānando bhagavato paccassosi. Atha kho bhagavā āyasmatā ānandena saddhiṃ yena pubbārāmo migāramātupāsādo tenupasaṅkami divāvihārāya. Atha kho bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisallānā vuṭṭhito āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantesi—  “āyāmānanda, yena pubbakoṭṭhako tenupasaṅkamissāma gattāni parisiñcitun”ti. “Evaṃ, bhante”ti kho āyasmā ānando bhagavato paccassosi.

935\. Atha kho bhagavā āyasmatā ānandena saddhiṃ yena pubbakoṭṭhako tenupasaṅkami gattāni parisiñcituṃ. Pubbakoṭṭhake gattāni parisiñcitvā paccuttaritvā ekacīvaro aṭṭhāsi gattāni pubbāpayamāno. Atha kho āyasmā ānando bhagavantaṃ etadavoca—  “ayaṃ, bhante, rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamo avidūre. Ramaṇīyo, bhante, rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamo; pāsādiko, bhante, rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamo. Sādhu, bhante, bhagavā yena rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamo tenupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā”ti. Adhivāsesi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvena.

936\. Atha kho bhagavā yena rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamo tenupasaṅkami. Tena kho pana samayena sambahulā bhikkhū rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assame dhammiyā kathāya sannisinnā honti. Atha kho bhagavā bahidvārakoṭṭhake aṭṭhāsi kathāpariyosānaṃ āgamayamāno. Atha kho bhagavā kathāpariyosānaṃ viditvā ukkāsitvā aggaḷaṃ ākoṭesi. Vivariṃsu kho te bhikkhū bhagavato dvāraṃ. Atha kho bhagavā rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamaṃ pavisitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi. Nisajja kho bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi—  “kāya nuttha, bhikkhave, etarahi kathāya sannisinnā? Kā ca pana vo antarākathā vippakatā”ti? “Bhagavantameva kho no, bhante, ārabbha dhammī kathā vippakatā, atha bhagavā anuppatto”ti.

937\. “Sādhu, bhikkhave. Etaṃ kho, bhikkhave, tumhākaṃ patirūpaṃ kulaputtānaṃ saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitānaṃ yaṃ tumhe dhammiyā kathāya sannisīdeyyātha. Sannipatitānaṃ vo, bhikkhave, dvayaṃ karaṇīyaṃ—  dhammī vā kathā, ariyo vā tuṇhībhāvo.

938\. Dvemā, bhikkhave, pariyesanā—  ariyā ca pariyesanā, anariyā ca pariyesanā.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
933\. 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀫𑁂 𑀲𑀼𑀢𑀁—  𑀏𑀓𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑀬𑀁 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀸 𑀲𑀸𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀢𑀺 𑀚𑁂𑀢𑀯𑀦𑁂 𑀅𑀦𑀸𑀣𑀧𑀺𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀺𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀆𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁂. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀡𑁆𑀳𑀲𑀫𑀬𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀯𑀸𑀲𑁂𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀘𑀻𑀯𑀭𑀫𑀸𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀲𑀸𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺𑀁 𑀧𑀺𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀸𑀬 𑀧𑀸𑀯𑀺𑀲𑀺. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑀳𑀼𑀮𑀸 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀬𑁂𑀦𑀸𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀆𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀤𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀁𑀲𑀼; 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀆𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀆𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀤𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀼𑀁—  “𑀘𑀺𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼𑀢𑀸 𑀦𑁄, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄 𑀆𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀤, 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀸 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀻 𑀓𑀣𑀸. 𑀲𑀸𑀥𑀼 𑀫𑀬𑀁, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄 𑀆𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀤, 𑀮𑀪𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀫 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀸 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀁 𑀓𑀣𑀁 𑀲𑀯𑀦𑀸𑀬𑀸”𑀢𑀺. “𑀢𑁂𑀦 𑀳𑀸𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀭𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀫𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀣; 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑁂𑀯 𑀦𑀸𑀫 𑀮𑀪𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀣 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀸 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀁 𑀓𑀣𑀁 𑀲𑀯𑀦𑀸𑀬𑀸”𑀢𑀺. “𑀏𑀯𑀫𑀸𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄”𑀢𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀢𑁂 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀆𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀢𑁄 𑀆𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁄𑀲𑀼𑀁.

934\. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀸 𑀲𑀸𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀧𑀺𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀸𑀬 𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀸𑀪𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀧𑀺𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀧𑀸𑀢𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀆𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀆𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀤𑀁 𑀆𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀲𑀺—  “𑀆𑀬𑀸𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀤, 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀫𑀺𑀕𑀸𑀭𑀫𑀸𑀢𑀼𑀧𑀸𑀲𑀸𑀤𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫 𑀤𑀺𑀯𑀸𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀸𑀬𑀸”𑀢𑀺. “𑀏𑀯𑀁, 𑀪𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂”𑀢𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀆𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀆𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀤𑁄 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀢𑁄 𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁄𑀲𑀺. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀸 𑀆𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀢𑀸 𑀆𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀤𑁂𑀦 𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀺𑀁 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀫𑀺𑀕𑀸𑀭𑀫𑀸𑀢𑀼𑀧𑀸𑀲𑀸𑀤𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺 𑀤𑀺𑀯𑀸𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀸𑀬. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀸 𑀲𑀸𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀳𑀲𑀫𑀬𑀁 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀲𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀸𑀦𑀸 𑀯𑀼𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀆𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀆𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀤𑀁 𑀆𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀲𑀺—  “𑀆𑀬𑀸𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀤, 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀓𑁄𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀓𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫 𑀕𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀲𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀺𑀢𑀼𑀦𑁆”𑀢𑀺. “𑀏𑀯𑀁, 𑀪𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂”𑀢𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀆𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀆𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀤𑁄 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀢𑁄 𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁄𑀲𑀺.

935\. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀸 𑀆𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀢𑀸 𑀆𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀤𑁂𑀦 𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀺𑀁 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀓𑁄𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀓𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺 𑀕𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀲𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀺𑀢𑀼𑀁. 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀓𑁄𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀓𑁂 𑀕𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀲𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀏𑀓𑀘𑀻𑀯𑀭𑁄 𑀅𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀸𑀲𑀺 𑀕𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀧𑀬𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄 𑀆𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀆𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀤𑁄 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘—  “𑀅𑀬𑀁, 𑀪𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀭𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀫𑁄 𑀅𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀽𑀭𑁂. 𑀭𑀫𑀡𑀻𑀬𑁄, 𑀪𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀭𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀫𑁄; 𑀧𑀸𑀲𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀓𑁄, 𑀪𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀭𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀫𑁄. 𑀲𑀸𑀥𑀼, 𑀪𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀸 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀭𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀫𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀢𑀼 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀓𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀁 𑀉𑀧𑀸𑀤𑀸𑀬𑀸”𑀢𑀺. 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀯𑀸𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀺 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀸 𑀢𑀼𑀡𑁆𑀳𑀻𑀪𑀸𑀯𑁂𑀦.

936\. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀸 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀭𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀫𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺. 𑀢𑁂𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀧𑀦 𑀲𑀫𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑀳𑀼𑀮𑀸 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀭𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀫𑁂 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀓𑀣𑀸𑀬 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀺𑀲𑀺𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸 𑀳𑁄𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀸 𑀩𑀳𑀺𑀤𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀭𑀓𑁄𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀓𑁂 𑀅𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀸𑀲𑀺 𑀓𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁄𑀲𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀆𑀕𑀫𑀬𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀸 𑀓𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁄𑀲𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀍𑀅𑀁 𑀆𑀓𑁄𑀝𑁂𑀲𑀺. 𑀯𑀺𑀯𑀭𑀺𑀁𑀲𑀼 𑀔𑁄 𑀢𑁂 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀢𑁄 𑀤𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀭𑀁. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀸 𑀭𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀸𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀡𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀫𑀁 𑀧𑀯𑀺𑀲𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂 𑀆𑀲𑀦𑁂 𑀦𑀺𑀲𑀻𑀤𑀺. 𑀦𑀺𑀲𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀔𑁄 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀸 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀆𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀲𑀺—  “𑀓𑀸𑀬 𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀣, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀭𑀳𑀺 𑀓𑀣𑀸𑀬 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀺𑀲𑀺𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸? 𑀓𑀸 𑀘 𑀧𑀦 𑀯𑁄 𑀅𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀸𑀓𑀣𑀸 𑀯𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀓𑀢𑀸”𑀢𑀺? “𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑁄 𑀦𑁄, 𑀪𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀆𑀭𑀩𑁆𑀪 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀻 𑀓𑀣𑀸 𑀯𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀓𑀢𑀸, 𑀅𑀣 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄”𑀢𑀺.

937\. “𑀲𑀸𑀥𑀼, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂. 𑀏𑀢𑀁 𑀔𑁄, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀢𑀼𑀫𑁆𑀳𑀸𑀓𑀁 𑀧𑀢𑀺𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀁 𑀓𑀼𑀮𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸 𑀅𑀕𑀸𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀅𑀦𑀕𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀧𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀚𑀺𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀬𑀁 𑀢𑀼𑀫𑁆𑀳𑁂 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀓𑀣𑀸𑀬 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀺𑀲𑀻𑀤𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀣. 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀺𑀧𑀢𑀺𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀯𑁄, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀤𑁆𑀯𑀬𑀁 𑀓𑀭𑀡𑀻𑀬𑀁—  𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀻 𑀯𑀸 𑀓𑀣𑀸, 𑀅𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁄 𑀯𑀸 𑀢𑀼𑀡𑁆𑀳𑀻𑀪𑀸𑀯𑁄.

938\. 𑀤𑁆𑀯𑁂𑀫𑀸, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀦𑀸—  𑀅𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀘 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀦𑀸, 𑀅𑀦𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀘 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀦𑀸.

933.1 Evaṃ me sutaṃ—  ekaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jetavane anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme.  
⏏️(evaṃ) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚻⨀①🔽(ta)(suta) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(eka) 🚹⨀②(samaya) 🚹⨀①(bhagavant) 🚺⨀⑦(sāvatthī) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(viharati) 🚻⨀⑦(jetavana) 🚹⨀⑥(anāthapiṇḍika) 🚹⨀⑦(ārāma)  
thus | by me | heard | one | time | the Bhagavā | at Sāvatthī | dwells | in Jeta's Grove | of Anāthapiṇḍika | in the park  
Thus heard by me — At one time the Bhagavā was dwelling at Sāvatthī, in Jeta's Grove, Anāthapiṇḍika's Park.

933.2 Atha kho bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsetvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi.  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀①(bhagavant) 🚹⨀②🆎(pubbaṇha+samaya) 🔽(tvā)(nivāseti) 🚻⨀②🆎(patta+cīvara) 🔽(ya)(ādāti) 🚺⨀②(sāvatthī) 🚹⨀④(piṇḍa) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(pavisati)  
then | indeed | the Bhagavā | in the morning | having dressed | bowl and robes | taking | Sāvatthī | for alms | entered  
Then, in the morning, having dressed and taking his bowl and robes, the Bhagavā entered Sāvatthī for alms.

933.3 Atha kho sambahulā bhikkhū yenāyasmā ānando tenupasaṅkamiṃsu;  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨂①(sambahula) 🚹⨂①(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨀①(āyasmant) 🚹⨀①(ānanda) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨂🤟⏮(upasaṅkamati)  
then | indeed | several | bhikkhū | by where | Venerable | Ānanda | by there | went  
Then several bhikkhū went to where āyasmā Ānanda was;

933.4 upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ etadavocuṃ—  “cirassutā no, āvuso ānanda, bhagavato sammukhā dhammī kathā.  
🔽(tvā)(upasaṅkamati) 🚹⨀②(āyasmant) 🚹⨀②(ānanda) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨂🤟⏮(vadati) 🚺⨀①🆎(cira+suta) *️⃣👆⚧⨂⑥(ahaṃ) ⏏️(āvuso) 🚹⨀⓪(ānanda) 🚹⨀⑥(bhagavant) ⏏️(sammukhā) 🚺⨀①(dhammī) 🚺⨀①(kathā)  
having approached | Venerable | Ānanda | this | said | long since heard | by us | Āvuso | Ānanda | of the Bhagavā | from the presence | on the Dhamma | talk  
having approached, they said this to āyasmā Ānanda: “It has been a long time, Āvuso Ānanda, since we heard a Dhamma talk from the Bhagavā’s own mouth.

933.5 Sādhu mayaṃ, āvuso ānanda, labheyyāma bhagavato sammukhā dhammiṃ kathaṃ savanāyā”ti.  
⏏️(sādhu) *️⃣👆⚧⨂①(ahaṃ) ⏏️(āvuso) 🚹⨀⓪(ānanda) 🟢⨂👆⏯(labhati) 🚹⨀⑥(bhagavant) ⏏️(sammukhā) 🚺⨀②(dhammī) 🚺⨀②(kathā) 🚻⨀④(savana) ⏏️(iti)  
it would be good | we | Āvuso | Ānanda | could get | of the Bhagavā | from the presence | on the Dhamma | talk | to hear | (end quote)  
It would be good, Āvuso Ānanda, if we could hear a Dhamma talk from the Bhagavā’s own mouth.”

933.6 “Tena hāyasmanto yena rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamo tenupasaṅkamatha;  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) ⏏️(hi) 🚹⨂⓪(āyasmant) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨀⑥(rammaka) 🚹⨀⑥(brāhmaṇa) 🚹⨀①(assama) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨂🤘⏹(upasaṅkamati)  
in that case | indeed | Āyasmanto | by where | of Rammaka | the brahmin | hermitage | by there | go  
“Then, venerable ones, go to the hermitage of Rammaka the brahmin;

933.7 appeva nāma labheyyātha bhagavato sammukhā dhammiṃ kathaṃ savanāyā”ti.  
⏏️(api) ⏏️(eva) ⏏️(nāma) 🟢⨂🤘⏯(labhati) 🚹⨀⑥(bhagavant) ⏏️(sammukhā) 🚺⨀②(dhammī) 🚺⨀②(kathā) 🚻⨀④(savana) ⏏️(iti)  
perhaps | just | indeed | you might get | of the Bhagavā | from the presence | on the Dhamma | talk | to hear | (end quote)  
perhaps you might get to hear a Dhamma talk from the Bhagavā’s own mouth.”

933.8 “Evamāvuso”ti kho te bhikkhū āyasmato ānandassa paccassosuṃ.  
⏏️(evaṃ) ⏏️(āvuso) ⏏️(iti) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ta) 🚹⨂①(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀④(āyasmant) 🚹⨀④(ānanda) 🟢⨂🤟⏮(paṭissuṇāti)  
yes | Āvuso | (end quote) | indeed | those | bhikkhū | to Venerable | Ānanda | replied  
“Yes, Āvuso,” those bhikkhū replied to āyasmā Ānanda.

934.1 Atha kho bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭikkanto āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantesi—  “āyāmānanda, yena pubbārāmo migāramātupāsādo tenupasaṅkamissāma divāvihārāyā”ti.  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀①(bhagavant) 🚺⨀⑦(sāvatthī) 🚹⨀④(piṇḍa) 🔽(tvā)(carati) ⏏️(pacchābhattaṃ) 🚹⨀①🆎(piṇḍa+pāta+paṭikkanta) 🚹⨀②(āyasmant) 🚹⨀②(ānanda) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(āmanteti) 🟢⨂👆⏹(āyāti) 🚹⨀⓪(ānanda) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨀①(pubbārāma) 🚹⨀①(migāramātupāsāda) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨂👆⏭(upasaṅkamati) 🚹⨀④🆎(divā+vihāra) ⏏️(iti)  
then | indeed | the Bhagavā | in Sāvatthī | for alms | having wandered | after the meal | returned from his alms-round | Venerable | Ānanda | addressed | let us go | Ānanda | by where | the Eastern Park | the Palace of Migāra's Mother | by there | we will go | for the day's abiding | (end quote)  
Then, after wandering for alms in Sāvatthī and returning from his alms-round after the meal, the Bhagavā addressed āyasmā Ānanda: “Come, Ānanda, let us go to the Eastern Park, the Palace of Migāra’s Mother, for the day’s abiding.”

934.2 “Evaṃ, bhante”ti kho āyasmā ānando bhagavato paccassosi.  
⏏️(evaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) ⏏️(iti) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀①(āyasmant) 🚹⨀①(ānanda) 🚹⨀④(bhagavant) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(paṭissuṇāti)  
yes | Bhante | (end quote) | indeed | Venerable | Ānanda | to the Bhagavā | replied  
“Yes, Bhante,” āyasmā Ānanda replied to the Bhagavā.

934.3 Atha kho bhagavā āyasmatā ānandena saddhiṃ yena pubbārāmo migāramātupāsādo tenupasaṅkami divāvihārāya.  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀①(bhagavant) 🚹⨀③(āyasmant) 🚹⨀③(ānanda) ⏏️(saddhiṃ) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨀①(pubbārāma) 🚹⨀①(migāramātupāsāda) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(upasaṅkamati) 🚹⨀④🆎(divā+vihāra)  
then | indeed | the Bhagavā | with Venerable | Ānanda | together | by where | the Eastern Park | the Palace of Migāra's Mother | by there | went | for the day's abiding  
Then the Bhagavā, together with āyasmā Ānanda, went to the Eastern Park, the Palace of Migāra’s Mother, for the day’s abiding.

934.4 Atha kho bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisallānā vuṭṭhito āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantesi—  “āyāmānanda, yena pubbakoṭṭhako tenupasaṅkamissāma gattāni parisiñcitun”ti.  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀①(bhagavant) 🚹⨀②🆎(sāyanha+samaya) 🚻⨀⑤(paṭisallāna) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(vuṭṭhita) 🚹⨀②(āyasmant) 🚹⨀②(ānanda) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(āmanteti) 🟢⨂👆⏹(āyāti) 🚹⨀⓪(ānanda) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨀①(pubbakoṭṭhaka) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨂👆⏭(upasaṅkamati) 🚻⨂②(gatta) 🔽(tuṃ)(parisiñcati) ⏏️(iti)  
then | indeed | the Bhagavā | in the evening | from seclusion | having risen | Venerable | Ānanda | addressed | let us go | Ānanda | by where | the Eastern Gatehouse | by there | we will go | limbs | to bathe | (end quote)  
Then, in the evening, having risen from seclusion, the Bhagavā addressed āyasmā Ānanda: “Come, Ānanda, let us go to the Eastern Gatehouse to bathe our limbs.”

934.5 “Evaṃ, bhante”ti kho āyasmā ānando bhagavato paccassosi.  
⏏️(evaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) ⏏️(iti) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀①(āyasmant) 🚹⨀①(ānanda) 🚹⨀④(bhagavant) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(paṭissuṇāti)  
yes | Bhante | (end quote) | indeed | Venerable | Ānanda | to the Bhagavā | replied  
“Yes, Bhante,” āyasmā Ānanda replied to the Bhagavā.

935.1 Atha kho bhagavā āyasmatā ānandena saddhiṃ yena pubbakoṭṭhako tenupasaṅkami gattāni parisiñcituṃ.  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀①(bhagavant) 🚹⨀③(āyasmant) 🚹⨀③(ānanda) ⏏️(saddhiṃ) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨀①(pubbakoṭṭhaka) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(upasaṅkamati) 🚻⨂②(gatta) 🔽(tuṃ)(parisiñcati)  
then | indeed | the Bhagavā | with Venerable | Ānanda | together | by where | the Eastern Gatehouse | by there | went | limbs | to bathe  
Then the Bhagavā, together with āyasmā Ānanda, went to the Eastern Gatehouse to bathe his limbs.

935.2 Pubbakoṭṭhake gattāni parisiñcitvā paccuttaritvā ekacīvaro aṭṭhāsi gattāni pubbāpayamāno.  
🚹⨀⑦(pubbakoṭṭhaka) 🚻⨂②(gatta) 🔽(tvā)(parisiñcati) 🔽(tvā)(paccuttarati) 🚹⨀①🆎(eka+cīvara) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(tiṭṭhati) 🚻⨂②(gatta) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(pubbāpayamāna)  
at the Eastern Gatehouse | limbs | having bathed | having come out | dressed in a single robe | stood | limbs | drying  
Having bathed his limbs at the Eastern Gatehouse, and having come out, he stood there, dressed in a single robe, drying his limbs.

935.3 Atha kho āyasmā ānando bhagavantaṃ etadavoca—  “ayaṃ, bhante, rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamo avidūre.  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀①(āyasmant) 🚹⨀①(ānanda) 🚹⨀②(bhagavant) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(vadati) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀⑥(rammaka) 🚹⨀⑥(brāhmaṇa) 🚹⨀①(assama) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(na+vidūra)  
then | indeed | Venerable | Ānanda | the Bhagavā | this | said | this | Bhante | of Rammaka | the brahmin | hermitage | not far away  
Then āyasmā Ānanda said this to the Bhagavā: “Bhante, the hermitage of Rammaka the brahmin is not far away.

935.4 Ramaṇīyo, bhante, rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamo;  
🚹⨀①🔽(anīya)(ramaṇīya) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀⑥(rammaka) 🚹⨀⑥(brāhmaṇa) 🚹⨀①(assama)  
delightful | Bhante | of Rammaka | the brahmin | hermitage  
Delightful, Bhante, is the hermitage of Rammaka the brahmin;

935.5 pāsādiko, bhante, rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamo.  
🚹⨀①🔽(ṇika)(pāsādika) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀⑥(rammaka) 🚹⨀⑥(brāhmaṇa) 🚹⨀①(assama)  
charming | Bhante | of Rammaka | the brahmin | hermitage  
charming, Bhante, is the hermitage of Rammaka the brahmin.

935.6 Sādhu, bhante, bhagavā yena rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamo tenupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā”ti.  
⏏️(sādhu) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀①(bhagavant) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨀⑥(rammaka) 🚹⨀⑥(brāhmaṇa) 🚹⨀①(assama) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨀🤟⏹(upasaṅkamati) 🚺⨀②(anukampā) ⏏️(upādāya) ⏏️(iti)  
it would be good | Bhante | the Bhagavā | by where | of Rammaka | the brahmin | hermitage | by there | may go | compassion | out of | (end quote)  
It would be good, Bhante, if the Bhagavā would go to the hermitage of Rammaka the brahmin, out of compassion.”

The Bhagavā consented by silence.

936.1 Atha kho bhagavā yena rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamo tenupasaṅkami.  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀①(bhagavant) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨀⑥(rammaka) 🚹⨀⑥(brāhmaṇa) 🚹⨀①(assama) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(upasaṅkamati)  
then | indeed | the Bhagavā | by where | of Rammaka | the brahmin | hermitage | by there | went  
Then the Bhagavā went to the hermitage of Rammaka the brahmin.

936.2 Tena kho pana samayena sambahulā bhikkhū rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assame dhammiyā kathāya sannisinnā honti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(pana) 🚹⨀③(samaya) 🚹⨂①(sambahula) 🚹⨂①(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀⑥(rammaka) 🚹⨀⑥(brāhmaṇa) 🚹⨀⑦(assama) 🚺⨀③(dhammī) 🚺⨀③(kathā) 🚹⨂①🔽(ta)(sannisinna) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(hoti)  
at that | indeed | now | time | several | bhikkhū | of Rammaka | the brahmin | in the hermitage | with a Dhamma | talk | sitting together | were  
Now at that time, several bhikkhū were sitting together in Rammaka the brahmin’s hermitage, engaged in a Dhamma talk.

936.3 Atha kho bhagavā bahidvārakoṭṭhake aṭṭhāsi kathāpariyosānaṃ āgamayamāno.  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀①(bhagavant) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(bahi+dvāra+koṭṭhaka) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(tiṭṭhati) 🚻⨀②🆎(kathā+pariyosāna) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(āgamayamāna)  
then | indeed | the Bhagavā | outside the outer door | stood | the end of the talk | waiting for  
Then the Bhagavā stood outside the outer door, waiting for their talk to finish.

936.4 Atha kho bhagavā kathāpariyosānaṃ viditvā ukkāsitvā aggaḷaṃ ākoṭesi.  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀①(bhagavant) 🚻⨀②🆎(kathā+pariyosāna) 🔽(tvā)(vidati) 🔽(tvā)(ukkāsati) 🚺⨀②(aggaḷā) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(ākoṭeti)  
then | indeed | the Bhagavā | the end of the talk | having known | having coughed | the door bar | tapped on  
Then, knowing that their talk had finished, the Bhagavā coughed and tapped on the door bar.

936.5 Vivariṃsu kho te bhikkhū bhagavato dvāraṃ.  
🟢⨂🤟⏮(vivarati) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ta) 🚹⨂①(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀④(bhagavant) 🚻⨀②(dvāra)  
opened | indeed | those | bhikkhū | for the Bhagavā | the door  
Those bhikkhū opened the door for the Bhagavā.

936.6 Atha kho bhagavā rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamaṃ pavisitvā paññatte āsane nisīdi.

It is indeed fitting for you, Bhikkhave, as young men of good families who have gone forth from the home life into homelessness out of faith, that you should sit together engaged in a Dhamma talk.

937.3 Sannipatitānaṃ vo, bhikkhave, dvayaṃ karaṇīyaṃ—  dhammī vā kathā, ariyo vā tuṇhībhāvo.

## The Quest of the Unliberated

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
939\. And what, Bhikkhave, is the quest of the unliberated? Here, Bhikkhave, someone, being liable to birth from his own doing, searches only for what is liable to birth; being liable to old age from his own doing, he searches only for what is liable to old age; being liable to sickness from his own doing, he searches only for what is liable to sickness; being liable to death from his own doing, he searches only for what is liable to death; being liable to sorrow from his own doing, he searches only for what is liable to sorrow; being liable to defilement from his own doing, he searches only for what is liable to defilement.

940\. And what, Bhikkhave, do you call liable to birth? Children and wives, Bhikkhave, are liable to birth; male and female slaves are liable to birth; goats and sheep are liable to birth; chickens and pigs are liable to birth; elephants, cattle, horses, and mares are liable to birth; gold and silver are liable to birth. For these, Bhikkhave, are acquisitions liable to birth. Entangled, infatuated, and engrossed in these, being liable to birth from his own doing, he searches only for what is liable to birth.

941\. And what, Bhikkhave, do you call liable to old age? Children and wives, Bhikkhave, are liable to old age; male and female slaves are liable to old age; goats and sheep are liable to old age; chickens and pigs are liable to old age; elephants, cattle, horses, and mares are liable to old age; gold and silver are liable to old age. For these, Bhikkhave, are acquisitions liable to old age. Entangled, infatuated, and engrossed in these, being liable to old age from his own doing, he searches only for what is liable to old age.

942\. And what, Bhikkhave, do you call liable to sickness? Children and wives, Bhikkhave, are liable to sickness; male and female slaves are liable to sickness; goats and sheep are liable to sickness; chickens and pigs are liable to sickness; elephants, cattle, horses, and mares are liable to sickness. For these, Bhikkhave, are acquisitions liable to sickness. Entangled, infatuated, and engrossed in these, being liable to sickness from his own doing, he searches only for what is liable to sickness.

943\. And what, Bhikkhave, do you call liable to death? Children and wives, Bhikkhave, are liable to death; male and female slaves are liable to death; goats and sheep are liable to death; chickens and pigs are liable to death; elephants, cattle, horses, and mares are liable to death. For these, Bhikkhave, are acquisitions liable to death. Entangled, infatuated, and engrossed in these, being liable to death from his own doing, he searches only for what is liable to death.

944\. And what, Bhikkhave, do you call liable to sorrow? Children and wives, Bhikkhave, are liable to sorrow; male and female slaves are liable to sorrow; goats and sheep are liable to sorrow; chickens and pigs are liable to sorrow; elephants, cattle, horses, and mares are liable to sorrow. For these, Bhikkhave, are acquisitions liable to sorrow. Entangled, infatuated, and engrossed in these, being liable to sorrow from his own doing, he searches only for what is liable to sorrow.

945\. And what, Bhikkhave, do you call liable to defilement? Children and wives, Bhikkhave, are liable to defilement; male and female slaves are liable to defilement; goats and sheep are liable to defilement; chickens and pigs are liable to defilement; elephants, cattle, horses, and mares are liable to defilement; gold and silver are liable to defilement. For these, Bhikkhave, are acquisitions liable to defilement. Entangled, infatuated, and engrossed in these, being liable to defilement from his own doing, he searches only for what is liable to defilement. This, Bhikkhave, is the quest of the unliberated.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
939\. Katamā ca, bhikkhave, anariyā pariyesanā? Idha, bhikkhave, ekacco attanā jātidhammo samāno jātidhammaṃyeva pariyesati, attanā jarādhammo samāno jarādhammaṃyeva pariyesati, attanā byādhidhammo samāno byādhidhammaṃyeva pariyesati, attanā maraṇadhammo samāno maraṇadhammaṃyeva pariyesati, attanā sokadhammo samāno sokadhammaṃyeva pariyesati, attanā saṃkilesadhammo samāno saṃkilesadhammaṃyeva pariyesati.

940\. Kiñca, bhikkhave, jātidhammaṃ vadetha? Puttabhariyaṃ, bhikkhave, jātidhammaṃ, dāsidāsaṃ jātidhammaṃ, ajeḷakaṃ jātidhammaṃ, kukkuṭasūkaraṃ jātidhammaṃ, hatthigavāssavaḷavaṃ jātidhammaṃ, jātarūparajataṃ jātidhammaṃ. Jātidhammā hete, bhikkhave, upadhayo. Etthāyaṃ gathito mucchito ajjhāpanno attanā jātidhammo samāno jātidhammaṃyeva pariyesati.

941\. Kiñca, bhikkhave, jarādhammaṃ vadetha? Puttabhariyaṃ, bhikkhave, jarādhammaṃ, dāsidāsaṃ jarādhammaṃ, ajeḷakaṃ jarādhammaṃ, kukkuṭasūkaraṃ jarādhammaṃ, hatthigavāssavaḷavaṃ jarādhammaṃ, jātarūparajataṃ jarādhammaṃ. Jarādhammā hete, bhikkhave, upadhayo. Etthāyaṃ gathito mucchito ajjhāpanno attanā jarādhammo samāno jarādhammaṃyeva pariyesati.

942\. Kiñca, bhikkhave, byādhidhammaṃ vadetha? Puttabhariyaṃ, bhikkhave, byādhidhammaṃ, dāsidāsaṃ byādhidhammaṃ, ajeḷakaṃ byādhidhammaṃ, kukkuṭasūkaraṃ byādhidhammaṃ, hatthigavāssavaḷavaṃ byādhidhammaṃ. Byādhidhammā hete, bhikkhave, upadhayo. Etthāyaṃ gathito mucchito ajjhāpanno attanā byādhidhammo samāno byādhidhammaṃyeva pariyesati.

943\. Kiñca, bhikkhave, maraṇadhammaṃ vadetha? Puttabhariyaṃ, bhikkhave, maraṇadhammaṃ, dāsidāsaṃ maraṇadhammaṃ, ajeḷakaṃ maraṇadhammaṃ, kukkuṭasūkaraṃ maraṇadhammaṃ, hatthigavāssavaḷavaṃ maraṇadhammaṃ. Maraṇadhammā hete, bhikkhave, upadhayo. Etthāyaṃ gathito mucchito ajjhāpanno attanā maraṇadhammo samāno maraṇadhammaṃyeva pariyesati.

944\. Kiñca, bhikkhave, sokadhammaṃ vadetha? Puttabhariyaṃ, bhikkhave, sokadhammaṃ, dāsidāsaṃ sokadhammaṃ, ajeḷakaṃ sokadhammaṃ, kukkuṭasūkaraṃ sokadhammaṃ, hatthigavāssavaḷavaṃ sokadhammaṃ. Sokadhammā hete, bhikkhave, upadhayo. Etthāyaṃ gathito mucchito ajjhāpanno attanā sokadhammo samāno sokadhammaṃyeva pariyesati.

945\. Kiñca, bhikkhave, saṃkilesadhammaṃ vadetha? Puttabhariyaṃ, bhikkhave, saṃkilesadhammaṃ, dāsidāsaṃ saṃkilesadhammaṃ, ajeḷakaṃ saṃkilesadhammaṃ, kukkuṭasūkaraṃ saṃkilesadhammaṃ, hatthigavāssavaḷavaṃ saṃkilesadhammaṃ, jātarūparajataṃ saṃkilesadhammaṃ. Saṃkilesadhammā hete, bhikkhave, upadhayo. Etthāyaṃ gathito mucchito ajjhāpanno attanā saṃkilesadhammo samāno saṃkilesadhammaṃyeva pariyesati. Ayaṃ, bhikkhave, anariyā pariyesanā.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
939\. 𑀓𑀢𑀫𑀸 𑀘, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀅𑀦𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀦𑀸? 𑀇𑀥, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀘𑁄 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺.

940\. 𑀓𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀘, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀯𑀤𑁂𑀣? 𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀪𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀤𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀤𑀸𑀲𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀚𑁂𑀍𑀅𑀓𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀓𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀼𑀝𑀲𑀽𑀓𑀭𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀳𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺𑀕𑀯𑀸𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀯𑀍𑀅𑀯𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀭𑀚𑀢𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁. 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀳𑁂𑀢𑁂, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀉𑀧𑀥𑀬𑁄. 𑀏𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀸𑀬𑀁 𑀕𑀣𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀫𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀸𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑁄 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺.

941\. 𑀓𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀘, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀯𑀤𑁂𑀣? 𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀪𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀤𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀤𑀸𑀲𑀁 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀚𑁂𑀍𑀅𑀓𑀁 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀓𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀼𑀝𑀲𑀽𑀓𑀭𑀁 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀳𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺𑀕𑀯𑀸𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀯𑀍𑀅𑀯𑀁 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀭𑀚𑀢𑀁 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁. 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀳𑁂𑀢𑁂, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀉𑀧𑀥𑀬𑁄. 𑀏𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀸𑀬𑀁 𑀕𑀣𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀫𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀸𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑁄 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺.

942\. 𑀓𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀘, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀯𑀤𑁂𑀣? 𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀪𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀤𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀤𑀸𑀲𑀁 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀚𑁂𑀍𑀅𑀓𑀁 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀓𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀼𑀝𑀲𑀽𑀓𑀭𑀁 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀳𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺𑀕𑀯𑀸𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀯𑀍𑀅𑀯𑀁 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁. 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀳𑁂𑀢𑁂, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀉𑀧𑀥𑀬𑁄. 𑀏𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀸𑀬𑀁 𑀕𑀣𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀫𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀸𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑁄 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺.

943\. 𑀓𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀘, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀯𑀤𑁂𑀣? 𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀪𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀤𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀤𑀸𑀲𑀁 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀚𑁂𑀍𑀅𑀓𑀁 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀓𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀼𑀝𑀲𑀽𑀓𑀭𑀁 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀳𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺𑀕𑀯𑀸𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀯𑀍𑀅𑀯𑀁 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁. 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀳𑁂𑀢𑁂, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀉𑀧𑀥𑀬𑁄. 𑀏𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀸𑀬𑀁 𑀕𑀣𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀫𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀸𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑁄 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺.

944\. 𑀓𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀘, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀯𑀤𑁂𑀣? 𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀪𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀤𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀤𑀸𑀲𑀁 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀚𑁂𑀍𑀅𑀓𑀁 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀓𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀼𑀝𑀲𑀽𑀓𑀭𑀁 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀳𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺𑀕𑀯𑀸𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀯𑀍𑀅𑀯𑀁 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁. 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀳𑁂𑀢𑁂, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀉𑀧𑀥𑀬𑁄. 𑀏𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀸𑀬𑀁 𑀕𑀣𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀫𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀸𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑁄 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺.

945\. 𑀓𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀘, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀯𑀤𑁂𑀣? 𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀪𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀤𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀤𑀸𑀲𑀁 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀚𑁂𑀍𑀅𑀓𑀁 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀓𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀼𑀝𑀲𑀽𑀓𑀭𑀁 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀳𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺𑀕𑀯𑀸𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀯𑀍𑀅𑀯𑀁 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀭𑀚𑀢𑀁 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁. 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀳𑁂𑀢𑁂, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀉𑀧𑀥𑀬𑁄. 𑀏𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀸𑀬𑀁 𑀕𑀣𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀫𑀼𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀸𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑁄 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺. 𑀅𑀬𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀅𑀦𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀦𑀸.

939.1 Katamā ca, bhikkhave, anariyā pariyesanā?  
*️⃣🤟🚺⨀①(katama) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚺⨀①🆎(na+ariya) 🚺⨀①(pariyesanā)  
which | and | Bhikkhave | of the unliberated | quest  
And what, Bhikkhave, is the quest of the unliberated?

939.2 Idha, bhikkhave, ekacco attanā jātidhammo samāno jātidhammaṃyeva pariyesati, attanā jarādhammo samāno jarādhammaṃyeva pariyesati, attanā byādhidhammo samāno byādhidhammaṃyeva pariyesati, attanā maraṇadhammo samāno maraṇadhammaṃyeva pariyesati, attanā sokadhammo samāno sokadhammaṃyeva pariyesati, attanā saṃkilesadhammo samāno saṃkilesadhammaṃyeva pariyesati.

here | Bhikkhave | a certain | by himself | liable to birth | being | what is liable to birth | only | searches for | by himself | liable to old age | being | what is liable to old age | only | searches for | by himself | liable to get sick | being | what is liable to sickness | only | searches for | by himself | liable to death | being | what is liable to death | only | searches for | by himself | liable to sorrow | being | what is liable to sorrow | only | searches for | by himself | liable to defilement | being | what is liable to defilement | only | searches for  
Here, Bhikkhave, someone, being liable to birth from his own doing, searches only for what is liable to birth; being liable to old age from his own doing, he searches only for what is liable to old age; being liable to sickness from his own doing, he searches only for what is liable to sickness; being liable to death from his own doing, he searches only for what is liable to death; being liable to sorrow from his own doing, he searches only for what is liable to sorrow; being liable to defilement from his own doing, he searches only for what is liable to defilement.

940.1 Kiñca, bhikkhave, jātidhammaṃ vadetha?  
*️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(kiṃ) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚻⨀②🆎(jāti+dhamma) 🟢⨂🤘⏯(vadati)  
what | and | Bhikkhave | liable to birth | would you call  
And what, Bhikkhave, do you call liable to birth?

940.2 Puttabhariyaṃ, bhikkhave, jātidhammaṃ, dāsidāsaṃ jātidhammaṃ, ajeḷakaṃ jātidhammaṃ, kukkuṭasūkaraṃ jātidhammaṃ, hatthigavāssavaḷavaṃ jātidhammaṃ, jātarūparajataṃ jātidhammaṃ.  
🚻⨀②🆎(putta+bhariyā) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚻⨀②🆎(jāti+dhamma) 🚻⨀②🆎(dāsī+dāsa) 🚻⨀②🆎(jāti+dhamma) 🚻⨀②🆎(aja+eḷaka) 🚻⨀②🆎(jāti+dhamma) 🚻⨀②🆎(kukkuṭa+sūkara) 🚻⨀②🆎(jāti+dhamma) 🚻⨀②🆎(hatthī+gava+assa+vaḷavā) 🚻⨀②🆎(jāti+dhamma) 🚻⨀②🆎(jāta+rūpa+rajata) 🚻⨀②🆎(jāti+dhamma)  
children and wives | Bhikkhave | liable to birth | male and female slaves | liable to birth | goats and sheep | liable to birth | chickens and pigs | liable to birth | elephants, cattle, horses, and mares | liable to birth | gold and silver | liable to birth  
Children and wives, Bhikkhave, are liable to birth; male and female slaves are liable to birth; goats and sheep are liable to birth; chickens and pigs are liable to birth; elephants, cattle, horses, and mares are liable to birth; gold and silver are liable to birth.

940.3 Jātidhammā hete, bhikkhave, upadhayo.  
🚹⨂①🆎(jāti+dhamma) ⏏️(hi) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(eta) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨂①(upadhi)

## The Quest of the Liberated

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
  946\. And what, Bhikkhave, is the quest of the liberated? Here, Bhikkhave, someone, being liable to birth from his own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to birth, searches for the unsurpassed sanctuary of `nibbāna` (extinguishment) from being born; being liable to old age from his own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to old age, searches for the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-ageing through extinguishment; being liable to sickness from his own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to sickness, searches for the unsurpassed sanctuary of health through extinguishment; being liable to death from his own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to death, searches for the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-death through extinguishment; being liable to sorrow from his own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to sorrow, searches for the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-sorrow through extinguishment; being liable to defilement from his own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to defilement, searches for the unsurpassed sanctuary of undefilement through extinguishment. This, Bhikkhave, is the quest of the liberated.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
946\. Katamā ca, bhikkhave, ariyā pariyesanā? Idha, bhikkhave, ekacco attanā jātidhammo samāno jātidhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesati, attanā jarādhammo samāno jarādhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesati, attanā byādhidhammo samāno byādhidhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā abyādhiṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesati, attanā maraṇadhammo samāno maraṇadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā amataṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesati, attanā sokadhammo samāno sokadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā asokaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesati, attanā saṃkilesadhammo samāno saṃkilesadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesati. Ayaṃ, bhikkhave, ariyā pariyesanā.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
946\. 𑀓𑀢𑀫𑀸 𑀘, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀅𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀦𑀸? 𑀇𑀥, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀘𑁄 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀚𑀭𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀫𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀢𑀺. 𑀅𑀬𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀅𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀦𑀸.

946.1 Katamā ca, bhikkhave, ariyā pariyesanā?  
*️⃣🤟🚺⨀①(katama) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚺⨀①(ariya) 🚺⨀①(pariyesanā)  
which | and | Bhikkhave | of the liberated | quest  
And what, Bhikkhave, is the quest of the liberated?

946.2 Idha, bhikkhave, ekacco attanā jātidhammo samāno jātidhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesati, attanā jarādhammo samāno jarādhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesati, attanā byādhidhammo samāno byādhidhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā abyādhiṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesati, attanā maraṇadhammo samāno maraṇadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā amataṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesati, attanā sokadhammo samāno sokadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā asokaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesati, attanā saṃkilesadhammo samāno saṃkilesadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesati.

here | Bhikkhave | a certain | by himself | liable to birth | being | in liable to birth | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | unborn | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | searches for | by himself | liable to old age | being | in liable to old age | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | not ageing | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | searches for | by himself | liable to get sick | being | in liable to get sick | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | healthy | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | searches for | by himself | liable to death | being | in liable to death | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | non-death | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | searches for | by himself | liable to sorrow | being | in liable to sorrow | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | non-sorrow | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | searches for | by himself | liable to defilement | being | in liable to defilement | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | undefiled | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | searches for  
Here, Bhikkhave, someone, being liable to birth from his own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to birth, searches for the unsurpassed sanctuary of `nibbāna` (extinguishment) from being born; being liable to old age from his own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to old age, searches for the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-ageing through extinguishment; being liable to sickness from his own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to sickness, searches for the unsurpassed sanctuary of health through extinguishment; being liable to death from his own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to death, searches for the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-death through extinguishment; being liable to sorrow from his own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to sorrow, searches for the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-sorrow through extinguishment; being liable to defilement from his own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to defilement, searches for the unsurpassed sanctuary of undefilement through extinguishment.

946.3 Ayaṃ, bhikkhave, ariyā pariyesanā.

this | Bhikkhave | of the liberated | quest  
This, Bhikkhave, is the quest of the liberated.

## Renunciation

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
  947\. Bhikkhave, I too, before my `sambodhi` (full awakening), while still a Bodhisatta, not fully awakened, being liable to birth from my own doing, searched only for what is liable to birth; being liable to old age from my own doing, I searched only for what is liable to old age; being liable to sickness from my own doing, I searched only for what is liable to sickness; being liable to death from my own doing, I searched only for what is liable to death; being liable to sorrow from my own doing, I searched only for what is liable to sorrow; being liable to defilement from my own doing, I searched only for what is liable to defilement. Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me: ‘Why do I, being liable to birth from my own doing, search only for what is liable to birth; being liable to old age from my own doing, search only for what is liable to old age; being liable to sickness from my own doing, search only for what is liable to sickness; being liable to death from my own doing, search only for what is liable to death; being liable to sorrow from my own doing, search only for what is liable to sorrow; being liable to defilement from my own doing, search only for what is liable to defilement? What if I, being liable to birth from my own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to birth, were to search for the unsurpassed sanctuary of extinguishment from being born; being liable to old age from my own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to old age, were to search for the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-ageing through extinguishment; being liable to sickness from my own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to sickness, were to search for the unsurpassed sanctuary of health through extinguishment; being liable to death from my own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to death, were to search for the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-death through extinguishment; being liable to sorrow from my own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to sorrow, were to search for the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-sorrow through extinguishment; being liable to defilement from my own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to defilement, were to search for the unsurpassed sanctuary of undefilement through extinguishment?’
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
947\. Ahampi sudaṃ, bhikkhave, pubbeva sambodhā anabhisambuddho bodhisattova samāno attanā jātidhammo samāno jātidhammaṃyeva pariyesāmi, attanā jarādhammo samāno jarādhammaṃyeva pariyesāmi, attanā byādhidhammo samāno byādhidhammaṃyeva pariyesāmi, attanā maraṇadhammo samāno maraṇadhammaṃyeva pariyesāmi, attanā sokadhammo samāno sokadhammaṃyeva pariyesāmi, attanā saṃkilesadhammo samāno saṃkilesadhammaṃyeva pariyesāmi. Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘kiṃ nu kho ahaṃ attanā jātidhammo samāno jātidhammaṃyeva pariyesāmi, attanā jarādhammo samāno…pe…  byādhidhammo samāno…  maraṇadhammo samāno…  sokadhammo samāno…  attanā saṃkilesadhammo samāno saṃkilesadhammaṃyeva pariyesāmi? Yannūnāhaṃ attanā jātidhammo samāno jātidhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyeseyyaṃ, attanā jarādhammo samāno jarādhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyeseyyaṃ, attanā byādhidhammo samāno byādhidhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā abyādhiṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyeseyyaṃ, attanā maraṇadhammo samāno maraṇadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā amataṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyeseyyaṃ, attanā sokadhammo samāno sokadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā asokaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyeseyyaṃ, attanā saṃkilesadhammo samāno saṃkilesadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyeseyyan’ti.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
947\. 𑀅𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀺 𑀲𑀼𑀤𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂𑀯 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑁄𑀥𑀸 𑀅𑀦𑀪𑀺𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀩𑁄𑀥𑀺𑀲𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄𑀯 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀺. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀓𑀺𑀁 𑀦𑀼 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄…𑀧𑁂…  𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄…  𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄…  𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄…  𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁𑀬𑁂𑀯 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀺? 𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀽𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀚𑀭𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀫𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺.
</TabItem>
<TabItem label="Interlinear">
947.1 Ahampi sudaṃ, bhikkhave, pubbeva sambodhā anabhisambuddho bodhisattova samāno attanā jātidhammo samāno jātidhammaṃyeva pariyesāmi, attanā jarādhammo samāno jarādhammaṃyeva pariyesāmi, attanā byādhidhammo samāno byādhidhammaṃyeva pariyesāmi, attanā maraṇadhammo samāno maraṇadhammaṃyeva pariyesāmi, attanā sokadhammo samāno sokadhammaṃyeva pariyesāmi, attanā saṃkilesadhammo samāno saṃkilesadhammaṃyeva pariyesāmi.  
*️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) ⏏️(api) ⏏️(sudaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) ⏏️(pubbe) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀⑤(sambodha) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)🆎(na+abhisambuddha) 🚹⨀①🆎(bodhi+satta) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚹⨀③(atta) 🚹⨀①🆎(jāti+dhamma) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚹⨀②🆎(jāti+dhamma) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀👆▶️(pariyesati) 🚹⨀③(atta) 🚹⨀①🆎(jarā+dhamma) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚹⨀②🆎(jarā+dhamma) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀👆▶️(pariyesati) 🚹⨀③(atta) 🚹⨀①🆎(byādhi+dhamma) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚹⨀②🆎(byādhi+dhamma) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀👆▶️(pariyesati) 🚹⨀③(atta) 🚹⨀①🆎(maraṇa+dhamma) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚹⨀②🆎(maraṇa+dhamma) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀👆▶️(pariyesati) 🚹⨀③(atta) 🚹⨀①🆎(soka+dhamma) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚹⨀②🆎(soka+dhamma) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀👆▶️(pariyesati) 🚹⨀③(atta) 🚹⨀①🆎(saṃkilesa+dhamma) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚹⨀②🆎(saṃkilesa+dhamma) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀👆▶️(pariyesati)  
I | too | indeed | Bhikkhave | before | just | full awakening | not fully awakened | a Bodhisatta | still | being | by myself | liable to birth | being | what is liable to birth | only | I searched for | by myself | liable to old age | being | what is liable to old age | only | I searched for | by myself | liable to get sick | being | what is liable to get sick | only | I searched for | by myself | liable to death | being | what is liable to death | only | I searched for | by myself | liable to sorrow | being | what is liable to sorrow | only | I searched for | by myself | liable to defilement | being | what is liable to defilement | only | I searched for  
Bhikkhave, I too, before my `sambodhi` (full awakening), while still a Bodhisatta, not fully awakened, being liable to birth from my own doing, searched only for what is liable to birth; being liable to old age from my own doing, I searched only for what is liable to old age; being liable to sickness from my own doing, I searched only for what is liable to sickness; being liable to death from my own doing, I searched only for what is liable to death; being liable to sorrow from my own doing, I searched only for what is liable to sorrow; being liable to defilement from my own doing, I searched only for what is liable to defilement.

947.2 Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘kiṃ nu kho ahaṃ attanā jātidhammo samāno jātidhammaṃyeva pariyesāmi, attanā jarādhammo samāno…pe…  byādhidhammo samāno…  maraṇadhammo samāno…  sokadhammo samāno…  attanā saṃkilesadhammo samāno saṃkilesadhammaṃyeva pariyesāmi?  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(kiṃ) ⏏️(nu) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀③(atta) 🚹⨀①🆎(jāti+dhamma) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚹⨀②🆎(jāti+dhamma) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀👆▶️(pariyesati) 🚹⨀③(atta) 🚹⨀①🆎(jarā+dhamma) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) ⏏️(peyyāla) 🚹⨀①🆎(byādhi+dhamma) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚹⨀①🆎(maraṇa+dhamma) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚹⨀①🆎(soka+dhamma) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚹⨀③(atta) 🚹⨀①🆎(saṃkilesa+dhamma) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚹⨀②🆎(saṃkilesa+dhamma) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀👆▶️(pariyesati)  
to that | to me | Bhikkhave | this | occurred | why | (question) | indeed | I | by myself | liable to birth | being | what is liable to birth | only | I search for | by myself | liable to old age | being | (and so on) | liable to get sick | being | liable to death | being | liable to sorrow | being | by myself | liable to defilement | being | what is liable to defilement | only | I search for  
Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me: ‘Why do I, being liable to birth from my own doing, search only for what is liable to birth; being liable to old age from my own doing, search only for what is liable to old age; being liable to sickness from my own doing, search only for what is liable to sickness; being liable to death from my own doing, search only for what is liable to death; being liable to sorrow from my own doing, search only for what is liable to sorrow; being liable to defilement from my own doing, search only for what is liable to defilement?

947.3 Yannūnāhaṃ attanā jātidhammo samāno jātidhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyeseyyaṃ, attanā jarādhammo samāno jarādhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyeseyyaṃ, attanā byādhidhammo samāno byādhidhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā abyādhiṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyeseyyaṃ, attanā maraṇadhammo samāno maraṇadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā amataṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyeseyyaṃ, attanā sokadhammo samāno sokadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā asokaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyeseyyaṃ, attanā saṃkilesadhammo samāno saṃkilesadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyeseyyan’ti.

what | if | I | by myself | liable to birth | being | in liable to birth | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | unborn | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | I should search for | by myself | liable to old age | being | in liable to old age | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | not ageing | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | I should search for | by myself | liable to get sick | being | in liable to get sick | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | healthy | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | I should search for | by myself | liable to death | being | in liable to death | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | non-death | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | I should search for | by myself | liable to sorrow | being | in liable to sorrow | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | non-sorrow | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | I should search for | by myself | liable to defilement | being | in liable to defilement | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | undefiled | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | I should search for | (end quote)  
What if I, being liable to birth from my own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to birth, were to search for the unsurpassed sanctuary of extinguishment from being born; being liable to old age from my own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to old age, were to search for the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-ageing through extinguishment; being liable to sickness from my own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to sickness, were to search for the unsurpassed sanctuary of health through extinguishment; being liable to death from my own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to death, were to search for the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-death through extinguishment; being liable to sorrow from my own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to sorrow, were to search for the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-sorrow through extinguishment; being liable to defilement from my own doing, having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to defilement, were to search for the unsurpassed sanctuary of undefilement through extinguishment?’
