</Tabs>

## Āḷāra Kālāma

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
948\. So, Bhikkhave, at a later time, while I was still young, with jet-black hair, endowed with the prime of youth, in the first stage of life—though my unwilling mother and father wept with tear-filled faces—having shaved off my hair and beard, and having put on saffron robes, I went forth from the home life into homelessness. Having thus gone forth, seeking what is wholesome, searching for the unsurpassed state of supreme peace, I approached where Āḷāra Kālāma was. Having approached, I said this to Āḷāra Kālāma: “I wish, Āvuso Kālāma, to lead the `brahmacariya` (pure life) in this Dhamma and Discipline.” When this was said, Bhikkhave, Āḷāra Kālāma said this to me: “May the venerable one stay; such is this Dhamma that a wise person, in no long time, having realised his own teacher's doctrine for himself through `abhiññā` (experiential understanding), may enter and dwell in it.” So, Bhikkhave, quickly, in no long time, I learned that Dhamma. So, Bhikkhave, merely by lip-service, by mere verbal declaration, I would speak of the doctrine of knowledge and the doctrine of elders, and I would claim, ‘I know, I see,’ and so would others. Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me: ‘It is not by mere faith alone that Āḷāra Kālāma declares this Dhamma as something he enters and dwells in, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding; surely Āḷāra Kālāma dwells in this Dhamma knowing and seeing it.’

949\. Then, Bhikkhave, I approached where Āḷāra Kālāma was; having approached, I said this to Āḷāra Kālāma: ‘To what extent, Āvuso Kālāma, do you declare this Dhamma as something you enter and dwell in, having realised it for yourself through experiential understanding?’ When this was said, Bhikkhave, Āḷāra Kālāma declared the `ākiñcaññāyatana` (base of nothingness). Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me: ‘It is not only Āḷāra Kālāma who has `saddhā` (faith); I also have faith; it is not only Āḷāra Kālāma who has `vīriya` (energy); I also have energy; it is not only Āḷāra Kālāma who has `sati` (awareness); I also have awareness; it is not only Āḷāra Kālāma who has `samādhi` (focus); I also have focus; it is not only Āḷāra Kālāma who has `paññā` (wisdom); I also have wisdom. What if I were to strive for the realisation of that Dhamma which Āḷāra Kālāma declares he enters and dwells in, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding?’ So, Bhikkhave, quickly, in no long time, having realised that Dhamma for myself through experiential understanding, I entered and dwelt in it.

950\. Then, Bhikkhave, I approached where Āḷāra Kālāma was; having approached, I said this to Āḷāra Kālāma:

951\. ‘Is it to this extent, Āvuso Kālāma, that you declare this Dhamma, having realised it for yourself through experiential understanding and entered upon it?’

952\. ‘To this extent indeed, Āvuso, do I declare this Dhamma, having realised it for myself through experiential understanding and entered upon it.’

953\. ‘Indeed I too, Āvuso, to this extent enter and dwell in this Dhamma, having realised it for myself through experiential understanding.’

954\. ‘It is a gain for us, Āvuso, it is a great gain for us, that we see such a venerable one as a companion in the pure life. Thus, the Dhamma that I declare, having realised it for myself through experiential understanding and entered upon it, that Dhamma you enter and dwell in, having realised it for yourself through experiential understanding. The Dhamma that you enter and dwell in, having realised it for yourself through experiential understanding, that Dhamma I declare, having realised it for myself through experiential understanding and entered upon it. Thus, the Dhamma that I know, that Dhamma you know; the Dhamma that you know, that Dhamma I know. Thus, as I am, so are you; as you are, so am I. Come now, Āvuso, let us both, being present, lead this company.’

955\. Thus, Bhikkhave, Āḷāra Kālāma, being my teacher, placed me, his own pupil, on the same level as himself, and honoured me with great honour. Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me: ‘This Dhamma does not lead to giving up, nor to dispassion, nor to cessation, nor to calm, nor to experiential understanding, nor to full awakening, nor to extinguishment; it leads only to reappearance in the base of nothingness.’ So, Bhikkhave, not valuing that Dhamma, having given up that Dhamma, I departed.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
948\. So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, aparena samayena daharova samāno susukāḷakeso, bhadrena yobbanena samannāgato paṭhamena vayasā akāmakānaṃ mātāpitūnaṃ assumukhānaṃ rudantānaṃ kesamassuṃ ohāretvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādetvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajiṃ. So evaṃ pabbajito samāno kiṃkusalagavesī anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ pariyesamāno yena āḷāro kālāmo tenupasaṅkamiṃ. Upasaṅkamitvā āḷāraṃ kālāmaṃ etadavocaṃ—  ‘icchāmahaṃ, āvuso kālāma, imasmiṃ dhammavinaye brahmacariyaṃ caritun’ti. Evaṃ vutte, bhikkhave, āḷāro kālāmo maṃ etadavoca—  ‘viharatāyasmā; tādiso ayaṃ dhammo yattha viññū puriso nacirasseva sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyyā’ti. So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, nacirasseva khippameva taṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇiṃ. So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, tāvatakeneva oṭṭhapahatamattena lapitalāpanamattena ñāṇavādañca vadāmi theravādañca, ‘jānāmi passāmī’ti ca paṭijānāmi ahañceva aññe ca. Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘na kho āḷāro kālāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ kevalaṃ saddhāmattakena sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedeti; addhā āḷāro kālāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ viharatī’ti.

949\. Atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhave, yena āḷāro kālāmo tenupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā āḷāraṃ kālāmaṃ etadavocaṃ—  ‘kittāvatā no, āvuso kālāma, imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedesī’ti? Evaṃ vutte, bhikkhave, āḷāro kālāmo ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ pavedesi. Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘na kho āḷārasseva kālāmassa atthi saddhā, mayhampatthi saddhā; na kho āḷārasseva kālāmassa atthi vīriyaṃ, mayhampatthi vīriyaṃ; na kho āḷārasseva kālāmassa atthi sati, mayhampatthi sati; na kho āḷārasseva kālāmassa atthi samādhi, mayhampatthi samādhi; na kho āḷārasseva kālāmassa atthi paññā, mayhampatthi paññā. Yannūnāhaṃ yaṃ dhammaṃ āḷāro kālāmo sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedeti, tassa dhammassa sacchikiriyāya padaheyyan’ti. So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, nacirasseva khippameva taṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihāsiṃ.

950\. Atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhave, yena āḷāro kālāmo tenupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā āḷāraṃ kālāmaṃ etadavocaṃ—

951\. ‘Ettāvatā no, āvuso kālāma, imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesī’ti?

952\. ‘Ettāvatā kho ahaṃ, āvuso, imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedemī’ti.

953\. ‘Ahampi kho, āvuso, ettāvatā imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmī’ti.

954\. ‘Lābhā no, āvuso, suladdhaṃ no, āvuso, ye mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ tādisaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ passāma. Iti yāhaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedemi taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharasi. Yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharasi tamahaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedemi. Iti yāhaṃ dhammaṃ jānāmi taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi, yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi tamahaṃ dhammaṃ jānāmi. Iti yādiso ahaṃ tādiso tuvaṃ, yādiso tuvaṃ tādiso ahaṃ. Ehi dāni, āvuso, ubhova santā imaṃ gaṇaṃ pariharāmā’ti.

955\. Iti kho, bhikkhave, āḷāro kālāmo ācariyo me samāno attano antevāsiṃ maṃ samānaṃ attanā samasamaṃ ṭhapesi, uḷārāya ca maṃ pūjāya pūjesi. Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘nāyaṃ dhammo nibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati, yāvadeva ākiñcaññāyatanūpapattiyā’ti. So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, taṃ dhammaṃ analaṅkaritvā tasmā dhammā nibbijja apakkamiṃ.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
948\. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀅𑀧𑀭𑁂𑀦 𑀲𑀫𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀤𑀳𑀭𑁄𑀯 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀲𑀼𑀲𑀼𑀓𑀸𑀍𑀅𑀓𑁂𑀲𑁄, 𑀪𑀤𑁆𑀭𑁂𑀦 𑀬𑁄𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀦𑁂𑀦 𑀲𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸𑀕𑀢𑁄 𑀧𑀞𑀫𑁂𑀦 𑀯𑀬𑀲𑀸 𑀅𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀓𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀫𑀸𑀢𑀸𑀧𑀺𑀢𑀽𑀦𑀁 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼𑀫𑀼𑀔𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀭𑀼𑀤𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀓𑁂𑀲𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼𑀁 𑀑𑀳𑀸𑀭𑁂𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀓𑀸𑀲𑀸𑀬𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀅𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀸𑀤𑁂𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀕𑀸𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀅𑀦𑀕𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀧𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀚𑀺𑀁. 𑀲𑁄 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀧𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀚𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀓𑀺𑀁𑀓𑀼𑀲𑀮𑀕𑀯𑁂𑀲𑀻 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺𑀯𑀭𑀧𑀤𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀁. 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑀁 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀁—  ‘𑀇𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀸𑀫𑀳𑀁, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫, 𑀇𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀯𑀺𑀦𑀬𑁂 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀢𑀼𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺. 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀫𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘—  ‘𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀢𑀸𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀸; 𑀢𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀬𑀢𑁆𑀣 𑀯𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀽 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀦𑀘𑀺𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀲𑀓𑀁 𑀆𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀓𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀦𑀘𑀺𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀢𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀧𑀼𑀡𑀺𑀁. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀢𑀓𑁂𑀦𑁂𑀯 𑀑𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀧𑀳𑀢𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀦 𑀮𑀧𑀺𑀢𑀮𑀸𑀧𑀦𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀦 𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀯𑀸𑀤𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀯𑀤𑀸𑀫𑀺 𑀣𑁂𑀭𑀯𑀸𑀤𑀜𑁆𑀘, ‘𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀫𑀺 𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀻’𑀢𑀺 𑀘 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀫𑀺 𑀅𑀳𑀜𑁆𑀘𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀜𑁆𑀜𑁂 𑀘. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀓𑁂𑀯𑀮𑀁 𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀓𑁂𑀦 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀻𑀢𑀺 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀢𑀺; 𑀅𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀢𑀻’𑀢𑀺.

949\. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀁; 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑀁 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀁—  ‘𑀓𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀦𑁄, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫, 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀻𑀢𑀺 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀻’𑀢𑀺? 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀆𑀓𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀬𑀢𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀺. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸, 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸; 𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀯𑀻𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁, 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀯𑀻𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁; 𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀲𑀢𑀺, 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀲𑀢𑀺; 𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀥𑀺, 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀥𑀺; 𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸, 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸. 𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀽𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀬𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀻𑀢𑀺 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀢𑀺, 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀺𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀬 𑀧𑀤𑀳𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀦𑀘𑀺𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀢𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁.

950\. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀁; 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑀁 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀁—

951\. ‘𑀏𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀦𑁄, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫, 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀻’𑀢𑀺?

952\. ‘𑀏𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀫𑀻’𑀢𑀺.

953\. ‘𑀅𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀏𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀻’𑀢𑀺.

954\. ‘𑀮𑀸𑀪𑀸 𑀦𑁄, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀲𑀼𑀮𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀁 𑀦𑁄, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀬𑁂 𑀫𑀬𑀁 𑀆𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀢𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑀁 𑀲𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀘𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀁 𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫. 𑀇𑀢𑀺 𑀬𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀫𑀺 𑀢𑀁 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀲𑀺. 𑀬𑀁 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀲𑀺 𑀢𑀫𑀳𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀫𑀺. 𑀇𑀢𑀺 𑀬𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀫𑀺 𑀢𑀁 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀺, 𑀬𑀁 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀺 𑀢𑀫𑀳𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀫𑀺. 𑀇𑀢𑀺 𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁 𑀢𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀢𑀼𑀯𑀁, 𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀢𑀼𑀯𑀁 𑀢𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁. 𑀏𑀳𑀺 𑀤𑀸𑀦𑀺, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀉𑀪𑁄𑀯 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀸 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀕𑀡𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀸’𑀢𑀺.

955\. 𑀇𑀢𑀺 𑀔𑁄, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀆𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁄 𑀫𑁂 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑁄 𑀅𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀯𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁 𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀫𑀲𑀫𑀁 𑀞𑀧𑁂𑀲𑀺, 𑀉𑀍𑀆𑀭𑀸𑀬 𑀘 𑀫𑀁 𑀧𑀽𑀚𑀸𑀬 𑀧𑀽𑀚𑁂𑀲𑀺. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀦𑀸𑀬𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀺𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀯𑀺𑀭𑀸𑀕𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀦𑀺𑀭𑁄𑀥𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑁄𑀥𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀬 𑀲𑀁𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀢𑀺, 𑀬𑀸𑀯𑀤𑁂𑀯 𑀆𑀓𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀬𑀢𑀦𑀽𑀧𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀢𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀮𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀭𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀺𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀅𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀁.
</TabItem>
<TabItem label="Interlinear">
948.1 So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, aparena samayena daharova samāno susukāḷakeso, bhadrena yobbanena samannāgato paṭhamena vayasā akāmakānaṃ mātāpitūnaṃ assumukhānaṃ rudantānaṃ kesamassuṃ ohāretvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādetvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(apara) 🚹⨀③(samaya) 🚹⨀①(dahara) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚹⨀①🆎(susu+kāḷa+kesa) 🚻⨀③(bhadra) 🚻⨀③(yobbana) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(samannāgata) 🚹⨀③(paṭhama) 🚹⨀③(vayas) 🚹⨂⑥🆎(na+kāmaka) 🚹⨂⑥🆎(mātar+pitar) 🚹⨂⑥🆎(assu+mukha) 🚹⨂⑥🔽(anta)(rudanta) 🚻⨀②🆎(kesa+massu) 🔽(tvā)(ohāreti) 🚻⨂②(kāsāya) 🚻⨂②(vattha) 🔽(tvā)(acchādeti) 🚻⨀⑤(agāra) 🚻⨀②🆎(na+agāriya) 🟢⨀👆⏮(pabbajati)  
that | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | at a later | time | young | still | being | with jet-black hair | with the prime | of youth | endowed with | in the first | stage of life | of the unwilling | mother and father | with tear-filled faces | weeping | hair and beard | having shaved off | ochre | robes | having put on | from household life | to homeless state | renounced  
So, Bhikkhave, at a later time, while I was still young, with jet-black hair, endowed with the prime of youth, in the first stage of life—though my unwilling mother and father wept with tear-filled faces—having shaved off my hair and beard, and having put on saffron robes, I went forth from the home life into homelessness.

948.2 So evaṃ pabbajito samāno kiṃkusalagavesī anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ pariyesamāno yena āḷāro kālāmo tenupasaṅkamiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(evaṃ) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(pabbajita) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚹⨀①🆎(kiṃ+kusala+gavesī) 🚻⨀②🆎(na+uttara) 🚻⨀②🆎(santi+vara+pada) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(pariyesamāna) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨀①(āḷāra) 🚹⨀①(kālāma) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(upasaṅkamati)  
that | thus | gone forth | being | seeking what is wholesome | unsurpassed | state of supreme peace | searching for | by where | Āḷāra | Kālāma | by there | I approached  
Having thus gone forth, seeking what is wholesome, searching for the unsurpassed state of supreme peace, I approached where Āḷāra Kālāma was.

948.3 Upasaṅkamitvā āḷāraṃ kālāmaṃ etadavocaṃ—  ‘icchāmahaṃ, āvuso kālāma, imasmiṃ dhammavinaye brahmacariyaṃ caritun’ti.  
🔽(tvā)(upasaṅkamati) 🚹⨀②(āḷāra) 🚹⨀②(kālāma) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(vadati) 🟢⨀👆▶️(icchati) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) ⏏️(āvuso) 🚹⨀⓪(kālāma) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑦(ima) 🚹⨀⑦🆎(dhamma+vinaya) 🚻⨀②🆎(brahma+cariya) 🔽(tuṃ)(carati) ⏏️(iti)  
having approached | Āḷāra | Kālāma | this | I said | I wish | I | Āvuso | Kālāma | in this | Dhamma and Discipline | the pure life | to lead | (end quote)  
Having approached, I said this to Āḷāra Kālāma: “I wish, Āvuso Kālāma, to lead the `brahmacariya` (pure life) in this Dhamma and Discipline.”

948.4 Evaṃ vutte, bhikkhave, āḷāro kālāmo maṃ etadavoca—  ‘viharatāyasmā;  
⏏️(evaṃ) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(vutta) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀①(āḷāra) 🚹⨀①(kālāma) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(vadati) 🟢⨀🤟⏹(viharati) 🚹⨀①(āyasmant)  
thus | said | Bhikkhave | Āḷāra | Kālāma | me | this | said | may stay | the venerable one  
When this was said, Bhikkhave, Āḷāra Kālāma said this to me: “May the venerable one stay;

948.5 tādiso ayaṃ dhammo yattha viññū puriso nacirasseva sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyyā’ti.  
🚹⨀①(tādisa) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀①(dhamma) ⏏️(yattha) 🚹⨀①(viññū) 🚹⨀①(purisa) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(cirassa) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀②(saka) 🚹⨀②(ācariyaka) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(viharati) ⏏️(iti)  
such | this | Dhamma | in which | wise | person | not | after a long time | just | his own | teacher's doctrine | for himself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | may dwell | (end quote)  
such is this Dhamma that a wise person, in no long time, having realised his own teacher's doctrine for himself through `abhiññā` (experiential understanding), may enter and dwell in it.”

948.6 So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, nacirasseva khippameva taṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(cirassa) ⏏️(eva) 🚻⨀②(khippa) ⏏️(eva) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🟢⨀👆⏮(pariyāpuṇāti)  
that | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | not | after a long time | just | quickly | just | that | Dhamma | I learned  
So, Bhikkhave, quickly, in no long time, I learned that Dhamma.

948.7 So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, tāvatakeneva oṭṭhapahatamattena lapitalāpanamattena ñāṇavādañca vadāmi theravādañca, ‘jānāmi passāmī’ti ca paṭijānāmi ahañceva aññe ca.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚻⨀③(tāvataka) ⏏️(eva) 🚻⨀③🆎(oṭṭha+pahata+matta) 🚻⨀③🆎(lapita+lāpana+matta) 🚹⨀②🆎(ñāṇa+vāda) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨀👆▶️(vadati) 🚹⨀②🆎(thera+vāda) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨀👆▶️(jānāti) 🟢⨀👆▶️(passati) ⏏️(iti) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨀👆▶️(paṭijānāti) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) ⏏️(ca) ⏏️(eva) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(añña) ⏏️(ca)  
that | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | by just so much | merely | by mere lip-service | by mere verbal declaration | the doctrine of knowledge | and | I speak of | the doctrine of elders | and | I know | I see | (end quote) | and | I claim | I | and | indeed | others | and  
So, Bhikkhave, merely by lip-service, by mere verbal declaration, I would speak of the doctrine of knowledge and the doctrine of elders, and I would claim, ‘I know, I see,’ and so would others.

948.8 Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘na kho āḷāro kālāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ kevalaṃ saddhāmattakena sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedeti;  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀①(āḷāra) 🚹⨀①(kālāma) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🚻⨀②(kevala) 🚻⨀③🆎(saddhā+mattaka) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀👆▶️(viharati) ⏏️(iti) 🟠⨀🤟▶️(pavedeti)  
to that | to me | Bhikkhave | this | occurred | not | indeed | Āḷāra | Kālāma | this | Dhamma | only | by mere faith | for himself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | I dwell | (end quote) | declares  
Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me: ‘It is not by mere faith alone that Āḷāra Kālāma declares this Dhamma as something he enters and dwells in, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding;

948.9 addhā āḷāro kālāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ viharatī’ti.  
⏏️(addhā) 🚹⨀①(āḷāra) 🚹⨀①(kālāma) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🚹⨀①🔽(anta)(jānanta) 🚹⨀①🔽(anta)(passanta) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(viharati) ⏏️(iti)  
surely | Āḷāra | Kālāma | this | Dhamma | knowing | seeing | dwells | (end quote)  
surely Āḷāra Kālāma dwells in this Dhamma knowing and seeing it.’

949.1 Atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhave, yena āḷāro kālāmo tenupasaṅkamiṃ;  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨀①(āḷāra) 🚹⨀①(kālāma) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(upasaṅkamati)  
then | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | by where | Āḷāra | Kālāma | by there | I approached  
Then, Bhikkhave, I approached where Āḷāra Kālāma was;

949.2 upasaṅkamitvā āḷāraṃ kālāmaṃ etadavocaṃ—  ‘kittāvatā no, āvuso kālāma, imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedesī’ti?  
🔽(tvā)(upasaṅkamati) 🚹⨀②(āḷāra) 🚹⨀②(kālāma) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(vadati) ⏏️(kittāvatā) ⏏️(no) ⏏️(āvuso) 🚹⨀⓪(kālāma) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀👆▶️(viharati) ⏏️(iti) 🟠⨀🤘▶️(pavedeti) ⏏️(iti)  
having approached | Āḷāra | Kālāma | this | I said | to what extent | then | Āvuso | Kālāma | this | Dhamma | for yourself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | I dwell | (end quote) | you declare | (end quote)  
having approached, I said this to Āḷāra Kālāma: ‘To what extent, Āvuso Kālāma, do you declare this Dhamma as something you enter and dwell in, having realised it for yourself through experiential understanding?’

949.3 Evaṃ vutte, bhikkhave, āḷāro kālāmo ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ pavedesi.  
⏏️(evaṃ) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(vutta) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀①(āḷāra) 🚹⨀①(kālāma) 🚻⨀②🆎(ākiñcañña+āyatana) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(pavedeti)  
thus | said | Bhikkhave | Āḷāra | Kālāma | the base of nothingness | declared  
When this was said, Bhikkhave, Āḷāra Kālāma declared the `ākiñcaññāyatana` (base of nothingness).

949.4 Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘na kho āḷārasseva kālāmassa atthi saddhā, mayhampatthi saddhā;  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⑥(āḷāra) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀⑥(kālāma) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) 🚺⨀①(saddhā) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) ⏏️(api) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) 🚺⨀①(saddhā)  
to that | to me | Bhikkhave | this | occurred | not | indeed | of Āḷāra | only | of Kālāma | there is | faith | of me | too | there is | faith  
Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me: ‘It is not only Āḷāra Kālāma who has `saddhā` (faith); I also have faith;

949.5 na kho āḷārasseva kālāmassa atthi vīriyaṃ, mayhampatthi vīriyaṃ;  
⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⑥(āḷāra) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀⑥(kālāma) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) 🚻⨀①(vīriya) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) ⏏️(api) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) 🚻⨀①(vīriya)  
not | indeed | of Āḷāra | only | of Kālāma | there is | energy | of me | too | there is | energy  
it is not only Āḷāra Kālāma who has `vīriya` (energy); I also have energy;

949.6 na kho āḷārasseva kālāmassa atthi sati, mayhampatthi sati;  
⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⑥(āḷāra) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀⑥(kālāma) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) 🚺⨀①(sati) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) ⏏️(api) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) 🚺⨀①(sati)  
not | indeed | of Āḷāra | only | of Kālāma | there is | awareness | of me | too | there is | awareness  
it is not only Āḷāra Kālāma who has `sati` (awareness); I also have awareness;

949.7 na kho āḷārasseva kālāmassa atthi samādhi, mayhampatthi samādhi;  
⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⑥(āḷāra) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀⑥(kālāma) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) 🚹⨀①(samādhi) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) ⏏️(api) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) 🚹⨀①(samādhi)  
not | indeed | of Āḷāra | only | of Kālāma | there is | focus | of me | too | there is | focus  
it is not only Āḷāra Kālāma who has `samādhi` (focus); I also have focus;

949.8 na kho āḷārasseva kālāmassa atthi paññā, mayhampatthi paññā.  
⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⑥(āḷāra) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀⑥(kālāma) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) 🚺⨀①(paññā) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) ⏏️(api) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) 🚺⨀①(paññā)  
not | indeed | of Āḷāra | only | of Kālāma | there is | wisdom | of me | too | there is | wisdom  
it is not only Āḷāra Kālāma who has `paññā` (wisdom); I also have wisdom.

949.9 Yannūnāhaṃ yaṃ dhammaṃ āḷāro kālāmo sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedeti, tassa dhammassa sacchikiriyāya padaheyyan’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ya) ⏏️(nūna) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ya) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🚹⨀①(āḷāra) 🚹⨀①(kālāma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀👆▶️(viharati) ⏏️(iti) 🟠⨀🤟▶️(pavedeti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑥(ta) 🚹⨀⑥(dhamma) 🚺⨀④🆎(sacchi+kiriyā) 🔵⨀👆⏯(padahati) ⏏️(iti)  
what | if | I | which | Dhamma | Āḷāra | Kālāma | for himself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | I dwell | (end quote) | declares | of that | Dhamma | for the realisation | I were to strive | (end quote)  
What if I were to strive for the realisation of that Dhamma which Āḷāra Kālāma declares he enters and dwells in, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding?’

949.10 So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, nacirasseva khippameva taṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihāsiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(cirassa) ⏏️(eva) 🚻⨀②(khippa) ⏏️(eva) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀👆⏮(viharati)  
that | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | not | after a long time | just | quickly | just | that | Dhamma | for myself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | I dwelt  
So, Bhikkhave, quickly, in no long time, having realised that Dhamma for myself through experiential understanding, I entered and dwelt in it.

950.1 Atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhave, yena āḷāro kālāmo tenupasaṅkamiṃ;  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨀①(āḷāra) 🚹⨀①(kālāma) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(upasaṅkamati)  
then | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | by where | Āḷāra | Kālāma | by there | I approached  
Then, Bhikkhave, I approached where Āḷāra Kālāma was;

950.2 upasaṅkamitvā āḷāraṃ kālāmaṃ etadavocaṃ—  
🔽(tvā)(upasaṅkamati) 🚹⨀②(āḷāra) 🚹⨀②(kālāma) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(vadati)  
having approached | Āḷāra | Kālāma | this | I said  
having approached, I said this to Āḷāra Kālāma:

951.1 ‘Ettāvatā no, āvuso kālāma, imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesī’ti?  
⏏️(ettāvatā) ⏏️(no) ⏏️(āvuso) 🚹⨀⓪(kālāma) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟠⨀🤘▶️(pavedeti) ⏏️(iti)  
to this extent | then | Āvuso | Kālāma | this | Dhamma | for yourself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | you declare | (end quote)  
‘Is it to this extent, Āvuso Kālāma, that you declare this Dhamma, having realised it for yourself through experiential understanding and entered upon it?’

952.1 ‘Ettāvatā kho ahaṃ, āvuso, imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedemī’ti.  
⏏️(ettāvatā) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) ⏏️(āvuso) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟠⨀👆▶️(pavedeti) ⏏️(iti)  
to this extent | indeed | I | Āvuso | this | Dhamma | for myself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | I declare | (end quote)  
‘To this extent indeed, Āvuso, do I declare this Dhamma, having realised it for myself through experiential understanding and entered upon it.’

953.1 ‘Ahampi kho, āvuso, ettāvatā imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmī’ti.  
*️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) ⏏️(api) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(āvuso) ⏏️(ettāvatā) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀👆▶️(viharati) ⏏️(iti)  
I | too | indeed | Āvuso | to this extent | this | Dhamma | for myself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | I dwell in | (end quote)  
‘Indeed I too, Āvuso, to this extent enter and dwell in this Dhamma, having realised it for myself through experiential understanding.’

954.1 ‘Lābhā no, āvuso, suladdhaṃ no, āvuso, ye mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ tādisaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ passāma.  
⏏️(lābhā) *️⃣👆⚧⨂④(ahaṃ) ⏏️(āvuso) ⏏️(suladdhaṃ) *️⃣👆⚧⨂④(ahaṃ) ⏏️(āvuso) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ya) *️⃣👆⚧⨂①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀②(āyasmant) 🚹⨀②(tādisa) 🚹⨀②🆎(sa+brahmacārī) 🟢⨂👆▶️(passati)  
it is a gain | for us | Āvuso | it is a great gain | for us | Āvuso | who | we | the venerable one | such | a companion in the pure life | we see  
‘It is a gain for us, Āvuso, it is a great gain for us, that we see such a venerable one as a companion in the pure life.

954.2 Iti yāhaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedemi taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharasi.  
⏏️(iti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ya) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟠⨀👆▶️(pavedeti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀🤘▶️(viharati)  
thus | which | I | Dhamma | for myself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | I declare | that | you | Dhamma | for yourself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | you dwell in  
Thus, the Dhamma that I declare, having realised it for myself through experiential understanding and entered upon it, that Dhamma you enter and dwell in, having realised it for yourself through experiential understanding.

954.3 Yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharasi tamahaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedemi.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ya) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀🤘▶️(viharati) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟠⨀👆▶️(pavedeti)  
which | you | Dhamma | for yourself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | you dwell in | that | I | Dhamma | for myself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | I declare  
The Dhamma that you enter and dwell in, having realised it for yourself through experiential understanding, that Dhamma I declare, having realised it for myself through experiential understanding and entered upon it.

954.4 Iti yāhaṃ dhammaṃ jānāmi taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi, yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi tamahaṃ dhammaṃ jānāmi.  
⏏️(iti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ya) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🟢⨀👆▶️(jānāti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🟢⨀🤘▶️(jānāti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ya) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🟢⨀🤘▶️(jānāti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🟢⨀👆▶️(jānāti)  
thus | which | I | Dhamma | I know | that | you | Dhamma | you know | which | you | Dhamma | you know | that | I | Dhamma | I know  
Thus, the Dhamma that I know, that Dhamma you know; the Dhamma that you know, that Dhamma I know.

954.5 Iti yādiso ahaṃ tādiso tuvaṃ, yādiso tuvaṃ tādiso ahaṃ.  
⏏️(iti) 🚹⨀①(yādisa) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀①(tādisa) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨀①(yādisa) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨀①(tādisa) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ)  
thus | of whatever kind | I | of such kind | you | of whatever kind | you | of such kind | I  
Thus, as I am, so are you; as you are, so am I.

954.6 Ehi dāni, āvuso, ubhova santā imaṃ gaṇaṃ pariharāmā’ti.  
🟢⨀🤘⏹(eti) ⏏️(dāni) ⏏️(āvuso) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ubha) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨂①🔽(anta)(santa) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(gaṇa) 🟢⨂👆⏹(pariharati) ⏏️(iti)  
come | now | Āvuso | both | just | being present | this | company | let us lead | (end quote)  
Come now, Āvuso, let us both, being present, lead this company.’

955.1 Iti kho, bhikkhave, āḷāro kālāmo ācariyo me samāno attano antevāsiṃ maṃ samānaṃ attanā samasamaṃ ṭhapesi, uḷārāya ca maṃ pūjāya pūjesi.  
⏏️(iti) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀①(āḷāra) 🚹⨀①(kālāma) 🚹⨀①(ācariya) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚹⨀⑥(atta) 🚹⨀②🆎(ante+vāsī) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀②🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚹⨀③(atta) 🚹⨀②🆎(sama+sama) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(ṭhapeti) 🚺⨀③(uḷāra) ⏏️(ca) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) 🚺⨀③(pūjā) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(pūjeti)  
thus | indeed | Bhikkhave | Āḷāra | Kālāma | teacher | my | being | his own | pupil | me | being | with himself | on the same level | placed | with great | and | me | honour | honoured  
Thus, Bhikkhave, Āḷāra Kālāma, being my teacher, placed me, his own pupil, on the same level as himself, and honoured me with great honour.

955.2 Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘nāyaṃ dhammo nibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati, yāvadeva ākiñcaññāyatanūpapattiyā’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) ⏏️(na) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀①(dhamma) 🚺⨀④(nibbidā) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨀④(virāga) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨀④(nirodha) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨀④(upasama) ⏏️(na) 🚺⨀④(abhiññā) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨀④(sambodha) ⏏️(na) 🚻⨀④(nibbāna) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(saṃvattati) ⏏️(yāva) ⏏️(eva) 🚺⨀④🆎(ākiñcañña+āyatana+upapatti) ⏏️(iti)  
to that | to me | Bhikkhave | this | was | not | this | Dhamma | to giving up | nor | to detachment | nor | to cessation | nor | to calm | nor | to experiential understanding | nor | to full awakening | nor | to extinguishment | leads | as far as | only | to reappearance in the base of nothingness | (end quote)  
Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me: ‘This Dhamma does not lead to giving up, nor to dispassion, nor to cessation, nor to calm, nor to experiential understanding, nor to full awakening, nor to extinguishment; it leads only to reappearance in the base of nothingness.’

955.3 So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, taṃ dhammaṃ analaṅkaritvā tasmā dhammā nibbijja apakkamiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🔽(tvā)🆎(na+alaṅkaroti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑤(ta) 🚹⨀⑤(dhamma) 🔽(ya)(nibbindati) 🟢⨀👆⏮(apakkamati)  
that | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | that | Dhamma | not valuing | from that | Dhamma | having given up | I departed  
So, Bhikkhave, not valuing that Dhamma, having given up that Dhamma, I departed.

## Uddaka son of Rāma

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
956\. So, Bhikkhave, seeking what is wholesome, searching for the unsurpassed state of supreme peace, I approached where Uddaka son of Rāma was. Having approached, I said this to Uddaka son of Rāma: “I wish, Āvuso, to lead the pure life in this Dhamma and Discipline.” When this was said, Bhikkhave, Uddaka son of Rāma said this to me: “May the venerable one stay; such is this Dhamma that a wise person, in no long time, having realised his own teacher's doctrine for himself through experiential understanding, may enter and dwell in it.” So, Bhikkhave, quickly, in no long time, I learned that Dhamma. So, Bhikkhave, merely by lip-service, by mere verbal declaration, I would speak of the doctrine of knowledge and the doctrine of elders, and I would claim, ‘I know, I see,’ and so would others. Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me: ‘It was not by mere faith alone that Rāma declared this Dhamma as something he entered and dwelt in, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding; surely Rāma dwelt in this Dhamma knowing and seeing it.’

957\. Then, Bhikkhave, I approached where Uddaka son of Rāma was; having approached, I said this to Uddaka son of Rāma: ‘To what extent, Āvuso, did Rāma declare this Dhamma as something he entered and dwelt in, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding?’ When this was said, Bhikkhave, Uddaka son of Rāma declared the `nevasaññānāsaññāyatana` (base of neither apperception nor non-apperception). Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me: ‘It was not only Rāma who had faith; I also have faith; it was not only Rāma who had energy; I also have energy; it was not only Rāma who had awareness; I also have awareness; it was not only Rāma who had focus; I also have focus; it was not only Rāma who had wisdom; I also have wisdom. What if I were to strive for the realisation of that Dhamma which Rāma declared he entered and dwelt in, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding?’ So, Bhikkhave, quickly, in no long time, having realised that Dhamma for myself through experiential understanding, I entered and dwelt in it.

958\. Then, Bhikkhave, I approached where Uddaka son of Rāma was; having approached, I said this to Uddaka son of Rāma:

959\. ‘Was it to this extent, Āvuso, that Rāma declared this Dhamma, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding and entered upon it?’

960\. ‘To this extent indeed, Āvuso, did Rāma declare this Dhamma, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding and entered upon it.’

961\. ‘Indeed I too, Āvuso, to this extent enter and dwell in this Dhamma, having realised it for myself through experiential understanding.’

962\. ‘It is a gain for us, Āvuso, it is a great gain for us, that we see such a venerable one as a companion in the pure life. Thus, the Dhamma that Rāma declared, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding and entered upon it, that Dhamma you enter and dwell in, having realised it for yourself through experiential understanding. The Dhamma that you enter and dwell in, having realised it for yourself through experiential understanding, that Dhamma Rāma declared, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding and entered upon it. Thus, the Dhamma that Rāma experientially understood, that Dhamma you know; the Dhamma that you know, that Dhamma Rāma experientially understood. Thus, as Rāma was, so are you; as you are, so was Rāma. Come now, Āvuso, you lead this company.’

963\. Thus, Bhikkhave, Uddaka son of Rāma, being my companion in the pure life, placed me in the position of teacher, and honoured me with great honour. Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me: ‘This Dhamma does not lead to giving up, nor to dispassion, nor to cessation, nor to calm, nor to experiential understanding, nor to full awakening, nor to extinguishment; it leads only to reappearance in the base of neither apperception nor non-apperception.’ So, Bhikkhave, not valuing that Dhamma, having given up that Dhamma, I departed.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
956\. So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, kiṃ kusalagavesī anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ pariyesamāno yena udako rāmaputto tenupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ etadavocaṃ—  ‘icchāmahaṃ, āvuso, imasmiṃ dhammavinaye brahmacariyaṃ caritun’ti. Evaṃ vutte, bhikkhave, udako rāmaputto maṃ etadavoca—  ‘viharatāyasmā; tādiso ayaṃ dhammo yattha viññū puriso nacirasseva sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyyā’ti. So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, nacirasseva khippameva taṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇiṃ. So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, tāvatakeneva oṭṭhapahatamattena lapitalāpanamattena ñāṇavādañca vadāmi theravādañca, ‘jānāmi passāmī’ti ca paṭijānāmi ahañceva aññe ca. Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘na kho rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ kevalaṃ saddhāmattakena sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedesi; addhā rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ vihāsī’ti.

957\. Atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhave, yena udako rāmaputto tenupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ etadavocaṃ—  ‘kittāvatā no, āvuso, rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedesī’ti? Evaṃ vutte, bhikkhave, udako rāmaputto nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ pavedesi. Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘na kho rāmasseva ahosi saddhā, mayhampatthi saddhā; na kho rāmasseva ahosi vīriyaṃ, mayhampatthi vīriyaṃ; na kho rāmasseva ahosi sati, mayhampatthi sati; na kho rāmasseva ahosi samādhi, mayhampatthi samādhi, na kho rāmasseva ahosi paññā, mayhampatthi paññā. Yannūnāhaṃ yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmo sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedesi, tassa dhammassa sacchikiriyāya padaheyyan’ti. So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, nacirasseva khippameva taṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihāsiṃ.

958\. Atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhave, yena udako rāmaputto tenupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ etadavocaṃ—

959\. ‘Ettāvatā no, āvuso, rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesī’ti?

960\. ‘Ettāvatā kho, āvuso, rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesī’ti.

961\. ‘Ahampi kho, āvuso, ettāvatā imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmī’ti.

962\. ‘Lābhā no, āvuso, suladdhaṃ no, āvuso, ye mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ tādisaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ passāma. Iti yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmo sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesi, taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharasi. Yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharasi, taṃ dhammaṃ rāmo sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesi. Iti yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmo abhiññāsi taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi, yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi, taṃ dhammaṃ rāmo abhiññāsi. Iti yādiso rāmo ahosi tādiso tuvaṃ, yādiso tuvaṃ tādiso rāmo ahosi. Ehi dāni, āvuso, tuvaṃ imaṃ gaṇaṃ pariharā’ti.

963\. Iti kho, bhikkhave, udako rāmaputto sabrahmacārī me samāno ācariyaṭṭhāne maṃ ṭhapesi, uḷārāya ca maṃ pūjāya pūjesi. Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘nāyaṃ dhammo nibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati, yāvadeva nevasaññānāsaññāyatanūpapattiyā’ti. So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, taṃ dhammaṃ analaṅkaritvā tasmā dhammā nibbijja apakkamiṃ.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
  956\. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀓𑀺𑀁 𑀓𑀼𑀲𑀮𑀕𑀯𑁂𑀲𑀻 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺𑀯𑀭𑀧𑀤𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀁; 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑀁 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀁—  ‘𑀇𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀸𑀫𑀳𑀁, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀇𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀯𑀺𑀦𑀬𑁂 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀢𑀼𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺. 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀫𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘—  ‘𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀢𑀸𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀸; 𑀢𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀬𑀢𑁆𑀣 𑀯𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀽 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀦𑀘𑀺𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀲𑀓𑀁 𑀆𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀓𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀦𑀘𑀺𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀢𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀧𑀼𑀡𑀺𑀁. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀢𑀓𑁂𑀦𑁂𑀯 𑀑𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀧𑀳𑀢𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀦 𑀮𑀧𑀺𑀢𑀮𑀸𑀧𑀦𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀦 𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀯𑀸𑀤𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀯𑀤𑀸𑀫𑀺 𑀣𑁂𑀭𑀯𑀸𑀤𑀜𑁆𑀘, ‘𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀫𑀺 𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀻’𑀢𑀺 𑀘 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀫𑀺 𑀅𑀳𑀜𑁆𑀘𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀜𑁆𑀜𑁂 𑀘. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀓𑁂𑀯𑀮𑀁 𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸𑀫𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀓𑁂𑀦 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀻𑀢𑀺 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀺; 𑀅𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀸𑀲𑀻’𑀢𑀺.
  
  957\. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀁; 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑀁 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀁—  ‘𑀓𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀦𑁄, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀻𑀢𑀺 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀻’𑀢𑀺? 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀦𑁂𑀯𑀲𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀬𑀢𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀺. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺 𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸, 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸; 𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺 𑀯𑀻𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁, 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀯𑀻𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁; 𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺 𑀲𑀢𑀺, 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀲𑀢𑀺; 𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀥𑀺, 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀥𑀺, 𑀦 𑀔𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸, 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸. 𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀽𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀬𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀻𑀢𑀺 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀺, 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀺𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀬 𑀧𑀤𑀳𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀦𑀘𑀺𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀢𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁.
  
  958\. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀁; 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑀁 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀁—
  
  959\. ‘𑀏𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀦𑁄, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀻’𑀢𑀺?
  
  960\. ‘𑀏𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀔𑁄, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀻’𑀢𑀺.
  
  961\. ‘𑀅𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀏𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀻’𑀢𑀺.
  
  962\. ‘𑀮𑀸𑀪𑀸 𑀦𑁄, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀲𑀼𑀮𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀁 𑀦𑁄, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀬𑁂 𑀫𑀬𑀁 𑀆𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀢𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑀁 𑀲𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀘𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀁 𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀫. 𑀇𑀢𑀺 𑀬𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀺, 𑀢𑀁 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀲𑀺. 𑀬𑀁 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀲𑀺, 𑀢𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀧𑀯𑁂𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀺. 𑀇𑀢𑀺 𑀬𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀲𑀺 𑀢𑀁 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀺, 𑀬𑀁 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀺, 𑀢𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀲𑀺. 𑀇𑀢𑀺 𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺 𑀢𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀢𑀼𑀯𑀁, 𑀬𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀢𑀼𑀯𑀁 𑀢𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺. 𑀏𑀳𑀺 𑀤𑀸𑀦𑀺, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀢𑀼𑀯𑀁 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀕𑀡𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀸’𑀢𑀺.
  
  963\. 𑀇𑀢𑀺 𑀔𑁄, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀘𑀸𑀭𑀻 𑀫𑁂 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀆𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀸𑀦𑁂 𑀫𑀁 𑀞𑀧𑁂𑀲𑀺, 𑀉𑀍𑀆𑀭𑀸𑀬 𑀘 𑀫𑀁 𑀧𑀽𑀚𑀸𑀬 𑀧𑀽𑀚𑁂𑀲𑀺. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀦𑀸𑀬𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀺𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀯𑀺𑀭𑀸𑀕𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀦𑀺𑀭𑁄𑀥𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑁄𑀥𑀸𑀬 𑀦 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀬 𑀲𑀁𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀢𑀺, 𑀬𑀸𑀯𑀤𑁂𑀯 𑀦𑁂𑀯𑀲𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀬𑀢𑀦𑀽𑀧𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀢𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀮𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀭𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀺𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀅𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀁.
</TabItem>
<TabItem label="Interlinear">
956.1 So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, kiṃ kusalagavesī anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ pariyesamāno yena udako rāmaputto tenupasaṅkamiṃ;  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(kiṃ) 🚹⨀①🆎(kusala+gavesī) 🚻⨀②🆎(na+uttara) 🚻⨀②🆎(santi+vara+pada) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(pariyesamāna) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨀①(udaka) 🚹⨀①(rāmaputta) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(upasaṅkamati)  
that | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | what | seeking what is wholesome | unsurpassed | state of supreme peace | searching for | by where | Udaka | Rāmaputta | by there | I approached  
So, Bhikkhave, seeking what is wholesome, searching for the unsurpassed state of supreme peace, I approached where Uddaka son of Rāma was.

956.2 upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ etadavocaṃ—  ‘icchāmahaṃ, āvuso, imasmiṃ dhammavinaye brahmacariyaṃ caritun’ti.  
🔽(tvā)(upasaṅkamati) 🚹⨀②(udaka) 🚹⨀②(rāmaputta) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(vadati) 🟢⨀👆▶️(icchati) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) ⏏️(āvuso) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑦(ima) 🚹⨀⑦🆎(dhamma+vinaya) 🚻⨀②🆎(brahma+cariya) 🔽(tuṃ)(carati) ⏏️(iti)  
having approached | Udaka | Rāmaputta | this | I said | I wish | I | Āvuso | in this | Dhamma and Discipline | the pure life | to lead | (end quote)  
Having approached, I said this to Uddaka son of Rāma: “I wish, Āvuso, to lead the pure life in this Dhamma and Discipline.”

956.3 Evaṃ vutte, bhikkhave, udako rāmaputto maṃ etadavoca—  ‘viharatāyasmā;  
⏏️(evaṃ) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(vutta) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀①(udaka) 🚹⨀①(rāmaputta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(vadati) 🟢⨀🤟⏹(viharati) 🚹⨀①(āyasmant)  
thus | said | Bhikkhave | Udaka | Rāmaputta | me | this | said | may stay | the venerable one  
When this was said, Bhikkhave, Uddaka son of Rāma said this to me: “May the venerable one stay;

956.4 tādiso ayaṃ dhammo yattha viññū puriso nacirasseva sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihareyyā’ti.  
🚹⨀①(tādisa) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀①(dhamma) ⏏️(yattha) 🚹⨀①(viññū) 🚹⨀①(purisa) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(cirassa) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀②(saka) 🚹⨀②(ācariyaka) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(viharati) ⏏️(iti)  
such | this | Dhamma | in which | wise | person | not | after a long time | just | his own | teacher's doctrine | for himself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | may dwell | (end quote)  
such is this Dhamma that a wise person, in no long time, having realised his own teacher's doctrine for himself through experiential understanding, may enter and dwell in it.”

956.5 So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, nacirasseva khippameva taṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(cirassa) ⏏️(eva) 🚻⨀②(khippa) ⏏️(eva) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🟢⨀👆⏮(pariyāpuṇāti)  
that | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | not | after a long time | just | quickly | just | that | Dhamma | I learned  
So, Bhikkhave, quickly, in no long time, I learned that Dhamma.

956.6 So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, tāvatakeneva oṭṭhapahatamattena lapitalāpanamattena ñāṇavādañca vadāmi theravādañca, ‘jānāmi passāmī’ti ca paṭijānāmi ahañceva aññe ca.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚻⨀③(tāvataka) ⏏️(eva) 🚻⨀③🆎(oṭṭha+pahata+matta) 🚻⨀③🆎(lapita+lāpana+matta) 🚹⨀②🆎(ñāṇa+vāda) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨀👆▶️(vadati) 🚹⨀②🆎(thera+vāda) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨀👆▶️(jānāti) 🟢⨀👆▶️(passati) ⏏️(iti) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨀👆▶️(paṭijānāti) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) ⏏️(ca) ⏏️(eva) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(añña) ⏏️(ca)  
that | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | by just so much | merely | by mere lip-service | by mere verbal declaration | the doctrine of knowledge | and | I speak of | the doctrine of elders | and | I know | I see | (end quote) | and | I claim | I | and | indeed | others | and  
So, Bhikkhave, merely by lip-service, by mere verbal declaration, I would speak of the doctrine of knowledge and the doctrine of elders, and I would claim, ‘I know, I see,’ and so would others.

956.7 Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘na kho rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ kevalaṃ saddhāmattakena sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedesi;  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀①(rāma) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🚻⨀②(kevala) 🚻⨀③🆎(saddhā+mattaka) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀👆▶️(viharati) ⏏️(iti) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(pavedeti)  
to that | to me | Bhikkhave | this | was | not | indeed | Rāma | this | Dhamma | only | by mere faith | for himself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | I dwell in | (end quote) | declared  
Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me: ‘It was not by mere faith alone that Rāma declared this Dhamma as something he entered and dwelt in, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding;

956.8 addhā rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ vihāsī’ti.  
⏏️(addhā) 🚹⨀①(rāma) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🚹⨀①🔽(anta)(jānanta) 🚹⨀①🔽(anta)(passanta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(viharati) ⏏️(iti)  
surely | Rāma | this | Dhamma | knowing | seeing | dwelt in | (end quote)  
surely Rāma dwelt in this Dhamma knowing and seeing it.’

957.1 Atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhave, yena udako rāmaputto tenupasaṅkamiṃ;  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨀①(udaka) 🚹⨀①(rāmaputta) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(upasaṅkamati)  
then | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | by where | Udaka | Rāmaputta | by there | I approached  
Then, Bhikkhave, I approached where Uddaka son of Rāma was;

957.2 upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ etadavocaṃ—  ‘kittāvatā no, āvuso, rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedesī’ti?  
🔽(tvā)(upasaṅkamati) 🚹⨀②(udaka) 🚹⨀②(rāmaputta) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(vadati) ⏏️(kittāvatā) ⏏️(no) ⏏️(āvuso) 🚹⨀①(rāma) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀👆▶️(viharati) ⏏️(iti) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(pavedeti) ⏏️(iti)  
having approached | Udaka | Rāmaputta | this | I said | to what extent | then | Āvuso | Rāma | this | Dhamma | for himself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | I dwell in | (end quote) | declared | (end quote)  
having approached, I said this to Uddaka son of Rāma: ‘To what extent, Āvuso, did Rāma declare this Dhamma as something he entered and dwelt in, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding?’

957.3 Evaṃ vutte, bhikkhave, udako rāmaputto nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ pavedesi.  
⏏️(evaṃ) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(vutta) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀①(udaka) 🚹⨀①(rāmaputta) 🚻⨀②🆎(na+eva+saññā+na+saññā+āyatana) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(pavedeti)  
thus | when said | Bhikkhave | Udaka | Rāmaputta | the base of neither apperception nor non-apperception | declared  
When this was said, Bhikkhave, Uddaka son of Rāma declared the `nevasaññānāsaññāyatana` (base of neither apperception nor non-apperception).

957.4 Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘na kho rāmasseva ahosi saddhā, mayhampatthi saddhā;  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⑥(rāma) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) 🚺⨀①(saddhā) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) ⏏️(api) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) 🚺⨀①(saddhā)  
to that | to me | Bhikkhave | this | was | not | indeed | of Rāma | only | was | faith | of me | also | there is | faith  
Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me: ‘It was not only Rāma who had faith; I also have faith;

957.5 na kho rāmasseva ahosi vīriyaṃ, mayhampatthi vīriyaṃ;  
⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⑥(rāma) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) 🚻⨀①(vīriya) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) ⏏️(api) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) 🚻⨀①(vīriya)  
not | indeed | of Rāma | only | was | energy | of me | also | there is | energy  
it was not only Rāma who had energy; I also have energy;

957.6 na kho rāmasseva ahosi sati, mayhampatthi sati;  
⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⑥(rāma) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) 🚺⨀①(sati) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) ⏏️(api) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) 🚺⨀①(sati)  
not | indeed | of Rāma | only | was | awareness | of me | also | there is | awareness  
it was not only Rāma who had awareness; I also have awareness;

957.7 na kho rāmasseva ahosi samādhi, mayhampatthi samādhi, na kho rāmasseva ahosi paññā, mayhampatthi paññā.  
⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⑥(rāma) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) 🚹⨀①(samādhi) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) ⏏️(api) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) 🚹⨀①(samādhi) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨀⑥(rāma) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) 🚺⨀①(paññā) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) ⏏️(api) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) 🚺⨀①(paññā)  
not | indeed | of Rāma | only | was | focus | of me | also | there is | focus | not | indeed | of Rāma | only | was | wisdom | of me | also | there is | wisdom  
it was not only Rāma who had focus; I also have focus; it was not only Rāma who had wisdom; I also have wisdom.

957.8 Yannūnāhaṃ yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmo sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmīti pavedesi, tassa dhammassa sacchikiriyāya padaheyyan’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ya) ⏏️(nūna) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ya) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🚹⨀①(rāma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀👆▶️(viharati) ⏏️(iti) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(pavedeti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑥(ta) 🚹⨀⑥(dhamma) 🚺⨀④🆎(sacchi+kiriyā) 🔵⨀👆⏯(padahati) ⏏️(iti)  
what | if | I | which | Dhamma | Rāma | for himself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | I dwell in | (end quote) | declared | of that | Dhamma | for the realisation | I were to strive | (end quote)  
What if I were to strive for the realisation of that Dhamma which Rāma declared he entered and dwelt in, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding?’

957.9 So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, nacirasseva khippameva taṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja vihāsiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(cirassa) ⏏️(eva) 🚻⨀②(khippa) ⏏️(eva) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀👆⏮(viharati)  
that | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | not | after a long time | just | quickly | just | that | Dhamma | for myself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | I dwelt  
So, Bhikkhave, quickly, in no long time, having realised that Dhamma for myself through experiential understanding, I entered and dwelt in it.

958.1 Atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhave, yena udako rāmaputto tenupasaṅkamiṃ;  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨀①(udaka) 🚹⨀①(rāmaputta) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(upasaṅkamati)  
then | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | by where | Udaka | Rāmaputta | by there | I approached  
Then, Bhikkhave, I approached where Uddaka son of Rāma was;

958.2 upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ etadavocaṃ—  
🔽(tvā)(upasaṅkamati) 🚹⨀②(udaka) 🚹⨀②(rāmaputta) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(vadati)  
having approached | Udaka | Rāmaputta | this | I said  
having approached, I said this to Uddaka son of Rāma:

959.1 ‘Ettāvatā no, āvuso, rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesī’ti?  
⏏️(ettāvatā) ⏏️(no) ⏏️(āvuso) 🚹⨀①(rāma) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(pavedeti) ⏏️(iti)  
to this extent | then | Āvuso | Rāma | this | Dhamma | for himself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | declared | (end quote)  
‘Was it to this extent, Āvuso, that Rāma declared this Dhamma, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding and entered upon it?’

960.1 ‘Ettāvatā kho, āvuso, rāmo imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesī’ti.  
⏏️(ettāvatā) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(āvuso) 🚹⨀①(rāma) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(pavedeti) ⏏️(iti)  
to this extent | indeed | Āvuso | Rāma | this | Dhamma | for himself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | declared | (end quote)  
‘To this extent indeed, Āvuso, did Rāma declare this Dhamma, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding and entered upon it.’

961.1 ‘Ahampi kho, āvuso, ettāvatā imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharāmī’ti.  
*️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) ⏏️(api) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(āvuso) ⏏️(ettāvatā) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀👆▶️(viharati) ⏏️(iti)  
I | too | indeed | Āvuso | to this extent | this | Dhamma | for myself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | I dwell in | (end quote)  
‘Indeed I too, Āvuso, to this extent enter and dwell in this Dhamma, having realised it for myself through experiential understanding.’

962.1 ‘Lābhā no, āvuso, suladdhaṃ no, āvuso, ye mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ tādisaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ passāma.  
⏏️(lābhā) *️⃣👆⚧⨂④(ahaṃ) ⏏️(āvuso) ⏏️(suladdhaṃ) *️⃣👆⚧⨂④(ahaṃ) ⏏️(āvuso) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ya) *️⃣👆⚧⨂①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀②(āyasmant) 🚹⨀②(tādisa) 🚹⨀②🆎(sa+brahmacārī) 🟢⨂👆▶️(passati)  
it is a gain | for us | Āvuso | it is a great gain | for us | Āvuso | who | we | the venerable one | such | a companion in the pure life | we see  
‘It is a gain for us, Āvuso, it is a great gain for us, that we see such a venerable one as a companion in the pure life.

962.2 Iti yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmo sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesi, taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharasi.  
⏏️(iti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ya) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🚹⨀①(rāma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(pavedeti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀🤘▶️(viharati)  
thus | which | Dhamma | Rāma | for himself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | declared | that | you | Dhamma | for yourself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | you dwell in  
Thus, the Dhamma that Rāma declared, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding and entered upon it, that Dhamma you enter and dwell in, having realised it for yourself through experiential understanding.

962.3 Yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharasi, taṃ dhammaṃ rāmo sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja pavedesi.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ya) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨀🤘▶️(viharati) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🚹⨀①(rāma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(pavedeti)  
which | you | Dhamma | for yourself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | you dwell in | that | Dhamma | Rāma | for himself | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | declared  
The Dhamma that you enter and dwell in, having realised it for yourself through experiential understanding, that Dhamma Rāma declared, having realised it for himself through experiential understanding and entered upon it.

962.4 Iti yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmo abhiññāsi taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi, yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi, taṃ dhammaṃ rāmo abhiññāsi.  
⏏️(iti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ya) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🚹⨀①(rāma) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(abhijānāti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🟢⨀🤘▶️(jānāti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ya) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🟢⨀🤘▶️(jānāti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🚹⨀①(rāma) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(abhijānāti)  
thus | which | Dhamma | Rāma | experientially understood | that | you | Dhamma | you know | which | you | Dhamma | you know | that | Dhamma | Rāma | experientially understood  
Thus, the Dhamma that Rāma experientially understood, that Dhamma you know; the Dhamma that you know, that Dhamma Rāma experientially understood.

962.5 Iti yādiso rāmo ahosi tādiso tuvaṃ, yādiso tuvaṃ tādiso rāmo ahosi.  
⏏️(iti) 🚹⨀①(yādisa) 🚹⨀①(rāma) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) 🚹⨀①(tādisa) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨀①(yādisa) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨀①(tādisa) 🚹⨀①(rāma) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti)  
thus | of whatever kind | Rāma | was | of such kind | you | of whatever kind | you | of such kind | Rāma | was  
Thus, as Rāma was, so are you; as you are, so was Rāma.

962.6 Ehi dāni, āvuso, tuvaṃ imaṃ gaṇaṃ pariharā’ti.  
🟢⨀🤘⏹(eti) ⏏️(dāni) ⏏️(āvuso) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ima) 🚹⨀②(gaṇa) 🟢⨀🤘⏹(pariharati) ⏏️(iti)  
come | now | Āvuso | you | this | company | lead | (end quote)  
Come now, Āvuso, you lead this company.’

963.1 Iti kho, bhikkhave, udako rāmaputto sabrahmacārī me samāno ācariyaṭṭhāne maṃ ṭhapesi, uḷārāya ca maṃ pūjāya pūjesi.  
⏏️(iti) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀①(udaka) 🚹⨀①(rāmaputta) 🚹⨀①🆎(sa+brahmacārī) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(samāna) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(ācariya+ṭhāna) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) 🟠⨀🤟⏮(ṭhapeti) 🚺⨀③(uḷāra) ⏏️(ca) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) 🚺⨀③(pūjā) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(pūjeti)  
thus | indeed | Bhikkhave | Udaka | Rāmaputta | companion in the pure life | my | being | in the position of teacher | me | placed | with great | and | me | honour | honoured  
Thus, Bhikkhave, Uddaka son of Rāma, being my companion in the pure life, placed me in the position of teacher, and honoured me with great honour.

963.2 Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘nāyaṃ dhammo nibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati, yāvadeva nevasaññānāsaññāyatanūpapattiyā’ti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) ⏏️(na) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀①(dhamma) 🚺⨀④(nibbidā) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨀④(virāga) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨀④(nirodha) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨀④(upasama) ⏏️(na) 🚺⨀④(abhiññā) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨀④(sambodha) ⏏️(na) 🚻⨀④(nibbāna) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(saṃvattati) ⏏️(yāva) ⏏️(eva) 🚺⨀④🆎(na+eva+saññā+na+saññā+āyatana+upapatti) ⏏️(iti)  
to that | to me | Bhikkhave | this | was | not | this | Dhamma | to giving up | nor | to detachment | nor | to cessation | nor | to calm | nor | to experiential understanding | nor | to full awakening | nor | to extinguishment | leads | as far as | only | to reappearance in the base of neither apperception nor non-apperception | (end quote)  
Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me: ‘This Dhamma does not lead to giving up, nor to dispassion, nor to cessation, nor to calm, nor to experiential understanding, nor to full awakening, nor to extinguishment; it leads only to reappearance in the base of neither apperception nor non-apperception.’

963.3 So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, taṃ dhammaṃ analaṅkaritvā tasmā dhammā nibbijja apakkamiṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(ta) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🔽(tvā)🆎(na+alaṅkaroti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑤(ta) 🚹⨀⑤(dhamma) 🔽(ya)(nibbindati) 🟢⨀👆⏮(apakkamati)  
that | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | that | Dhamma | not valuing | from that | Dhamma | having given up | I departed  
So, Bhikkhave, not valuing that Dhamma, having given up that Dhamma, I departed.

## Awakening

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
964\. So, Bhikkhave, I, seeking what is wholesome, searching for the unsurpassed state of supreme peace, wandering by stages through Magadha, arrived at Uruvelā, the army township. There I saw a delightful stretch of land, a beautiful grove, a clear-flowing river with pleasant banks, and a village for alms around it. Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me: ‘Indeed, delightful is this stretch of land, beautiful is this grove, clear-flowing is this river with pleasant banks, and a village for alms is around it. This is indeed suitable for a young man of good family intent on striving.’ So, Bhikkhave, I sat down right there, thinking, ‘This is enough for striving.’

965\. That indeed, Bhikkhave, from my own doing I was liable to birth, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to birth, searching for the unsurpassed sanctuary of extinguishment from being born, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of extinguishment from being born. From my own doing I was liable to old age, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to old age, searching for the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-ageing through extinguishment, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-ageing through extinguishment. From my own doing I was liable to sickness, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to sickness, searching for the unsurpassed sanctuary of health through extinguishment, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of health through extinguishment. From my own doing I was liable to death, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to death, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-death through extinguishment. From my own doing I was liable to sorrow, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to sorrow, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-sorrow through extinguishment. From my own doing I was liable to defilement, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to defilement, searching for the unsurpassed sanctuary of undefilement through extinguishment, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of undefilement through extinguishment. And knowledge and insight arose in me: ‘Unshakeable is my liberation, this is the final birth (of suffering), there is now no more appearing again (of suffering).’
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
964\. So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, kiṃ kusalagavesī anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ pariyesamāno magadhesu anupubbena cārikaṃ caramāno yena uruvelā senānigamo tadavasariṃ. Tatthaddasaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ bhūmibhāgaṃ, pāsādikañca vanasaṇḍaṃ, nadiñca sandantiṃ setakaṃ supatitthaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ, samantā ca gocaragāmaṃ. Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘ramaṇīyo vata bho bhūmibhāgo, pāsādiko ca vanasaṇḍo, nadī ca sandati setakā supatitthā ramaṇīyā, samantā ca gocaragāmo. Alaṃ vatidaṃ kulaputtassa padhānatthikassa padhānāyā’ti. So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, tattheva nisīdiṃ—  alamidaṃ padhānāyāti.

965\. So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, attanā jātidhammo samāno jātidhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesamāno ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ, attanā jarādhammo samāno jarādhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesamāno ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ, attanā byādhidhammo samāno byādhidhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā abyādhiṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesamāno abyādhiṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ, attanā maraṇadhammo samāno maraṇadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā amataṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ, attanā sokadhammo samāno sokadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā asokaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ, attanā saṃkilesadhammo samāno saṃkilesadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesamāno asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ. Ñāṇañca pana me dassanaṃ udapādi—  ‘akuppā me vimutti, ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavo’ti.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
964\. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀓𑀺𑀁 𑀓𑀼𑀲𑀮𑀕𑀯𑁂𑀲𑀻 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺𑀯𑀭𑀧𑀤𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀫𑀕𑀥𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂𑀦 𑀘𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀓𑀁 𑀘𑀭𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀉𑀭𑀼𑀯𑁂𑀮𑀸 𑀲𑁂𑀦𑀸𑀦𑀺𑀕𑀫𑁄 𑀢𑀤𑀯𑀲𑀭𑀺𑀁. 𑀢𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀤𑁆𑀤𑀲𑀁 𑀭𑀫𑀡𑀻𑀬𑀁 𑀪𑀽𑀫𑀺𑀪𑀸𑀕𑀁, 𑀧𑀸𑀲𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀓𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀯𑀦𑀲𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀁, 𑀦𑀤𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀤𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺𑀁 𑀲𑁂𑀢𑀓𑀁 𑀲𑀼𑀧𑀢𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀁 𑀭𑀫𑀡𑀻𑀬𑀁, 𑀲𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀸 𑀘 𑀕𑁄𑀘𑀭𑀕𑀸𑀫𑀁. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀭𑀫𑀡𑀻𑀬𑁄 𑀯𑀢 𑀪𑁄 𑀪𑀽𑀫𑀺𑀪𑀸𑀕𑁄, 𑀧𑀸𑀲𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀓𑁄 𑀘 𑀯𑀦𑀲𑀡𑁆𑀟𑁄, 𑀦𑀤𑀻 𑀘 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀤𑀢𑀺 𑀲𑁂𑀢𑀓𑀸 𑀲𑀼𑀧𑀢𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀸 𑀭𑀫𑀡𑀻𑀬𑀸, 𑀲𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀸 𑀘 𑀕𑁄𑀘𑀭𑀕𑀸𑀫𑁄. 𑀅𑀮𑀁 𑀯𑀢𑀺𑀤𑀁 𑀓𑀼𑀮𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀢𑀢𑁆𑀣𑁂𑀯 𑀦𑀺𑀲𑀻𑀤𑀺𑀁—  𑀅𑀮𑀫𑀺𑀤𑀁 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀬𑀸𑀢𑀺.

965\. 𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀅𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀕𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀚𑀭𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀅𑀚𑀭𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀕𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀅𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀕𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀫𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀕𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀕𑀫𑀁, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀅𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀕𑀫𑀁. 𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀧𑀦 𑀫𑁂 𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑀁 𑀉𑀤𑀧𑀸𑀤𑀺—  ‘𑀅𑀓𑀼𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸 𑀫𑁂 𑀯𑀺𑀫𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀺, 𑀅𑀬𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺𑀫𑀸 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺, 𑀦𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀤𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀧𑀼𑀦𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀯𑁄’𑀢𑀺.

964.1 So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, kiṃ kusalagavesī anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ pariyesamāno magadhesu anupubbena cārikaṃ caramāno yena uruvelā senānigamo tadavasariṃ.

that | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | what | seeking what is wholesome | unsurpassed | state of supreme peace | searching for | through Magadha | by stages | a journey | wandering | by where | Uruvelā | the army township | there | I arrived  
So, Bhikkhave, I, seeking what is wholesome, searching for the unsurpassed state of supreme peace, wandering by stages through Magadha, arrived at Uruvelā, the army township.

964.2 Tatthaddasaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ bhūmibhāgaṃ, pāsādikañca vanasaṇḍaṃ, nadiñca sandantiṃ setakaṃ supatitthaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ, samantā ca gocaragāmaṃ.  
⏏️(tattha) 🟢⨀👆⏮(passati) 🚹⨀②🔽(anīya)(ramaṇīya) 🚹⨀②🆎(bhūmi+bhāga) 🚹⨀②(pāsādika) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀②🆎(vana+saṇḍa) 🚺⨀②(nadī) ⏏️(ca) 🚺⨀②🔽(anta)(sandanta) 🚺⨀②(setaka) 🚺⨀②(supatittha) 🚺⨀②🔽(anīya)(ramaṇīya) ⏏️(samantā) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀②🆎(gocara+gāma)  
there | I saw | delightful | stretch of land | beautiful | and | grove | river | and | flowing | clear | with pleasant banks | delightful | around | and | a village for alms  
There I saw a delightful stretch of land, a beautiful grove, a clear-flowing river with pleasant banks, and a village for alms around it.

964.3 Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘ramaṇīyo vata bho bhūmibhāgo, pāsādiko ca vanasaṇḍo, nadī ca sandati setakā supatitthā ramaṇīyā, samantā ca gocaragāmo.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) 🚹⨀①🔽(anīya)(ramaṇīya) ⏏️(vata) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀①🆎(bhūmi+bhāga) 🚹⨀①(pāsādika) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀①🆎(vana+saṇḍa) 🚺⨀①(nadī) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(sandati) 🚺⨀①(setaka) 🚺⨀①(supatittha) 🚺⨀①🔽(anīya)(ramaṇīya) ⏏️(samantā) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀①🆎(gocara+gāma)  
to that | to me | Bhikkhave | this | was | delightful | indeed | Bho | stretch of land | beautiful | and | grove | river | and | flows | clear | with pleasant banks | delightful | around | and | a village for alms  
Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me: ‘Indeed, delightful is this stretch of land, beautiful is this grove, clear-flowing is this river with pleasant banks, and a village for alms is around it.

964.4 Alaṃ vatidaṃ kulaputtassa padhānatthikassa padhānāyā’ti.  
⏏️(alaṃ) ⏏️(vata) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀④🆎(kula+putta) 🚹⨀④🆎(padhāna+atthika) 🚻⨀④(padhāna) ⏏️(iti)  
suitable | indeed | this | for a young man of good family | intent on striving | for striving | (end quote)  
This is indeed suitable for a young man of good family intent on striving.’

964.5 So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, tattheva nisīdiṃ—  alamidaṃ padhānāyāti.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) ⏏️(tattha) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀👆⏮(nisīdati) ⏏️(alaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ima) 🚻⨀④(padhāna) ⏏️(iti)  
that | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | there | right | I sat down | enough | this | for striving | (end quote)  
So, Bhikkhave, I sat down right there, thinking, ‘This is enough for striving.’

965.1 So kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, attanā jātidhammo samāno jātidhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesamāno ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ, attanā jarādhammo samāno jarādhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesamāno ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ, attanā byādhidhammo samāno byādhidhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā abyādhiṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesamāno abyādhiṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ, attanā maraṇadhammo samāno maraṇadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā amataṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ, attanā sokadhammo samāno sokadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā asokaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ, attanā saṃkilesadhammo samāno saṃkilesadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesamāno asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ.

that | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | by myself | liable to birth | being | in liable to birth | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | unborn | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | searching for | unborn | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | achieved | by myself | liable to old age | being | in liable to old age | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | not ageing | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | searching for | not ageing | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | achieved | by myself | liable to get sick | being | in liable to get sick | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | healthy | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | searching for | healthy | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | achieved | by myself | liable to death | being | in liable to death | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | non-death | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | achieved | by myself | liable to sorrow | being | in liable to sorrow | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | non-sorrow | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | achieved | by myself | liable to defilement | being | in liable to defilement | unsatisfactoriness | having perceived | undefiled | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | searching for | undefiled | unsurpassed | sanctuary | extinguishment | achieved  
That indeed, Bhikkhave, from my own doing I was liable to birth, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to birth, searching for the unsurpassed sanctuary of extinguishment from being born, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of extinguishment from being born. From my own doing I was liable to old age, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to old age, searching for the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-ageing through extinguishment, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-ageing through extinguishment. From my own doing I was liable to sickness, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to sickness, searching for the unsurpassed sanctuary of health through extinguishment, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of health through extinguishment. From my own doing I was liable to death, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to death, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-death through extinguishment. From my own doing I was liable to sorrow, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to sorrow, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-sorrow through extinguishment. From my own doing I was liable to defilement, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to defilement, searching for the unsurpassed sanctuary of undefilement through extinguishment, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of undefilement through extinguishment.

965.2 Ñāṇañca pana me dassanaṃ udapādi—  ‘akuppā me vimutti, ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavo’ti.  
🚻⨀①(ñāṇa) ⏏️(ca) ⏏️(pana) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚻⨀①(dassana) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(uppajjati) 🚺⨀①🆎(na+kuppa) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚺⨀①(vimutti) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀①(ima) 🚺⨀①(antima) 🚺⨀①(jāti) ⏏️(na) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(atthi) ⏏️(dāni) 🚹⨀①🆎(puna+bhava) ⏏️(iti)  
knowledge | and | moreover | for me | insight | arose | unshakeable | my | liberation | this | final | birth | not | there is | now | appearing again | (end quote)  
And knowledge and insight arose in me: ‘Unshakeable is my liberation, this is the final birth (of suffering), there is now no more appearing again (of suffering).’
