## Decision to teach

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
966\. So, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me— ‘This Dhamma I have realised is indeed profound, hard to see, hard to comprehend, peaceful, refined, beyond the scope of reasoning, subtle, to be experienced by the wise. But this generation delights in attachment, is pleased by attachment, rejoices in attachment. For a generation delighting in attachment, pleased by attachment, rejoicing in attachment, this state is hard to see, namely: specific causality, `paṭiccasamuppāda` (dependent origination). This state, too, is indeed hard to see, namely: the calming of all `saṅkhārā` (mental constructions), the relinquishing of all acquisitions, the destruction of craving, dispassion, cessation, extinguishment. But if I were to teach the Dhamma and others did not understand me, that would be weariness for me, that would be trouble for me.’ And then, Bhikkhave, these unheard-before, wonderful verses occurred to me—

967\. ‘With difficulty have I realised it, \
enough now with proclaiming it; \
for those consumed by lust and hatred, \
this Dhamma is not easily understood. \
968\. Going against the stream, subtle, \
profound, hard to see, minute; \
those inflamed by lust will not see it, \
shrouded as they are in darkness.’

969\. So, Bhikkhave, as I reflected thus, my mind inclined to inaction, not to teaching the Dhamma. Then, Bhikkhave, Brahmā Sahampati, knowing my thought process with his own mind, had this thought— ‘Alas, the world is perishing, alas, the world is being destroyed, inasmuch as the mind of the Tathāgata, the `arahant` (liberated one), the `sammāsambuddha` (perfectly awakened one), inclines to inaction, not to teaching the Dhamma!’ Then, Bhikkhave, Brahmā Sahampati—just as a strong man might extend his flexed arm or flex his extended arm—even so, having vanished from the Brahma-world, appeared before me. Then, Bhikkhave, Brahmā Sahampati, arranging his upper robe over one shoulder, and having extended his clasped hands towards me, said this to me— ‘Bhante, may the Bhagavā teach the Dhamma, may the Fortunate One teach the Dhamma. There are beings with little dust in their eyes; they are perishing through not hearing the Dhamma. There will be those who understand the Dhamma.’ This Brahmā Sahampati said, Bhikkhave. Having said this, he then said further—

970\. ‘In Magadha there arose before, \
an impure Dhamma, conceived by those stained; \
open this gate to the deathless, \
let them hear the Dhamma, realised by the Stainless One. \
971\. Just as one standing on a mountain peak, \
might see the people all around; \
similarly, O wise one, O all-seeing one, \
having ascended the Dhamma-pavilion; \
look down upon the people immersed in sorrow, free from sorrow, \
look upon the people overcome by birth and ageing. \
972\. Arise, hero, victor in battle, \
caravan-leader, debtless one, wander in the world; \
may the Bhagavā teach the Dhamma, \
there will be those who understand it.’

973\. Then, Bhikkhave, knowing the request of Brahmā and out of compassion for beings, I surveyed the world with the Buddha-eye. As I surveyed the world with the Buddha-eye, I saw, Bhikkhave, beings with little dust in their eyes and with much dust in their eyes, with keen faculties and with dull faculties, with good dispositions and with bad dispositions, easy to teach and hard to teach, some who dwelt seeing fear in the blameworthy actions of the next world, and some who did not dwell seeing fear in the blameworthy actions of the next world. Just as in a pond of blue lotuses, or red lotuses, or white lotuses, some blue lotuses, red lotuses, or white lotuses, born in the water, grown in the water, remaining submerged in the water, nourished below the surface; some blue lotuses, red lotuses, or white lotuses, born in the water, grown in the water, standing level with the water; some blue lotuses, red lotuses, or white lotuses, born in the water, grown in the water, rising above the water, unadulterated by the water; in the same way, Bhikkhave, as I surveyed the world with the Buddha-eye, I saw beings with little dust in their eyes and with much dust in their eyes, with keen faculties and with dull faculties, with good dispositions and with bad dispositions, easy to teach and hard to teach, some who dwelt seeing fear in the blameworthy actions of the next world, and some who did not dwell seeing fear in the blameworthy actions of the next world. Then, Bhikkhave, I addressed Brahmā Sahampati with a verse—

974\. ‘Open are these doors to the deathless, \
let those with ears release their faith; \
with an apperception of trouble, I did not teach the Dhamma, \
the subtle Dhamma among humans, O Brahmā.’

975\. Then, Bhikkhave, Brahmā Sahampati, thinking, ‘I have indeed been given an opportunity by the Bhagavā to teach the Dhamma,’ bowed to me, circumambulated me keeping his right side towards me, and vanished right there.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
966\. Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘adhigato kho myāyaṃ dhammo gambhīro duddaso duranubodho santo paṇīto atakkāvacaro nipuṇo paṇḍitavedanīyo. Ālayarāmā kho panāyaṃ pajā ālayaratā ālayasammuditā. Ālayarāmāya kho pana pajāya ālayaratāya ālayasammuditāya duddasaṃ idaṃ ṭhānaṃ yadidaṃ—  idappaccayatā paṭiccasamuppādo. Idampi kho ṭhānaṃ duddasaṃ yadidaṃ—  sabbasaṅkhārasamatho sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo taṇhākkhayo virāgo nirodho nibbānaṃ. Ahañceva kho pana dhammaṃ deseyyaṃ, pare ca me na ājāneyyuṃ, so mamassa kilamatho, sā mamassa vihesā’ti. Apissu maṃ, bhikkhave, imā anacchariyā gāthāyo paṭibhaṃsu pubbe assutapubbā—

967\. ‘Kicchena me adhigataṃ, \
halaṃ dāni pakāsituṃ; \
Rāgadosaparetehi, \
nāyaṃ dhammo susambudho. \
968\. Paṭisotagāmiṃ nipuṇaṃ, \
gambhīraṃ duddasaṃ aṇuṃ; \
Rāgarattā na dakkhanti, \
tamokhandhena āvuṭā’ti.

969\. Itiha me, bhikkhave, paṭisañcikkhato appossukkatāya cittaṃ namati, no dhammadesanāya. Atha kho, bhikkhave, brahmuno sahampatissa mama cetasā cetoparivitakkamaññāya etadahosi—  ‘nassati vata bho loko, vinassati vata bho loko, yatra hi nāma tathāgatassa arahato sammāsambuddhassa appossukkatāya cittaṃ namati, no dhammadesanāyā’ti. Atha kho, bhikkhave, brahmā sahampati—  seyyathāpi nāma balavā puriso samiñjitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāreyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñjeyya; evameva—  brahmaloke antarahito mama purato pāturahosi. Atha kho, bhikkhave, brahmā sahampati ekaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yenāhaṃ tenañjaliṃ paṇāmetvā maṃ etadavoca—  ‘desetu, bhante, bhagavā dhammaṃ, desetu sugato dhammaṃ. Santi sattā apparajakkhajātikā, assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti. Bhavissanti dhammassa aññātāro’ti. Idamavoca, bhikkhave, brahmā sahampati. Idaṃ vatvā athāparaṃ etadavoca—

970\. ‘Pāturahosi magadhesu pubbe, \
Dhammo asuddho samalehi cintito; \
Apāpuretaṃ amatassa dvāraṃ, \
Suṇantu dhammaṃ vimalenānubuddhaṃ. \
971\. Sele yathā pabbatamuddhaniṭṭhito, \
Yathāpi passe janataṃ samantato; \
Tathūpamaṃ dhammamayaṃ sumedha, \
Pāsādamāruyha samantacakkhu; \
Sokāvatiṇṇaṃ janatamapetasoko, \
Avekkhassu jātijarābhibhūtaṃ. \
972\. Uṭṭhehi vīra vijitasaṅgāma, \
Satthavāha aṇaṇa vicara loke; \
Desassu bhagavā dhammaṃ, \
Aññātāro bhavissantī’ti.

973\. Atha kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, brahmuno ca ajjhesanaṃ viditvā sattesu ca kāruññataṃ paṭicca buddhacakkhunā lokaṃ volokesiṃ. Addasaṃ kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, buddhacakkhunā lokaṃ volokento satte apparajakkhe mahārajakkhe, tikkhindriye mudindriye, svākāre dvākāre, suviññāpaye duviññāpaye, appekacce paralokavajjabhayadassāvine viharante, appekacce na paralokavajjabhayadassāvine viharante. Seyyathāpi nāma uppaliniyaṃ vā paduminiyaṃ vā puṇḍarīkiniyaṃ vā appekaccāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udake jātāni udake saṃvaḍḍhāni udakānuggatāni antonimuggaposīni, appekaccāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udake jātāni udake saṃvaḍḍhāni udakānuggatāni samodakaṃ ṭhitāni, appekaccāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udake jātāni udake saṃvaḍḍhāni udakaṃ accuggamma ṭhitāni anupalittāni udakena; evameva kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, buddhacakkhunā lokaṃ volokento addasaṃ satte apparajakkhe mahārajakkhe, tikkhindriye mudindriye, svākāre dvākāre, suviññāpaye duviññāpaye, appekacce paralokavajjabhayadassāvine viharante, appekacce na paralokavajjabhayadassāvine viharante. Atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhave, brahmānaṃ sahampatiṃ gāthāya paccabhāsiṃ—

974\. ‘Apārutā tesaṃ amatassa dvārā, \
Ye sotavanto pamuñcantu saddhaṃ; \
Vihiṃsasaññī paguṇaṃ na bhāsiṃ, \
Dhammaṃ paṇītaṃ manujesu brahme’ti.

975\. Atha kho, bhikkhave, brahmā sahampati ‘katāvakāso khomhi bhagavatā dhammadesanāyā’ti maṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthevantaradhāyi.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
966\. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀕𑀢𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀬𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀕𑀫𑁆𑀪𑀻𑀭𑁄 𑀤𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀤𑀲𑁄 𑀤𑀼𑀭𑀦𑀼𑀩𑁄𑀥𑁄 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀧𑀡𑀻𑀢𑁄 𑀅𑀢𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀸𑀯𑀘𑀭𑁄 𑀦𑀺𑀧𑀼𑀡𑁄 𑀧𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀺𑀢𑀯𑁂𑀤𑀦𑀻𑀬𑁄. 𑀆𑀮𑀬𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀸 𑀔𑁄 𑀧𑀦𑀸𑀬𑀁 𑀧𑀚𑀸 𑀆𑀮𑀬𑀭𑀢𑀸 𑀆𑀮𑀬𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀼𑀤𑀺𑀢𑀸. 𑀆𑀮𑀬𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀸𑀬 𑀔𑁄 𑀧𑀦 𑀧𑀚𑀸𑀬 𑀆𑀮𑀬𑀭𑀢𑀸𑀬 𑀆𑀮𑀬𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀼𑀤𑀺𑀢𑀸𑀬 𑀤𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀤𑀲𑀁 𑀇𑀤𑀁 𑀞𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀬𑀤𑀺𑀤𑀁—  𑀇𑀤𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀬𑀢𑀸 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀲𑀫𑀼𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸𑀤𑁄. 𑀇𑀤𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀞𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀤𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀤𑀲𑀁 𑀬𑀤𑀺𑀤𑀁—  𑀲𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀔𑀸𑀭𑀲𑀫𑀣𑁄 𑀲𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀽𑀧𑀥𑀺𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀕𑁆𑀕𑁄 𑀢𑀡𑁆𑀳𑀸𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀬𑁄 𑀯𑀺𑀭𑀸𑀕𑁄 𑀦𑀺𑀭𑁄𑀥𑁄 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁. 𑀅𑀳𑀜𑁆𑀘𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑁄 𑀧𑀦 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀤𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁, 𑀧𑀭𑁂 𑀘 𑀫𑁂 𑀦 𑀆𑀚𑀸𑀦𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀼𑀁, 𑀲𑁄 𑀫𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀓𑀺𑀮𑀫𑀣𑁄, 𑀲𑀸 𑀫𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑁂𑀲𑀸’𑀢𑀺. 𑀅𑀧𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼 𑀫𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀇𑀫𑀸 𑀅𑀦𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀕𑀸𑀣𑀸𑀬𑁄 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀪𑀁𑀲𑀼 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼𑀢𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸—

967\. ‘𑀓𑀺𑀘𑁆𑀙𑁂𑀦 𑀫𑁂 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀕𑀢𑀁, \
𑀳𑀮𑀁 𑀤𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀧𑀓𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀢𑀼𑀁; \
𑀭𑀸𑀕𑀤𑁄𑀲𑀧𑀭𑁂𑀢𑁂𑀳𑀺, \
𑀦𑀸𑀬𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀲𑀼𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑀼𑀥𑁄. \
968\. 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀲𑁄𑀢𑀕𑀸𑀫𑀺𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀧𑀼𑀡𑀁, \
𑀕𑀫𑁆𑀪𑀻𑀭𑀁 𑀤𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀤𑀲𑀁 𑀅𑀡𑀼𑀁; \
𑀭𑀸𑀕𑀭𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸 𑀦 𑀤𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺, \
𑀢𑀫𑁄𑀔𑀦𑁆𑀥𑁂𑀦 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀝𑀸’𑀢𑀺.

969\. 𑀇𑀢𑀺𑀳 𑀫𑁂, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀲𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀢𑁄 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑁄𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀢𑀸𑀬 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦𑀫𑀢𑀺, 𑀦𑁄 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀦𑀸𑀬. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀼𑀦𑁄 𑀲𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀫 𑀘𑁂𑀢𑀲𑀸 𑀘𑁂𑀢𑁄𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀯𑀺𑀢𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀬 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀦𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀢𑀺 𑀯𑀢 𑀪𑁄 𑀮𑁄𑀓𑁄, 𑀯𑀺𑀦𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀢𑀺 𑀯𑀢 𑀪𑁄 𑀮𑁄𑀓𑁄, 𑀬𑀢𑁆𑀭 𑀳𑀺 𑀦𑀸𑀫 𑀢𑀣𑀸𑀕𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀅𑀭𑀳𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑁄𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀢𑀸𑀬 𑀘𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀦𑀫𑀢𑀺, 𑀦𑁄 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀦𑀸𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀲𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑀺—  𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀦𑀸𑀫 𑀩𑀮𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀚𑀺𑀢𑀁 𑀯𑀸 𑀩𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀧𑀲𑀸𑀭𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬, 𑀧𑀲𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀢𑀁 𑀯𑀸 𑀩𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀚𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬; 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯—  𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀮𑁄𑀓𑁂 𑀅𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀳𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀫𑀫 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀢𑁄 𑀧𑀸𑀢𑀼𑀭𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀲𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑀺 𑀏𑀓𑀁𑀲𑀁 𑀉𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀸𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀕𑀁 𑀓𑀭𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀬𑁂𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀜𑁆𑀚𑀮𑀺𑀁 𑀧𑀡𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀫𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘—  ‘𑀤𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀢𑀼, 𑀪𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀸 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, 𑀤𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀢𑀼 𑀲𑀼𑀕𑀢𑁄 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁. 𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺 𑀲𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀭𑀚𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀓𑀸, 𑀅𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀯𑀦𑀢𑀸 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀳𑀸𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺. 𑀪𑀯𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀅𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀢𑀸𑀭𑁄’𑀢𑀺. 𑀇𑀤𑀫𑀯𑁄𑀘, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀲𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑀺. 𑀇𑀤𑀁 𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀭𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘—

970\. ‘𑀧𑀸𑀢𑀼𑀭𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺 𑀫𑀕𑀥𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂, \
𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁄 𑀅𑀲𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑀮𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀘𑀺𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺𑀢𑁄; \
𑀅𑀧𑀸𑀧𑀼𑀭𑁂𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀫𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀤𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀭𑀁, \
𑀲𑀼𑀡𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀼 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀫𑀮𑁂𑀦𑀸𑀦𑀼𑀩𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀁. \
971\. 𑀲𑁂𑀮𑁂 𑀬𑀣𑀸 𑀧𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀢𑀫𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀦𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀺𑀢𑁄, \
𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀧𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂 𑀚𑀦𑀢𑀁 𑀲𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀢𑁄; \
𑀢𑀣𑀽𑀧𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀫𑀬𑀁 𑀲𑀼𑀫𑁂𑀥, \
𑀧𑀸𑀲𑀸𑀤𑀫𑀸𑀭𑀼𑀬𑁆𑀳 𑀲𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀘𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀼; \
𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀸𑀯𑀢𑀺𑀡𑁆𑀡𑀁 𑀚𑀦𑀢𑀫𑀧𑁂𑀢𑀲𑁄𑀓𑁄, \
𑀅𑀯𑁂𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀪𑀺𑀪𑀽𑀢𑀁. \
972\. 𑀉𑀝𑁆𑀞𑁂𑀳𑀺 𑀯𑀻𑀭 𑀯𑀺𑀚𑀺𑀢𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀕𑀸𑀫, \
𑀲𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀯𑀸𑀳 𑀅𑀡𑀡 𑀯𑀺𑀘𑀭 𑀮𑁄𑀓𑁂; \
𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀼 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀸 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁, \
𑀅𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀢𑀸𑀭𑁄 𑀪𑀯𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀻’𑀢𑀺.

973\. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀼𑀦𑁄 𑀘 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑁂𑀲𑀦𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀲𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀘 𑀓𑀸𑀭𑀼𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀢𑀁 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀘𑁆𑀘 𑀩𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀘𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀼𑀦𑀸 𑀮𑁄𑀓𑀁 𑀯𑁄𑀮𑁄𑀓𑁂𑀲𑀺𑀁. 𑀅𑀤𑁆𑀤𑀲𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀩𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀘𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀼𑀦𑀸 𑀮𑁄𑀓𑀁 𑀯𑁄𑀮𑁄𑀓𑁂𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀭𑀚𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂 𑀫𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀚𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂, 𑀢𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀦𑁆𑀤𑁆𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂 𑀫𑀼𑀤𑀺𑀦𑁆𑀤𑁆𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂, 𑀲𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀓𑀸𑀭𑁂 𑀤𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀓𑀸𑀭𑁂, 𑀲𑀼𑀯𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀧𑀬𑁂 𑀤𑀼𑀯𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀧𑀬𑁂, 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑁂𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀘𑁂 𑀧𑀭𑀮𑁄𑀓𑀯𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀪𑀬𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀯𑀺𑀦𑁂 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑁂𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀘𑁂 𑀦 𑀧𑀭𑀮𑁄𑀓𑀯𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀪𑀬𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀯𑀺𑀦𑁂 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂. 𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀧𑀺 𑀦𑀸𑀫 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀮𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀤𑀼𑀫𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀼𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀭𑀻𑀓𑀺𑀦𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑁂𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀮𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀤𑀼𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀼𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀭𑀻𑀓𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁂 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁂 𑀲𑀁𑀯𑀟𑁆𑀠𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑀸𑀦𑀼𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀅𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄𑀦𑀺𑀫𑀼𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀧𑁄𑀲𑀻𑀦𑀺, 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑁂𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀮𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀤𑀼𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀼𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀭𑀻𑀓𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁂 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁂 𑀲𑀁𑀯𑀟𑁆𑀠𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑀸𑀦𑀼𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑁄𑀤𑀓𑀁 𑀞𑀺𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀺, 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑁂𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀉𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀮𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀤𑀼𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀼𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀭𑀻𑀓𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁂 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁂 𑀲𑀁𑀯𑀟𑁆𑀠𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑀁 𑀅𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀼𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀫𑁆𑀫 𑀞𑀺𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀮𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁂𑀦; 𑀏𑀯𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀩𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀘𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀼𑀦𑀸 𑀮𑁄𑀓𑀁 𑀯𑁄𑀮𑁄𑀓𑁂𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀅𑀤𑁆𑀤𑀲𑀁 𑀲𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀭𑀚𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂 𑀫𑀳𑀸𑀭𑀚𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂, 𑀢𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀦𑁆𑀤𑁆𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂 𑀫𑀼𑀤𑀺𑀦𑁆𑀤𑁆𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂, 𑀲𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀓𑀸𑀭𑁂 𑀤𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀓𑀸𑀭𑁂, 𑀲𑀼𑀯𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀧𑀬𑁂 𑀤𑀼𑀯𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀧𑀬𑁂, 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑁂𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀘𑁂 𑀧𑀭𑀮𑁄𑀓𑀯𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀪𑀬𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀯𑀺𑀦𑁂 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑁂𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀘𑁂 𑀦 𑀧𑀭𑀮𑁄𑀓𑀯𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀪𑀬𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀸𑀯𑀺𑀦𑁂 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀲𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑀺𑀁 𑀕𑀸𑀣𑀸𑀬 𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀪𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁—

974\. ‘𑀅𑀧𑀸𑀭𑀼𑀢𑀸 𑀢𑁂𑀲𑀁 𑀅𑀫𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀤𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀭𑀸, \
𑀬𑁂 𑀲𑁄𑀢𑀯𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀧𑀫𑀼𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀼 𑀲𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀁; \
𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀺𑀁𑀲𑀲𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀻 𑀧𑀕𑀼𑀡𑀁 𑀦 𑀪𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁, \
𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀧𑀡𑀻𑀢𑀁 𑀫𑀦𑀼𑀚𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑁂’𑀢𑀺.

975\. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀲𑀳𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀢𑀺 ‘𑀓𑀢𑀸𑀯𑀓𑀸𑀲𑁄 𑀔𑁄𑀫𑁆𑀳𑀺 𑀪𑀕𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀤𑁂𑀲𑀦𑀸𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺 𑀫𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀯𑀸𑀤𑁂𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀤𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀡𑀁 𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀢𑀢𑁆𑀣𑁂𑀯𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀥𑀸𑀬𑀺.

966.1 Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘adhigato kho myāyaṃ dhammo gambhīro duddaso duranubodho santo paṇīto atakkāvacaro nipuṇo paṇḍitavedanīyo.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀④(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(adhigata) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀①(dhamma) 🚹⨀①(gambhīra) 🚹⨀①🆎(dur+dasa) 🚹⨀①🆎(dur+anubodha) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(santa) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(paṇīta) 🚹⨀①🆎(na+takka+avacara) 🚹⨀①(nipuṇa) 🚹⨀①🆎(paṇḍita+vedanīya)  
to that | to me | Bhikkhave | this | occurred | realised | indeed | by me | this | Dhamma | profound | hard to see | hard to comprehend | peaceful | refined | beyond the scope of reasoning | subtle | to be experienced by the wise  
So, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me— ‘This Dhamma I have realised is indeed profound, hard to see, hard to comprehend, peaceful, refined, beyond the scope of reasoning, subtle, to be experienced by the wise.

966.2 Ālayarāmā kho panāyaṃ pajā ālayaratā ālayasammuditā.  
🚺⨀①🆎(ālaya+rāma) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(pana) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀①(ima) 🚺⨀①(pajā) 🚺⨀①🆎(ālaya+rata) 🚺⨀①🆎(ālaya+sammudita)  
delighting in attachment | indeed | but | this | generation | pleased by attachment | rejoicing in attachment  
But this generation delights in attachment, is pleased by attachment, rejoices in attachment.

966.3 Ālayarāmāya kho pana pajāya ālayaratāya ālayasammuditāya duddasaṃ idaṃ ṭhānaṃ yadidaṃ—  idappaccayatā paṭiccasamuppādo.  
🚺⨀④🆎(ālaya+rāma) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(pana) 🚺⨀④(pajā) 🚺⨀④🆎(ālaya+rata) 🚺⨀④🆎(ālaya+sammudita) 🚻⨀①🆎(dur+dasa) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ima) 🚻⨀①(ṭhāna) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ya) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ima) 🚺⨀①🆎(idaṃ+paccayatā) 🚹⨀①🆎(paṭicca+samuppāda)  
delighting in attachment | indeed | but | for a generation | pleased by attachment | rejoicing in attachment | hard to see | this | state | which | this | specific causality | dependent origination  
For a generation delighting in attachment, pleased by attachment, rejoicing in attachment, this state is hard to see, namely: specific causality, `paṭiccasamuppāda` (dependent origination).

966.4 Idampi kho ṭhānaṃ duddasaṃ yadidaṃ—  sabbasaṅkhārasamatho sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo taṇhākkhayo virāgo nirodho nibbānaṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ima) ⏏️(api) ⏏️(kho) 🚻⨀①(ṭhāna) 🚻⨀①🆎(dur+dasa) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ya) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀①🆎(sabba+saṅkhāra+samatha) 🚹⨀①🆎(sabba+upadhi+paṭinissagga) 🚹⨀①🆎(taṇhā+khaya) 🚹⨀①(virāga) 🚹⨀①(nirodha) 🚻⨀①(nibbāna)  
this | too | indeed | state | hard to see | which | this | calming of all mental constructions | relinquishing of all acquisitions | destruction of craving | detachment | cessation | extinguishment  
This state, too, is indeed hard to see, namely: the calming of all `saṅkhārā` (mental constructions), the relinquishing of all acquisitions, the destruction of craving, dispassion, cessation, extinguishment.

966.5 Ahañceva kho pana dhammaṃ deseyyaṃ, pare ca me na ājāneyyuṃ, so mamassa kilamatho, sā mamassa vihesā’ti.  
*️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) ⏏️(ce) ⏏️(eva) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(pana) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🔵⨀👆⏯(deseti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(para) ⏏️(ca) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) ⏏️(na) 🟢⨂🤟⏯(ājānāti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(atthi) 🚹⨀①(kilamatha) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀①(ta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(atthi) 🚺⨀①(vihesā) ⏏️(iti)  
I | if | just | indeed | but | the Dhamma | were to teach | others | and | me | not | would understand | that | for me | would be | weariness | that | for me | would be | trouble | (end quote)  
But if I were to teach the Dhamma and others did not understand me, that would be weariness for me, that would be trouble for me.’

966.6 Apissu maṃ, bhikkhave, imā anacchariyā gāthāyo paṭibhaṃsu pubbe assutapubbā—  
⏏️(api) ⏏️(ssu) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚺⨂①(ima) 🚺⨂①🆎(na+acchariya) 🚺⨂①(gāthā) 🟢⨂🤟⏮(paṭibhāti) ⏏️(pubbe) 🚺⨂①🆎(na+suta+pubba)  
and | then | to me | Bhikkhave | these | wonderful | verses | occurred | before | never heard before  
And then, Bhikkhave, these unheard-before, wonderful verses occurred to me—

967.1 ‘Kicchena me adhigataṃ,  
🚻⨀③(kiccha) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚻⨀①🔽(ta)(adhigata)  
with difficulty | by me | realised

⏏️(hi) ⏏️(alaṃ) ⏏️(dāni) 🔽(tuṃ)(pakāseti)  
indeed | enough | now | to proclaim  
967.3 Rāgadosaparetehi,  
🚹⨂③🆎(rāga+dosa+pareta)  
by those consumed by lust and hatred  
967.4 nāyaṃ dhammo susambudho.  
⏏️(na) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ima) 🚹⨀①(dhamma) 🚹⨀①🆎(su+sambudha)  
not | this | Dhamma | easily understood  

‘With difficulty have I realised it,  
enough now with proclaiming it;  
for those consumed by lust and hatred,  
this Dhamma is not easily understood.

968.1 Paṭisotagāmiṃ nipuṇaṃ,  
🚹⨀②🆎(paṭisota+gāmī) 🚹⨀②(nipuṇa)  
going against the stream | subtle  
968.2 gambhīraṃ duddasaṃ aṇuṃ;

profound | hard to see | minute  
968.3 Rāgarattā na dakkhanti,  
🚹⨂①🆎(rāga+ratta) ⏏️(na) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(dakkhati)  
those inflamed by lust | not | see  
968.4 tamokhandhena āvuṭā’ti.  
🚹⨀③🆎(tama+khandha) 🚹⨂①🔽(ta)(āvuṭa) ⏏️(iti)

Going against the stream, subtle,  
profound, hard to see, minute;  
those inflamed by lust will not see it,  
shrouded as they are in darkness.’

969.1 Itiha me, bhikkhave, paṭisañcikkhato appossukkatāya cittaṃ namati, no dhammadesanāya.  
⏏️(iti) ⏏️(ha) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀⑥🔽(anta)(paṭisañcikkhanta) 🚺⨀④(appossukkatā) 🚻⨀①(citta) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(namati) ⏏️(no) 🚺⨀④🆎(dhamma+desanā)  
thus | indeed | my | Bhikkhave | reflecting | to inaction | mind | inclines | not | to teaching the Dhamma  
So, Bhikkhave, as I reflected thus, my mind inclined to inaction, not to teaching the Dhamma.

969.2 Atha kho, bhikkhave, brahmuno sahampatissa mama cetasā cetoparivitakkamaññāya etadahosi—  ‘nassati vata bho loko, vinassati vata bho loko, yatra hi nāma tathāgatassa arahato sammāsambuddhassa appossukkatāya cittaṃ namati, no dhammadesanāyā’ti.  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀④(brahma) 🚹⨀④(sahampati) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀③(cetas) 🚹⨀②🆎(cetas+parivitakka) 🔽(ya)(ājānāti) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(hoti) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(nassati) ⏏️(vata) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀①(loka) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(vinassati) ⏏️(vata) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀①(loka) ⏏️(yatra) ⏏️(hi) ⏏️(nāma) 🚹⨀⑥🆎(tathā+gata) 🚹⨀⑥(arahant) 🚹⨀⑥🆎(sammā+sambuddha) 🚺⨀④(appossukkatā) 🚻⨀①(citta) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(namati) ⏏️(no) 🚺⨀④🆎(dhamma+desanā) ⏏️(iti)  
then | indeed | Bhikkhave | to Brahmā | to Sahampati | my | with mind | thought process | having known | this | occurred | is perishing | alas | Bho | the world | is being destroyed | alas | Bho | the world | inasmuch as | indeed | truly | of the Tathāgata | of the liberated one | of the perfectly awakened one | to inaction | mind | inclines | not | to teaching the Dhamma | (end quote)  
Then, Bhikkhave, Brahmā Sahampati, knowing my thought process with his own mind, had this thought— ‘Alas, the world is perishing, alas, the world is being destroyed, inasmuch as the mind of the Tathāgata, the `arahant` (liberated one), the `sammāsambuddha` (perfectly awakened one), inclines to inaction, not to teaching the Dhamma!’

969.3 Atha kho, bhikkhave, brahmā sahampati—  seyyathāpi nāma balavā puriso samiñjitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāreyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñjeyya;  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀①(brahma) 🚹⨀①(sahampati) ⏏️(seyyathāpi) ⏏️(nāma) 🚹⨀①🔽(vantu)(balavant) 🚹⨀①(purisa) 🚺⨀②🔽(ta)(samiñjita) ⏏️(vā) 🚺⨀②(bāhā) 🟠⨀🤟⏯(pasāreti) 🚺⨀②🔽(ta)(pasārita) ⏏️(vā) 🚺⨀②(bāhā) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(samiñjati)  
then | indeed | Bhikkhave | Brahmā | Sahampati | just as | indeed | strong | man | flexed | or | arm | might extend | extended | or | arm | might flex  
Then, Bhikkhave, Brahmā Sahampati—just as a strong man might extend his flexed arm or flex his extended arm—

969.4 evameva—  brahmaloke antarahito mama purato pāturahosi.  
⏏️(evaṃ) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀⑦🆎(brahma+loka) 🚹⨀①🆎(antara+hita) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) ⏏️(purato) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(pātubhavati)  
in the same way | just | from the Brahma-world | vanished | of me | in front | appeared  
even so, having vanished from the Brahma-world, appeared before me.

969.5 Atha kho, bhikkhave, brahmā sahampati ekaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yenāhaṃ tenañjaliṃ paṇāmetvā maṃ etadavoca—  ‘desetu, bhante, bhagavā dhammaṃ, desetu sugato dhammaṃ.  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀①(brahma) 🚹⨀①(sahampati) 🚹⨀②🆎(eka+aṃsa) 🚹⨀②🆎(uttara+āsaṅga) 🔽(tvā)(karoti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🚹⨀②(añjali) 🔽(tvā)(paṇāmeti) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(vadati) 🟠⨀🤟⏹(deseti) 🚹⨀⓪(bhavant) 🚹⨀①(bhagavant) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🟠⨀🤟⏹(deseti) 🚹⨀①🆎(su+gata) 🚹⨀②(dhamma)  
then | indeed | Bhikkhave | Brahmā | Sahampati | over one shoulder | upper robe | having arranged | by where | I | by there | clasped hands | having extended | me | this | said | may teach | Bhante | the Bhagavā | the Dhamma | may teach | the Fortunate One | the Dhamma  
Then, Bhikkhave, Brahmā Sahampati, arranging his upper robe over one shoulder, and having extended his clasped hands towards me, said this to me— ‘Bhante, may the Bhagavā teach the Dhamma, may the Fortunate One teach the Dhamma.

969.6 Santi sattā apparajakkhajātikā, assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti.  
🟢⨂🤟▶️(atthi) 🚹⨂①(satta) 🚹⨂①🆎(appa+rajas+akkha+jātika) 🚺⨀③(assavanatā) 🚹⨀⑥(dhamma) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(parihāyati)  
there are | beings | with little dust in their eyes | through not hearing | of the Dhamma | are wasting away  
There are beings with little dust in their eyes; they are perishing through not hearing the Dhamma.

969.7 Bhavissanti dhammassa aññātāro’ti.  
🟢⨂🤟⏭(bhavati) 🚹⨀⑥(dhamma) 🚹⨂①(aññātar) ⏏️(iti)  
there will be | of the Dhamma | those who understand | (end quote)  
There will be those who understand the Dhamma.’

969.8 Idamavoca, bhikkhave, brahmā sahampati.  
*️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(ima) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(vadati) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀①(brahma) 🚹⨀①(sahampati)  
this | said | Bhikkhave | Brahmā | Sahampati  
This Brahmā Sahampati said, Bhikkhave.

969.9 Idaṃ vatvā athāparaṃ etadavoca—  
*️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(ima) 🔽(tvā)(vadati) ⏏️(atha) ⏏️(aparaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(vadati)  
this | having said | then | further | this | said  
Having said this, he then said further—

970.1 ‘Pāturahosi magadhesu pubbe,  
🟢⨀🤟⏮(pātubhavati) 🚹⨂⑦(magadha) ⏏️(pubbe)  
arose | in Magadha | before  
970.2 Dhammo asuddho samalehi cintito;  
🚹⨀①(dhamma) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)🆎(na+suddha) 🚹⨂③(samala) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(cintita)  
Dhamma | impure | by those stained | conceived  
970.3 Apāpuretaṃ amatassa dvāraṃ,  
🟢⨀🤘⏹(apāpurati) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🚻⨀⑥🔽(ta)🆎(na+mata) 🚻⨀②(dvāra)  
open | this | of the deathless | gate  
970.4 Suṇantu dhammaṃ vimalenānubuddhaṃ.  
🟢⨂🤟⏹(suṇāti) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🚹⨀③(vimala) 🚹⨀②🔽(ta)(anubuddha)  
let them hear | the Dhamma | by the Stainless One | realised  

‘In Magadha there arose before,  
an impure Dhamma, conceived by those stained;  
open this gate to the deathless,  
let them hear the Dhamma, realised by the Stainless One.

971.1 Sele yathā pabbatamuddhaniṭṭhito,  
🚹⨀⑦(sela) ⏏️(yathā) 🚹⨀⑦🆎(pabbata+muddha) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(ṭhita)  
on a rock | as | on a mountain peak | standing  
971.2 Yathāpi passe janataṃ samantato;  
⏏️(yathā) ⏏️(api) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(passati) 🚺⨀②(janatā) ⏏️(samantato)  
just as | also | might see | the people | all around  
971.3 Tathūpamaṃ dhammamayaṃ sumedha,  
🚹⨀②🆎(tathā+upama) 🚹⨀②🆎(dhamma+maya) 🚹⨀⓪🆎(su+medha)  
similar to that | made of the Dhamma | O wise one  
971.4 Pāsādamāruyha samantacakkhu;  
🚹⨀②(pāsāda) 🔽(ya)(āruhati) 🚹⨀⓪🆎(samanta+cakkhu)  
the pavilion | having ascended | O all-seeing one  
971.5 Sokāvatiṇṇaṃ janatamapetasoko,  
🚺⨀②🆎(soka+avatiṇṇa) 🚺⨀②(janatā) 🚹⨀①🆎(apeta+soka)  
immersed in sorrow | the people | free from sorrow  
971.6 Avekkhassu jātijarābhibhūtaṃ.  
🔵⨀🤘⏹(avekkhati) 🚺⨀②🆎(jāti+jarā+abhibhūta)

Just as one standing on a mountain peak,  
might see the people all around;  
similarly, O wise one, O all-seeing one,  
having ascended the Dhamma-pavilion;  
look down upon the people immersed in sorrow, free from sorrow,  
look upon the people overcome by birth and ageing.

972.1 Uṭṭhehi vīra vijitasaṅgāma,  
🟠⨀🤘⏹(uṭṭheti) 🚹⨀⓪(vīra) 🚹⨀⓪🆎(vijita+saṅgāma)  
arise | hero | victor in battle  
972.2 Satthavāha aṇaṇa vicara loke;  
🚹⨀⓪🆎(sattha+vāha) 🚹⨀⓪🆎(na+iṇa) 🟢⨀🤘⏹(vicarati) 🚹⨀⑦(loka)  
caravan-leader | debtless one | wander | in the world  
972.3 Desassu bhagavā dhammaṃ,  
🔵⨀🤘⏹(deseti) 🚹⨀⓪(bhagavant) 🚹⨀②(dhamma)  
teach | Bhagavā | the Dhamma  
972.4 Aññātāro bhavissantī’ti.  
🚹⨂①(aññātar) 🟢⨂🤟⏭(bhavati) ⏏️(iti)  
those who understand | there will be | (end quote)  

Arise, hero, victor in battle,  
caravan-leader, debtless one, wander in the world;  
may the Bhagavā teach the Dhamma,  
there will be those who understand it.’

973.1 Atha kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, brahmuno ca ajjhesanaṃ viditvā sattesu ca kāruññataṃ paṭicca buddhacakkhunā lokaṃ volokesiṃ.  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀⑥(brahma) ⏏️(ca) 🚺⨀②(ajjhesanā) 🔽(tvā)(vidati) 🚹⨂⑦(satta) ⏏️(ca) 🚺⨀②(kāruññatā) 🔽(ya)(pacceti) 🚻⨀③🆎(buddha+cakkhu) 🚹⨀②(loka) 🟢⨀👆⏮(voloketi)  
then | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | of Brahmā | and | the request | having known | for beings | and | compassion | on account of | with the Buddha-eye | the world | I surveyed  
Then, Bhikkhave, knowing the request of Brahmā and out of compassion for beings, I surveyed the world with the Buddha-eye.

973.2 Addasaṃ kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, buddhacakkhunā lokaṃ volokento satte apparajakkhe mahārajakkhe, tikkhindriye mudindriye, svākāre dvākāre, suviññāpaye duviññāpaye, appekacce paralokavajjabhayadassāvine viharante, appekacce na paralokavajjabhayadassāvine viharante.  
🟢⨀👆⏮(passati) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚻⨀③🆎(buddha+cakkhu) 🚹⨀②(loka) 🚹⨀①🔽(anta)(volokenta) 🚹⨂②(satta) 🚹⨂②🆎(appa+rajas+akkha) 🚹⨂②🆎(mahā+rajas+akkha) 🚹⨂②🆎(tikkha+indriya) 🚹⨂②🆎(mudu+indriya) 🚹⨂②🆎(su+ākāra) 🚹⨂②🆎(dur+ākāra) 🚹⨂②🆎(su+viññāpaya) 🚹⨂②🆎(dur+viññāpaya) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂②🆎(api+ekacca) 🚹⨂②🆎(para+loka+vajja+bhaya+dassāvī) 🚹⨂②🔽(anta)(viharanta) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂②🆎(api+ekacca) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨂②🆎(para+loka+vajja+bhaya+dassāvī) 🚹⨂②🔽(anta)(viharanta)  
I saw | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | with the Buddha-eye | the world | surveying | beings | with little dust in their eyes | with much dust in their eyes | with keen faculties | with dull faculties | with good dispositions | with bad dispositions | easy to teach | hard to teach | some | seeing fear in the blameworthy actions of the next world | dwelling | some | not | seeing fear in the blameworthy actions of the next world | dwelling  
As I surveyed the world with the Buddha-eye, I saw, Bhikkhave, beings with little dust in their eyes and with much dust in their eyes, with keen faculties and with dull faculties, with good dispositions and with bad dispositions, easy to teach and hard to teach, some who dwelt seeing fear in the blameworthy actions of the next world, and some who did not dwell seeing fear in the blameworthy actions of the next world.

973.3 Seyyathāpi nāma uppaliniyaṃ vā paduminiyaṃ vā puṇḍarīkiniyaṃ vā appekaccāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udake jātāni udake saṃvaḍḍhāni udakānuggatāni antonimuggaposīni, appekaccāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udake jātāni udake saṃvaḍḍhāni udakānuggatāni samodakaṃ ṭhitāni, appekaccāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udake jātāni udake saṃvaḍḍhāni udakaṃ accuggamma ṭhitāni anupalittāni udakena;  
⏏️(seyyathāpi) ⏏️(nāma) 🚺⨀⑦(uppalinī) ⏏️(vā) 🚺⨀⑦(paduminī) ⏏️(vā) 🚺⨀⑦(puṇḍarīkinī) ⏏️(vā) *️⃣🤟🚻⨂①🆎(api+ekacca) 🚻⨂①(uppala) ⏏️(vā) 🚻⨂①(paduma) ⏏️(vā) 🚻⨂①(puṇḍarīka) ⏏️(vā) 🚻⨀⑦(udaka) 🚻⨂①🔽(ta)(jāta) 🚻⨀⑦(udaka) 🚻⨂①🔽(ta)(saṃvaḍḍha) 🚻⨂①🔽(ta)🆎(udaka+na+uggata) 🚻⨂①🆎(anto+nimugga+posī) *️⃣🤟🚻⨂①🆎(api+ekacca) 🚻⨂①(uppala) ⏏️(vā) 🚻⨂①(paduma) ⏏️(vā) 🚻⨂①(puṇḍarīka) ⏏️(vā) 🚻⨀⑦(udaka) 🚻⨂①🔽(ta)(jāta) 🚻⨀⑦(udaka) 🚻⨂①🔽(ta)(saṃvaḍḍha) 🚻⨂①🔽(ta)🆎(udaka+na+uggata) ⏏️🆎(sama+udaka) 🚻⨂①🔽(ta)(ṭhita) *️⃣🤟🚻⨂①🆎(api+ekacca) 🚻⨂①(uppala) ⏏️(vā) 🚻⨂①(paduma) ⏏️(vā) 🚻⨂①(puṇḍarīka) ⏏️(vā) 🚻⨀⑦(udaka) 🚻⨂①🔽(ta)(jāta) 🚻⨀⑦(udaka) 🚻⨂①🔽(ta)(saṃvaḍḍha) 🚻⨀②(udaka) 🔽(ya)(accuggacchati) 🚻⨂①🔽(ta)(ṭhita) 🚻⨂①🔽(ta)🆎(na+upalitta) 🚻⨀③(udaka)  
just as | indeed | in a pond of blue lotuses | or | in a pond of red lotuses | or | in a pond of white lotuses | or | some | blue lotuses | or | red lotuses | or | white lotuses | or | in the water | born | in the water | grown | not risen out of the water | nourished below the surface | some | blue lotuses | or | red lotuses | or | white lotuses | or | in the water | born | in the water | grown | not risen out of the water | level with the water | standing | some | blue lotuses | or | red lotuses | or | white lotuses | or | in the water | born | in the water | grown | the water | rising above | standing | unadulterated | by the water  
Just as in a pond of blue lotuses, or red lotuses, or white lotuses, some blue lotuses, red lotuses, or white lotuses, born in the water, grown in the water, remaining submerged in the water, nourished below the surface; some blue lotuses, red lotuses, or white lotuses, born in the water, grown in the water, standing level with the water; some blue lotuses, red lotuses, or white lotuses, born in the water, grown in the water, rising above the water, unadulterated by the water;

973.4 evameva kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, buddhacakkhunā lokaṃ volokento addasaṃ satte apparajakkhe mahārajakkhe, tikkhindriye mudindriye, svākāre dvākāre, suviññāpaye duviññāpaye, appekacce paralokavajjabhayadassāvine viharante, appekacce na paralokavajjabhayadassāvine viharante.  
⏏️(evaṃ) ⏏️(eva) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚻⨀③🆎(buddha+cakkhu) 🚹⨀②(loka) 🚹⨀①🔽(anta)(volokenta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(passati) 🚹⨂②(satta) 🚹⨂②🆎(appa+rajas+akkha) 🚹⨂②🆎(mahā+rajas+akkha) 🚹⨂②🆎(tikkha+indriya) 🚹⨂②🆎(mudu+indriya) 🚹⨂②🆎(su+ākāra) 🚹⨂②🆎(dur+ākāra) 🚹⨂②🆎(su+viññāpaya) 🚹⨂②🆎(dur+viññāpaya) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂②🆎(api+ekacca) 🚹⨂②🆎(para+loka+vajja+bhaya+dassāvī) 🚹⨂②🔽(anta)(viharanta) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂②🆎(api+ekacca) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨂②🆎(para+loka+vajja+bhaya+dassāvī) 🚹⨂②🔽(anta)(viharanta)  
in the same way | just | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | with the Buddha-eye | the world | surveying | I saw | beings | with little dust in their eyes | with much dust in their eyes | with keen faculties | with dull faculties | with good dispositions | with bad dispositions | easy to teach | hard to teach | some | seeing fear in the blameworthy actions of the next world | dwelling | some | not | seeing fear in the blameworthy actions of the next world | dwelling  
in the same way, Bhikkhave, as I surveyed the world with the Buddha-eye, I saw beings with little dust in their eyes and with much dust in their eyes, with keen faculties and with dull faculties, with good dispositions and with bad dispositions, easy to teach and hard to teach, some who dwelt seeing fear in the blameworthy actions of the next world, and some who did not dwell seeing fear in the blameworthy actions of the next world.

973.5 Atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhave, brahmānaṃ sahampatiṃ gāthāya paccabhāsiṃ—  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀②(brahma) 🚹⨀②(sahampati) 🚺⨀③(gāthā) 🟢⨀👆⏮(paṭibhāsati)  
then | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | Brahmā | Sahampati | with a verse | addressed  
Then, Bhikkhave, I addressed Brahmā Sahampati with a verse—

974.1 ‘Apārutā tesaṃ amatassa dvārā,  
🚻⨂①🔽(ta)(apāruta) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂④(ta) 🚻⨀⑥🔽(ta)🆎(na+mata) 🚻⨂①(dvāra)  
open | for them | of the deathless | the doors  
974.2 Ye sotavanto pamuñcantu saddhaṃ;  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(ya) 🚹⨂①🔽(vantu)(sotavant) 🟢⨂🤟⏹(pamuñcati) 🚺⨀②(saddhā)  
those who | with ears | let them release | faith  
974.3 Vihiṃsasaññī paguṇaṃ na bhāsiṃ,  
🚹⨀①🆎(vihiṃsā+saññī) 🚹⨀②(paguṇa) ⏏️(na) 🟢⨀👆⏮(bhāsati)  
with an apperception of trouble | well-practised | not | I spoke  
974.4 Dhammaṃ paṇītaṃ manujesu brahme’ti.  
🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🚹⨀②(paṇīta) 🚹⨂⑦🆎(manu+ja) 🚹⨀⓪(brahma) ⏏️(iti)  
the Dhamma | refined | among humans | O Brahmā | (end quote)  

‘Open are these doors to the deathless,  
let those with ears release their faith;  
with an apperception of trouble, I did not teach the Dhamma,  
the subtle Dhamma among humans, O Brahmā.’

975.1 Atha kho, bhikkhave, brahmā sahampati ‘katāvakāso khomhi bhagavatā dhammadesanāyā’ti maṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthevantaradhāyi.  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀①(brahma) 🚹⨀①(sahampati) 🚹⨀①🆎(kata+avakāsa) ⏏️(kho) 🟢⨀👆▶️(atthi) 🚹⨀③(bhagavant) 🚺⨀④🆎(dhamma+desanā) ⏏️(iti) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) 🔽(tvā)(abhivādeti) 🚻⨀②(padakkhiṇa) 🔽(tvā)(karoti) ⏏️(tattha) ⏏️(eva) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(antaradhāyati)  
then | indeed | Bhikkhave | Brahmā | Sahampati | given an opportunity | indeed | I am | by the Bhagavā | for teaching the Dhamma | (end quote) | me | having bowed to | keeping the right side towards | having done | there | just | vanished  
Then, Bhikkhave, Brahmā Sahampati, thinking, ‘I have indeed been given an opportunity by the Bhagavā to teach the Dhamma,’ bowed to me, circumambulated me keeping his right side towards me, and vanished right there.
</TabItem>
</Tabs>

## Deciding who to teach

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
976\. Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me— ‘To whom should I teach the Dhamma first? Who will quickly understand this Dhamma?’ Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me— ‘This Āḷāra Kālāma is indeed wise, skilled, intelligent, and has little dust in his eyes for a long time. What if I were to teach the Dhamma first to Āḷāra Kālāma? He will quickly understand this Dhamma.’ Then, Bhikkhave, a deity approached me and said this: ‘Bhante, Āḷāra Kālāma passed away seven days ago.’ And knowledge and insight arose in me: ‘Āḷāra Kālāma passed away seven days ago.’ Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me— ‘Great indeed was the loss of Āḷāra Kālāma. For if he were to hear this Dhamma, he would quickly understand it.’

977\. Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me— ‘To whom should I teach the Dhamma first? Who will quickly understand this Dhamma?’ Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me— ‘This Uddaka son of Rāma is indeed wise, skilled, intelligent, and has little dust in his eyes for a long time. What if I were to teach the Dhamma first to Uddaka son of Rāma? He will quickly understand this Dhamma.’ Then, Bhikkhave, a deity approached me and said this: ‘Bhante, Uddaka son of Rāma passed away last night.’ And knowledge and insight arose in me: ‘Uddaka son of Rāma passed away last night.’ Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me— ‘Great indeed was the loss of Uddaka son of Rāma. For if he were to hear this Dhamma, he would quickly understand it.’

978\. Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me— ‘To whom should I teach the Dhamma first? Who will quickly understand this Dhamma?’ Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me— ‘Very helpful to me indeed were the group of five bhikkhū, who attended to me when I was striving strenuously. What if I were to teach the Dhamma first to the group of five bhikkhū?’ Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me— ‘Where now are the group of five bhikkhū dwelling?’ I saw, Bhikkhave, with my divine eye, purified and surpassing that of humans, the group of five bhikkhū dwelling in Bārāṇasī, at Isipatana in the Deer Park. Then, Bhikkhave, having stayed at Uruvelā as long as I wished, I set out on a journey to Bārāṇasī.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
976\. Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘kassa nu kho ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ deseyyaṃ; ko imaṃ dhammaṃ khippameva ājānissatī’ti? Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘ayaṃ kho āḷāro kālāmo paṇḍito viyatto medhāvī dīgharattaṃ apparajakkhajātiko. Yannūnāhaṃ āḷārassa kālāmassa paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ deseyyaṃ. So imaṃ dhammaṃ khippameva ājānissatī’ti. Atha kho maṃ, bhikkhave, devatā upasaṅkamitvā etadavoca—  ‘sattāhakālaṅkato, bhante, āḷāro kālāmo’ti. Ñāṇañca pana me dassanaṃ udapādi—  ‘sattāhakālaṅkato āḷāro kālāmo’ti. Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘mahājāniyo kho āḷāro kālāmo. Sace hi so imaṃ dhammaṃ suṇeyya, khippameva ājāneyyā’ti.

977\. Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘kassa nu kho ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ deseyyaṃ; ko imaṃ dhammaṃ khippameva ājānissatī’ti? Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘ayaṃ kho udako rāmaputto paṇḍito viyatto medhāvī dīgharattaṃ apparajakkhajātiko. Yannūnāhaṃ udakassa rāmaputtassa paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ deseyyaṃ. So imaṃ dhammaṃ khippameva ājānissatī’ti. Atha kho maṃ, bhikkhave, devatā upasaṅkamitvā etadavoca—  ‘abhidosakālaṅkato, bhante, udako rāmaputto’ti. Ñāṇañca pana me dassanaṃ udapādi—  ‘abhidosakālaṅkato udako rāmaputto’ti. Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘mahājāniyo kho udako rāmaputto. Sace hi so imaṃ dhammaṃ suṇeyya, khippameva ājāneyyā’ti.

978\. Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘kassa nu kho ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ deseyyaṃ; ko imaṃ dhammaṃ khippameva ājānissatī’ti? Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘bahukārā kho me pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū, ye maṃ padhānapahitattaṃ upaṭṭhahiṃsu. Yannūnāhaṃ pañcavaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ deseyyan’ti. Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘kahaṃ nu kho etarahi pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū viharantī’ti? Addasaṃ kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena pañcavaggiye bhikkhū bārāṇasiyaṃ viharante isipatane migadāye. Atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhave, uruvelāyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yena bārāṇasī tena cārikaṃ pakkamiṃ.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
976\. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀦𑀼 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁 𑀧𑀞𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀤𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁; 𑀓𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀔𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀆𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀢𑀻’𑀢𑀺? 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑁄 𑀧𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀯𑀺𑀬𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀫𑁂𑀥𑀸𑀯𑀻 𑀤𑀻𑀖𑀭𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀭𑀚𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀓𑁄. 𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀽𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀧𑀞𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀤𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁. 𑀲𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀔𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀆𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀢𑀻’𑀢𑀺. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀤𑁂𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘—  ‘𑀲𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀳𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀢𑁄, 𑀪𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑁄’𑀢𑀺. 𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀧𑀦 𑀫𑁂 𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑀁 𑀉𑀤𑀧𑀸𑀤𑀺—  ‘𑀲𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸𑀳𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀢𑁄 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑁄’𑀢𑀺. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀫𑀳𑀸𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀺𑀬𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀆𑀍𑀆𑀭𑁄 𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀸𑀫𑁄. 𑀲𑀘𑁂 𑀳𑀺 𑀲𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀼𑀡𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬, 𑀔𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀆𑀚𑀸𑀦𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺.

977\. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀦𑀼 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁 𑀧𑀞𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀤𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁; 𑀓𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀔𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀆𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀢𑀻’𑀢𑀺? 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀧𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀺𑀢𑁄 𑀯𑀺𑀬𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀫𑁂𑀥𑀸𑀯𑀻 𑀤𑀻𑀖𑀭𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀭𑀚𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀓𑁄. 𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀽𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀧𑀞𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀤𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁. 𑀲𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀔𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀆𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀢𑀻’𑀢𑀺. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀤𑁂𑀯𑀢𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘—  ‘𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀤𑁄𑀲𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀢𑁄, 𑀪𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄’𑀢𑀺. 𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀧𑀦 𑀫𑁂 𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑀁 𑀉𑀤𑀧𑀸𑀤𑀺—  ‘𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀤𑁄𑀲𑀓𑀸𑀮𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀢𑁄 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄’𑀢𑀺. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀫𑀳𑀸𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀺𑀬𑁄 𑀔𑁄 𑀉𑀤𑀓𑁄 𑀭𑀸𑀫𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄. 𑀲𑀘𑁂 𑀳𑀺 𑀲𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀲𑀼𑀡𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬, 𑀔𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀆𑀚𑀸𑀦𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀸’𑀢𑀺.

978\. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀦𑀼 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁 𑀧𑀞𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀤𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁; 𑀓𑁄 𑀇𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀔𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀫𑁂𑀯 𑀆𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀢𑀻’𑀢𑀺? 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀩𑀳𑀼𑀓𑀸𑀭𑀸 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑁂 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀯𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽, 𑀬𑁂 𑀫𑀁 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀧𑀳𑀺𑀢𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀉𑀧𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀳𑀺𑀁𑀲𑀼. 𑀬𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀽𑀦𑀸𑀳𑀁 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀯𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀞𑀫𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀤𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺. 𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀫𑀬𑁆𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀳𑁄𑀲𑀺—  ‘𑀓𑀳𑀁 𑀦𑀼 𑀔𑁄 𑀏𑀢𑀭𑀳𑀺 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀯𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀻’𑀢𑀺? 𑀅𑀤𑁆𑀤𑀲𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀤𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂𑀦 𑀘𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀼𑀦𑀸 𑀯𑀺𑀲𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁂𑀦 𑀅𑀢𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀼𑀲𑀓𑁂𑀦 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀯𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑁂 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀩𑀸𑀭𑀸𑀡𑀲𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂 𑀇𑀲𑀺𑀧𑀢𑀦𑁂 𑀫𑀺𑀕𑀤𑀸𑀬𑁂. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀉𑀭𑀼𑀯𑁂𑀮𑀸𑀬𑀁 𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀪𑀺𑀭𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀩𑀸𑀭𑀸𑀡𑀲𑀻 𑀢𑁂𑀦 𑀘𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀓𑀁 𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀁.
</TabItem>

to that | to me | Bhikkhave | this | occurred | this | indeed | Āḷāra | Kālāma | wise | skilled | intelligent | for a long time | of a nature with little dust in the eyes  
Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me— ‘This Āḷāra Kālāma is indeed wise, skilled, intelligent, and has little dust in his eyes for a long time.

976.6 Atha kho maṃ, bhikkhave, devatā upasaṅkamitvā etadavoca—  ‘sattāhakālaṅkato, bhante, āḷāro kālāmo’ti.

976.7 Ñāṇañca pana me dassanaṃ udapādi—  ‘sattāhakālaṅkato āḷāro kālāmo’ti.

And knowledge and insight arose in me: ‘Āḷāra Kālāma passed away seven days ago.’

976.8 Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘mahājāniyo kho āḷāro kālāmo.

Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me— ‘Great indeed was the loss of Āḷāra Kālāma.

Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me— ‘Very helpful to me indeed were the group of five bhikkhū, who attended to me when I was striving strenuously.

978.4 Yannūnāhaṃ pañcavaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ deseyyan’ti.

What if I were to teach the Dhamma first to the group of five bhikkhū?’

978.5 Tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhave, etadahosi—  ‘kahaṃ nu kho etarahi pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū viharantī’ti?

to that | to me | Bhikkhave | this | occurred | where | (question) | indeed | now | of the group of five | bhikkhū | are dwelling | (end quote)  
Then, Bhikkhave, this thought occurred to me— ‘Where now are the group of five bhikkhū dwelling?’

978.6 Addasaṃ kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, dibbena cakkhunā visuddhena atikkantamānusakena pañcavaggiye bhikkhū bārāṇasiyaṃ viharante isipatane migadāye.  
🟢⨀👆⏮(passati) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚻⨀③(dibba) 🚻⨀③(cakkhu) 🚻⨀③🔽(ta)(visuddha) 🚻⨀③🆎(atikkanta+mānusaka) 🚹⨂②🆎(pañca+vaggiya) 🚹⨂②(bhikkhu) 🚺⨀⑦(bārāṇasī) 🚹⨂②🔽(anta)(viharanta) 🚻⨀⑦(isipatana) 🚹⨀⑦(migadāya)  
I saw | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | with the divine | eye | purified | surpassing the human | of the group of five | bhikkhū | in Bārāṇasī | dwelling | at Isipatana | in the Deer Park  
I saw, Bhikkhave, with my divine eye, purified and surpassing that of humans, the group of five bhikkhū dwelling in Bārāṇasī, at Isipatana in the Deer Park.

978.7 Atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhave, uruvelāyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yena bārāṇasī tena cārikaṃ pakkamiṃ.  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚺⨀⑦(uruvelā) ⏏️🆎(yathā+abhiranta) 🔽(tvā)(viharati) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚺⨀①(bārāṇasī) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🚺⨀②(cārikā) 🟢⨀👆⏮(pakkamati)  
then | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | at Uruvelā | as long as I wished | having stayed | by where | Bārāṇasī | by there | on a journey | I set out  
Then, Bhikkhave, having stayed at Uruvelā as long as I wished, I set out on a journey to Bārāṇasī.

## Upaka the Ājīvaka

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
979\. Then, Bhikkhave, Upaka the Ājīvaka saw me proceeding on the highway between Gayā and Bodhi. Having seen me, he said this to me: ‘Your faculties, Āvuso, are indeed clear; your skin is pure and bright. For whom, Āvuso, have you gone forth? Who is your teacher? Whose Dhamma do you approve of?’ When this was said, I, Bhikkhave, addressed Upaka the Ājīvaka with verses:

980\. ‘I am the all-subduing, the all-knowing, \
Untainted by all phenomena; \
Having abandoned all, released through the destruction of craving, \
Having experientially understood by myself, whom should I point to as my teacher? \
981\. I have no teacher, \
a peer for me does not exist; \
In the world with its devas, \
there is no one equal to me. \
982\. For I am a liberated one in the world, \
I am the unsurpassed teacher; \
I alone am the perfectly awakened one, \
cooled am I, extinguished. \
983\. To set in motion the wheel of Dhamma, \
I go to the city of Kāsi (Bārāṇasī); \
In this world that has become blind, \
I shall beat the drum of the deathless.’

984\. ‘As you indeed affirm, Āvuso, you are worthy to be an infinite victor!’

985\. ‘Indeed, such as I are conquerors, \
those who have reached the destruction of defilements; \
Evil characteristics have been conquered by me, \
therefore, Upaka, I am a conqueror.’

986\. When this was said, Bhikkhave, Upaka the Ājīvaka, saying ‘Perhaps, Āvuso’, shook his head, took a by-path, and departed.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
979\. Addasā kho maṃ, bhikkhave, upako ājīvako antarā ca gayaṃ antarā ca bodhiṃ addhānamaggappaṭipannaṃ. Disvāna maṃ etadavoca—  ‘vippasannāni kho te, āvuso, indriyāni, parisuddho chavivaṇṇo pariyodāto. Kaṃsi tvaṃ, āvuso, uddissa pabbajito, ko vā te satthā, kassa vā tvaṃ dhammaṃ rocesī’ti? Evaṃ vutte, ahaṃ, bhikkhave, upakaṃ ājīvakaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsiṃ—

980\. ‘Sabbābhibhū sabbavidūhamasmi, \
Sabbesu dhammesu anūpalitto; \
Sabbañjaho taṇhākkhaye vimutto, \
Sayaṃ abhiññāya kamuddiseyyaṃ. \
981\. Na me ācariyo atthi, \
sadiso me na vijjati; \
Sadevakasmiṃ lokasmiṃ, \
natthi me paṭipuggalo. \
982\. Ahañhi arahā loke, \
ahaṃ satthā anuttaro; \
Ekomhi sammāsambuddho, \
sītibhūtosmi nibbuto. \
983\. Dhammacakkaṃ pavattetuṃ, \
Gacchāmi kāsinaṃ puraṃ; \
Andhībhūtasmiṃ lokasmiṃ, \
Āhañchaṃ amatadundubhin’ti.

984\. ‘Yathā kho tvaṃ, āvuso, paṭijānāsi, arahasi anantajino’ti.

985\. ‘Mādisā ve jinā honti, \
ye pattā āsavakkhayaṃ; \
Jitā me pāpakā dhammā, \
tasmāhamupaka jino’ti.

986\. Evaṃ vutte, bhikkhave, upako ājīvako ‘hupeyyapāvuso’ti vatvā sīsaṃ okampetvā ummaggaṃ gahetvā pakkāmi.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
979\. 𑀅𑀤𑁆𑀤𑀲𑀸 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀉𑀧𑀓𑁄 𑀆𑀚𑀻𑀯𑀓𑁄 𑀅𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀸 𑀘 𑀕𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀸 𑀘 𑀩𑁄𑀥𑀺𑀁 𑀅𑀤𑁆𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀫𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀁. 𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀦 𑀫𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘—  ‘𑀯𑀺𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀲𑀦𑁆𑀦𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀢𑁂, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀇𑀦𑁆𑀤𑁆𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀦𑀺, 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀲𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄 𑀙𑀯𑀺𑀯𑀡𑁆𑀡𑁄 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁄𑀤𑀸𑀢𑁄. 𑀓𑀁𑀲𑀺 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀉𑀤𑁆𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀧𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀚𑀺𑀢𑁄, 𑀓𑁄 𑀯𑀸 𑀢𑁂 𑀲𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀸, 𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀯𑀸 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀭𑁄𑀘𑁂𑀲𑀻’𑀢𑀺? 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀉𑀧𑀓𑀁 𑀆𑀚𑀻𑀯𑀓𑀁 𑀕𑀸𑀣𑀸𑀳𑀺 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀪𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀁—

980\. ‘𑀲𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀪𑀺𑀪𑀽 𑀲𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀽𑀳𑀫𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀺, \
𑀲𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂𑀲𑀼 𑀅𑀦𑀽𑀧𑀮𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄; \
𑀲𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀜𑁆𑀚𑀳𑁄 𑀢𑀡𑁆𑀳𑀸𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀬𑁂 𑀯𑀺𑀫𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄, \
𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀬 𑀓𑀫𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀁. \
981\. 𑀦 𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁄 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺, \
𑀲𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁄 𑀫𑁂 𑀦 𑀯𑀺𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀢𑀺; \
𑀲𑀤𑁂𑀯𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀁 𑀮𑁄𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀁, \
𑀦𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀫𑁂 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑀼𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀮𑁄. \
982\. 𑀅𑀳𑀜𑁆𑀳𑀺 𑀅𑀭𑀳𑀸 𑀮𑁄𑀓𑁂, \
𑀅𑀳𑀁 𑀲𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀸 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑁄; \
𑀏𑀓𑁄𑀫𑁆𑀳𑀺 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄, \
𑀲𑀻𑀢𑀺𑀪𑀽𑀢𑁄𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀺 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀼𑀢𑁄. \
983\. 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀘𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀁 𑀧𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂𑀢𑀼𑀁, \
𑀕𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀸𑀫𑀺 𑀓𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀼𑀭𑀁; \
𑀅𑀦𑁆𑀥𑀻𑀪𑀽𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀁 𑀮𑁄𑀓𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀺𑀁, \
𑀆𑀳𑀜𑁆𑀙𑀁 𑀅𑀫𑀢𑀤𑀼𑀦𑁆𑀤𑀼𑀪𑀺𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺.

984\. ‘𑀬𑀣𑀸 𑀔𑁄 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀺, 𑀅𑀭𑀳𑀲𑀺 𑀅𑀦𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀚𑀺𑀦𑁄’𑀢𑀺.

985\. ‘𑀫𑀸𑀤𑀺𑀲𑀸 𑀯𑁂 𑀚𑀺𑀦𑀸 𑀳𑁄𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺, \
𑀬𑁂 𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸 𑀆𑀲𑀯𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀬𑀁; \
𑀚𑀺𑀢𑀸 𑀫𑁂 𑀧𑀸𑀧𑀓𑀸 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸, \
𑀢𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀸𑀳𑀫𑀼𑀧𑀓 𑀚𑀺𑀦𑁄’𑀢𑀺.

986\. 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀉𑀧𑀓𑁄 𑀆𑀚𑀻𑀯𑀓𑁄 ‘𑀳𑀼𑀧𑁂𑀬𑁆𑀬𑀧𑀸𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄’𑀢𑀺 𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀲𑀻𑀲𑀁 𑀑𑀓𑀫𑁆𑀧𑁂𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀁 𑀕𑀳𑁂𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀸𑀫𑀺.

🔽(tvā)(passati) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(vadati) 🚻⨂①🔽(ta)(vippasanna) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀⑥(tvaṃ) ⏏️(āvuso) 🚻⨂①(indriya) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(parisuddha) 🚹⨀①🆎(chavi+vaṇṇa) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(pariyodāta)

Having seen me, he said this to me: ‘Your faculties, Āvuso, are indeed clear; your skin is pure and bright.

979.3 Kaṃsi tvaṃ, āvuso, uddissa pabbajito, ko vā te satthā, kassa vā tvaṃ dhammaṃ rocesī’ti?

For whom, Āvuso, have you gone forth? Who is your teacher? Whose Dhamma do you approve of?’

979.4 Evaṃ vutte, ahaṃ, bhikkhave, upakaṃ ājīvakaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsiṃ—

When this was said, I, Bhikkhave, addressed Upaka the Ājīvaka with verses:

980.1 ‘Sabbābhibhū sabbavidūhamasmi,

‘I am the all-subduing, the all-knowing,  
Untainted by all phenomena;  
Having abandoned all, released through the destruction of craving,  
Having experientially understood by myself, whom should I point to as my teacher?

981.1 Na me ācariyo atthi,

with its devas | in the world

I have no teacher,  
a peer for me does not exist;  
In the world with its devas,  
there is no one equal to me.

I | teacher | unsurpassed

*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(eka) 🟢⨀👆▶️(atthi) 🚹⨀①🆎(sammā+sambuddha)

🚹⨀①🆎(sīta+bhūta) 🟢⨀👆▶️(atthi) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(nibbuta)

For I am a liberated one in the world,  
I am the unsurpassed teacher;  
I alone am the perfectly awakened one,  
cooled am I, extinguished.

983.1 Dhammacakkaṃ pavattetuṃ,  
🚻⨀②🆎(dhamma+cakka) 🔽(tuṃ)(pavatteti)  
the wheel of the Dhamma | to set in motion

🟢⨀👆▶️(gacchati) 🚹⨂⑥(kāsī) 🚻⨀②(pura)  
I go | of the Kāsis | to the city

I shall beat | the drum of the deathless | (end quote)  

To set in motion the wheel of Dhamma,  
I go to the city of Kāsi (Bārāṇasī);  
In this world that has become blind,  
I shall beat the drum of the deathless.’

984.1 ‘Yathā kho tvaṃ, āvuso, paṭijānāsi, arahasi anantajino’ti.  
⏏️(yathā) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) ⏏️(āvuso) 🟢⨀🤘▶️(paṭijānāti) 🟢⨀🤘▶️(arahati) 🚹⨀①🆎(ananta+jina) ⏏️(iti)  
as | indeed | you | Āvuso | affirm | are worthy | an infinite victor | (end quote)  
‘As you indeed affirm, Āvuso, you are worthy to be an infinite victor!’

985.1 ‘Mādisā ve jinā honti,  
🚹⨂①(mādisa) ⏏️(ve) 🚹⨂①(jina) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(hoti)

those who | have reached | the destruction of defilements

🚹⨂①🔽(ta)(jita) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂①(pāpaka) 🚹⨂①(dhamma)  
conquered | by me | evil | characteristics

⏏️(tasmā) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀⓪(upaka) 🚹⨀①(jina) ⏏️(iti)  
therefore | I | Upaka | a conqueror | (end quote)  

‘Indeed, such as I are conquerors,  
those who have reached the destruction of defilements;  
Evil characteristics have been conquered by me,  
therefore, Upaka, I am a conqueror.’

986.1 Evaṃ vutte, bhikkhave, upako ājīvako ‘hupeyyapāvuso’ti vatvā sīsaṃ okampetvā ummaggaṃ gahetvā pakkāmi.  
⏏️(evaṃ) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(vutta) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀①(upaka) 🚹⨀①(ājīvaka) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(hoti) ⏏️(api) ⏏️(āvuso) ⏏️(iti) 🔽(tvā)(vadati) 🚻⨀②(sīsa) 🔽(tvā)(okampetvā) 🚹⨀②(ummagga) 🔽(tvā)(gaṇhāti) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(pakkamati)  
thus | said | Bhikkhave | Upaka | the Ājīvaka | it may be | perhaps | Āvuso | (end quote) | having said | head | having shaken | a by-path | having taken | departed  
When this was said, Bhikkhave, Upaka the Ājīvaka, saying ‘Perhaps, Āvuso’, shook his head, took a by-path, and departed.

## The Five Companions

<Tabs syncKey="paliquote">
<TabItem label="My Translation">
987\. Then, Bhikkhave, I, wandering by stages, arrived at Bārāṇasī, Isipatana, in the Deer Park, where the group of five bhikkhū were. And, Bhikkhave, the group of five bhikkhū saw me coming from afar. Having seen me, they agreed among themselves: ‘Āvuso, this `samaṇa` (wanderer) Gotama is coming, who has become luxurious, given up striving, and reverted to luxury. He should neither be bowed to nor risen to meet; nor should his bowl and robe be received. Nevertheless, a seat should be prepared, (so that) if he wishes, he may sit down.’ However, Bhikkhave, as I approached, the group of five bhikkhū were unable to keep their own agreement. Some came forward to meet me, received my bowl and robe, some prepared a seat, some set out water for my feet. But they addressed me by name and with the address ‘āvuso’.

988\. When this was said, I, Bhikkhave, said this to the group of five bhikkhū: ‘Bhikkhave, do not address the Tathāgata by name or with the address ‘āvuso’. The Tathāgata, Bhikkhave, is a liberated one, a perfectly awakened one. Lend your ear, Bhikkhave! The deathless has been attained. I instruct, I teach the Dhamma. Acting in accordance with the instruction, before long—for the sake of which young men of good families rightly go forth from the home life into homelessness, that unsurpassed culmination of the pure life—having realised it for yourselves through experiential understanding in this very life, you will enter and dwell in it.’ When this was said, Bhikkhave, the group of five bhikkhū said this to me: ‘Even with that posture, that practice, that difficult asceticism, Āvuso Gotama, you did not attain any superhuman state, any distinction in knowledge and vision worthy of the liberated. How then will you now, having become luxurious, given up striving, and reverted to luxury, attain any superhuman state, any distinction in knowledge and vision worthy of the liberated?’ When this was said, I, Bhikkhave, said this to the group of five bhikkhū: ‘Bhikkhave, the Tathāgata is not luxurious, nor has he given up striving, nor has he reverted to luxury. The Tathāgata, Bhikkhave, is a liberated one, a perfectly awakened one. Lend your ear, Bhikkhave! The deathless has been attained. I instruct, I teach the Dhamma. Acting in accordance with the instruction, before long—for the sake of which young men of good families rightly go forth from the home life into homelessness, that unsurpassed culmination of the pure life—having realised it for yourselves through experiential understanding in this very life, you will enter and dwell in it.’

989\. A second time, Bhikkhave, the group of five bhikkhū said this to me: ‘Even with that posture, that practice, that difficult asceticism, Āvuso Gotama, you did not attain any superhuman state, any distinction in knowledge and vision worthy of the liberated. How then will you now, having become luxurious, given up striving, and reverted to luxury, attain any superhuman state, any distinction in knowledge and vision worthy of the liberated?’ A second time, I, Bhikkhave, said this to the group of five bhikkhū: ‘Bhikkhave, the Tathāgata is not luxurious, nor has he given up striving, nor has he reverted to luxury. The Tathāgata, Bhikkhave, is a liberated one, a perfectly awakened one. Lend your ear, Bhikkhave! The deathless has been attained. I instruct, I teach the Dhamma. Acting in accordance with the instruction, before long—for the sake of which young men of good families rightly go forth from the home life into homelessness, that unsurpassed culmination of the pure life—having realised it for yourselves through experiential understanding in this very life, you will enter and dwell in it.’

990\. A third time, Bhikkhave, the group of five bhikkhū said this to me: ‘Even with that posture, that practice, that difficult asceticism, Āvuso Gotama, you did not attain any superhuman state, any distinction in knowledge and vision worthy of the liberated. How then will you now, having become luxurious, given up striving, and reverted to luxury, attain any superhuman state, any distinction in knowledge and vision worthy of the liberated?’

991\. When this was said, I, Bhikkhave, said this to the group of five bhikkhū: ‘Do you recall, Bhikkhave, ever hearing me speak in such a way before this?’ ‘Of course not, Bhante.’ ‘The Tathāgata, Bhikkhave, is a liberated one, a perfectly awakened one. Lend your ear, Bhikkhave! The deathless has been attained. I instruct, I teach the Dhamma. Acting in accordance with the instruction, before long—for the sake of which young men of good families rightly go forth from the home life into homelessness, that unsurpassed culmination of the pure life—having realised it for yourselves through experiential understanding in this very life, you will enter and dwell in it.’

992\. I was indeed able, Bhikkhave, to convince the group of five bhikkhū. It so happened, Bhikkhave, that I would instruct two bhikkhū, while three bhikkhū went for alms. Whatever the three bhikkhū brought back from going for alms, with that we six sustained ourselves. It so happened, Bhikkhave, that I would instruct three bhikkhū, while two bhikkhū went for alms. Whatever the two bhikkhū brought back from going for alms, with that we six sustained ourselves.

993\. Then, Bhikkhave, the group of five bhikkhū, being thus instructed and thus admonished by me, from their own doing were liable to birth, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to birth, searching for the unsurpassed sanctuary of extinguishment from being born, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of extinguishment from being born. From their own doing they were liable to old age, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to old age, searching for the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-ageing through extinguishment, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-ageing through extinguishment. From their own doing they were liable to sickness, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to sickness, searching for the unsurpassed sanctuary of health through extinguishment, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of health through extinguishment. From their own doing they were liable to death, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to death, searching for the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-death through extinguishment, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-death through extinguishment. From their own doing they were liable to sorrow, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to sorrow, searching for the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-sorrow through extinguishment, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of non-sorrow through extinguishment. From their own doing they were liable to defilement, and having perceived the unsatisfactoriness of being liable to defilement, searching for the unsurpassed sanctuary of undefilement through extinguishment, achieved the unsurpassed sanctuary of undefilement through extinguishment. And knowledge and insight arose in them: ‘Unshakeable is our liberation, this is the final birth (of suffering), there is now no more appearing again (of suffering).’
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Roman IAST)">
987\. Atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhave, anupubbena cārikaṃ caramāno yena bārāṇasī isipatanaṃ migadāyo yena pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū tenupasaṅkamiṃ. Addasaṃsu kho maṃ, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū dūrato āgacchantaṃ. Disvāna aññamaññaṃ saṇṭhapesuṃ—  ‘ayaṃ kho, āvuso, samaṇo gotamo āgacchati bāhulliko padhānavibbhanto āvatto bāhullāya. So neva abhivādetabbo, na paccuṭṭhātabbo; nāssa pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahetabbaṃ. Api ca kho āsanaṃ ṭhapetabbaṃ, sace ākaṅkhissati nisīdissatī’ti. Yathā yathā kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, upasaṅkamiṃ tathā tathā pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū nāsakkhiṃsu sakāya katikāya saṇṭhātuṃ. Appekacce maṃ paccuggantvā pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahesuṃ, appekacce āsanaṃ paññapesuṃ, appekacce pādodakaṃ upaṭṭhapesuṃ. Api ca kho maṃ nāmena ca āvusovādena ca samudācaranti.

988\. Evaṃ vutte, ahaṃ, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiye bhikkhū etadavocaṃ—  ‘mā, bhikkhave, tathāgataṃ nāmena ca āvusovādena ca samudācaratha. Arahaṃ, bhikkhave, tathāgato sammāsambuddho. Odahatha, bhikkhave, sotaṃ, amatamadhigataṃ, ahamanusāsāmi, ahaṃ dhammaṃ desemi. Yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipajjamānā nacirasseva—  yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadeva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ—  brahmacariyapariyosānaṃ diṭṭheva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharissathā’ti. Evaṃ vutte, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū maṃ etadavocuṃ—  ‘tāyapi kho tvaṃ, āvuso gotama, iriyāya tāya paṭipadāya tāya dukkarakārikāya nājjhagamā uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisesaṃ, kiṃ pana tvaṃ etarahi bāhulliko padhānavibbhanto āvatto bāhullāya adhigamissasi uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisesan’ti? Evaṃ vutte, ahaṃ, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiye bhikkhū etadavocaṃ—  ‘na, bhikkhave, tathāgato bāhulliko, na padhānavibbhanto, na āvatto bāhullāya. Arahaṃ, bhikkhave, tathāgato sammāsambuddho. Odahatha, bhikkhave, sotaṃ, amatamadhigataṃ, ahamanusāsāmi, ahaṃ dhammaṃ desemi. Yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipajjamānā nacirasseva—  yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadeva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ—  brahmacariyapariyosānaṃ diṭṭheva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharissathā’ti.

989\. Dutiyampi kho, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū maṃ etadavocuṃ—  ‘tāyapi kho tvaṃ, āvuso gotama, iriyāya tāya paṭipadāya tāya dukkarakārikāya nājjhagamā uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisesaṃ, kiṃ pana tvaṃ etarahi bāhulliko padhānavibbhanto āvatto bāhullāya adhigamissasi uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisesan’ti? Dutiyampi kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiye bhikkhū etadavocaṃ—  ‘na, bhikkhave, tathāgato bāhulliko…pe…  upasampajja viharissathā’ti.

990\. Tatiyampi kho, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū maṃ etadavocuṃ—  ‘tāyapi kho tvaṃ, āvuso gotama, iriyāya tāya paṭipadāya tāya dukkarakārikāya nājjhagamā uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisesaṃ, kiṃ pana tvaṃ etarahi bāhulliko padhānavibbhanto āvatto bāhullāya adhigamissasi uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisesan’ti?

991\. Evaṃ vutte, ahaṃ, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiye bhikkhū etadavocaṃ—  ‘abhijānātha me no tumhe, bhikkhave, ito pubbe evarūpaṃ pabhāvitametan’ti? ‘No hetaṃ, bhante’. ‘Arahaṃ, bhikkhave, tathāgato sammāsambuddho. Odahatha, bhikkhave, sotaṃ, amatamadhigataṃ, ahamanusāsāmi, ahaṃ dhammaṃ desemi. Yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipajjamānā nacirasseva—  yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadeva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ—  brahmacariyapariyosānaṃ diṭṭheva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharissathā’ti.

992\. Asakkhiṃ kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiye bhikkhū saññāpetuṃ. Dvepi sudaṃ, bhikkhave, bhikkhū ovadāmi, tayo bhikkhū piṇḍāya caranti. Yaṃ tayo bhikkhū piṇḍāya caritvā āharanti tena chabbaggiyā yāpema. Tayopi sudaṃ, bhikkhave, bhikkhū ovadāmi, dve bhikkhū piṇḍāya caranti. Yaṃ dve bhikkhū piṇḍāya caritvā āharanti tena chabbaggiyā yāpema.

993\. Atha kho, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū mayā evaṃ ovadiyamānā evaṃ anusāsiyamānā attanā jātidhammā samānā jātidhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesamānā ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃsu, attanā jarādhammā samānā jarādhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesamānā ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃsu, attanā byādhidhammā samānā…pe…  attanā maraṇadhammā samānā…  attanā sokadhammā samānā…  attanā saṃkilesadhammā samānā saṃkilesadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesamānā asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃsu. Ñāṇañca pana nesaṃ dassanaṃ udapādi—  ‘akuppā no vimutti, ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavo’ti.
</TabItem>

<TabItem label="Pāḷi (Brahmi)">
987\. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂𑀦 𑀘𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀓𑀁 𑀘𑀭𑀫𑀸𑀦𑁄 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀩𑀸𑀭𑀸𑀡𑀲𑀻 𑀇𑀲𑀺𑀧𑀢𑀦𑀁 𑀫𑀺𑀕𑀤𑀸𑀬𑁄 𑀬𑁂𑀦 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀯𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀢𑁂𑀦𑀼𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀁. 𑀅𑀤𑁆𑀤𑀲𑀁𑀲𑀼 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀯𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀤𑀽𑀭𑀢𑁄 𑀆𑀕𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀁. 𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀯𑀸𑀦 𑀅𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀫𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀁 𑀲𑀡𑁆𑀞𑀧𑁂𑀲𑀼𑀁—  ‘𑀅𑀬𑀁 𑀔𑁄, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄, 𑀲𑀫𑀡𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫𑁄 𑀆𑀕𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀢𑀺 𑀩𑀸𑀳𑀼𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀺𑀓𑁄 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀯𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀆𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀩𑀸𑀳𑀼𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀸𑀬. 𑀲𑁄 𑀦𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀯𑀸𑀤𑁂𑀢𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁄, 𑀦 𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀼𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀸𑀢𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁄; 𑀦𑀸𑀲𑁆𑀲 𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀘𑀻𑀯𑀭𑀁 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀳𑁂𑀢𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀁. 𑀅𑀧𑀺 𑀘 𑀔𑁄 𑀆𑀲𑀦𑀁 𑀞𑀧𑁂𑀢𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀁, 𑀲𑀘𑁂 𑀆𑀓𑀗𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀢𑀺 𑀦𑀺𑀲𑀻𑀤𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀢𑀻’𑀢𑀺. 𑀬𑀣𑀸 𑀬𑀣𑀸 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀗𑁆𑀓𑀫𑀺𑀁 𑀢𑀣𑀸 𑀢𑀣𑀸 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀯𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀦𑀸𑀲𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀁𑀲𑀼 𑀲𑀓𑀸𑀬 𑀓𑀢𑀺𑀓𑀸𑀬 𑀲𑀡𑁆𑀞𑀸𑀢𑀼𑀁. 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑁂𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀘𑁂 𑀫𑀁 𑀧𑀘𑁆𑀘𑀼𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀧𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀘𑀻𑀯𑀭𑀁 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀳𑁂𑀲𑀼𑀁, 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑁂𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀘𑁂 𑀆𑀲𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀧𑁂𑀲𑀼𑀁, 𑀅𑀧𑁆𑀧𑁂𑀓𑀘𑁆𑀘𑁂 𑀧𑀸𑀤𑁄𑀤𑀓𑀁 𑀉𑀧𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀧𑁂𑀲𑀼𑀁. 𑀅𑀧𑀺 𑀘 𑀔𑁄 𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀦 𑀘 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄𑀯𑀸𑀤𑁂𑀦 𑀘 𑀲𑀫𑀼𑀤𑀸𑀘𑀭𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺.

988\. 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀯𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑁂 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀁—  ‘𑀫𑀸, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀢𑀣𑀸𑀕𑀢𑀁 𑀦𑀸𑀫𑁂𑀦 𑀘 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄𑀯𑀸𑀤𑁂𑀦 𑀘 𑀲𑀫𑀼𑀤𑀸𑀘𑀭𑀣. 𑀅𑀭𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀢𑀣𑀸𑀕𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄. 𑀑𑀤𑀳𑀣, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀲𑁄𑀢𑀁, 𑀅𑀫𑀢𑀫𑀥𑀺𑀕𑀢𑀁, 𑀅𑀳𑀫𑀦𑀼𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀅𑀳𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀤𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀫𑀺. 𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀦𑀼𑀲𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀢𑀣𑀸 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀸 𑀦𑀘𑀺𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯—  𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀸𑀬 𑀓𑀼𑀮𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀤𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀕𑀸𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀅𑀦𑀕𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀧𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀚𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺, 𑀢𑀤𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁—  𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁄𑀲𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀤𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑁂𑀯 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀣𑀸’𑀢𑀺. 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀯𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀫𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀼𑀁—  ‘𑀢𑀸𑀬𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫, 𑀇𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀸𑀬 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀸𑀬 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀭𑀓𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀓𑀸𑀬 𑀦𑀸𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀕𑀫𑀸 𑀉𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀺 𑀫𑀦𑀼𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀅𑀮𑀫𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑀯𑀺𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀁, 𑀓𑀺𑀁 𑀧𑀦 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀭𑀳𑀺 𑀩𑀸𑀳𑀼𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀺𑀓𑁄 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀯𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀆𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀩𑀸𑀳𑀼𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀸𑀬 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀕𑀫𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀲𑀺 𑀉𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀺 𑀫𑀦𑀼𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀅𑀮𑀫𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑀯𑀺𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺? 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀯𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑁂 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀁—  ‘𑀦, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀢𑀣𑀸𑀕𑀢𑁄 𑀩𑀸𑀳𑀼𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀺𑀓𑁄, 𑀦 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀯𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄, 𑀦 𑀆𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀩𑀸𑀳𑀼𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀸𑀬. 𑀅𑀭𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀢𑀣𑀸𑀕𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄. 𑀑𑀤𑀳𑀣, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀲𑁄𑀢𑀁, 𑀅𑀫𑀢𑀫𑀥𑀺𑀕𑀢𑀁, 𑀅𑀳𑀫𑀦𑀼𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀅𑀳𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀤𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀫𑀺. 𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀦𑀼𑀲𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀢𑀣𑀸 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀸 𑀦𑀘𑀺𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯—  𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀸𑀬 𑀓𑀼𑀮𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀤𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀕𑀸𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀅𑀦𑀕𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀧𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀚𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺, 𑀢𑀤𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁—  𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁄𑀲𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀤𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑁂𑀯 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀣𑀸’𑀢𑀺.

989\. 𑀤𑀼𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀯𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀫𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀼𑀁—  ‘𑀢𑀸𑀬𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫, 𑀇𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀸𑀬 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀸𑀬 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀭𑀓𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀓𑀸𑀬 𑀦𑀸𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀕𑀫𑀸 𑀉𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀺 𑀫𑀦𑀼𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀅𑀮𑀫𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑀯𑀺𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀁, 𑀓𑀺𑀁 𑀧𑀦 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀭𑀳𑀺 𑀩𑀸𑀳𑀼𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀺𑀓𑁄 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀯𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀆𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀩𑀸𑀳𑀼𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀸𑀬 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀕𑀫𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀲𑀺 𑀉𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀺 𑀫𑀦𑀼𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀅𑀮𑀫𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑀯𑀺𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺? 𑀤𑀼𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀯𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑁂 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀁—  ‘𑀦, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀢𑀣𑀸𑀕𑀢𑁄 𑀩𑀸𑀳𑀼𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀺𑀓𑁄…𑀧𑁂…  𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀣𑀸’𑀢𑀺.

990\. 𑀢𑀢𑀺𑀬𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀯𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀫𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀼𑀁—  ‘𑀢𑀸𑀬𑀧𑀺 𑀔𑁄 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁, 𑀆𑀯𑀼𑀲𑁄 𑀕𑁄𑀢𑀫, 𑀇𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀸𑀬 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑀤𑀸𑀬 𑀢𑀸𑀬 𑀤𑀼𑀓𑁆𑀓𑀭𑀓𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀓𑀸𑀬 𑀦𑀸𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀕𑀫𑀸 𑀉𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀺 𑀫𑀦𑀼𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀅𑀮𑀫𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑀯𑀺𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀁, 𑀓𑀺𑀁 𑀧𑀦 𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀁 𑀏𑀢𑀭𑀳𑀺 𑀩𑀸𑀳𑀼𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀺𑀓𑁄 𑀧𑀥𑀸𑀦𑀯𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀆𑀯𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁄 𑀩𑀸𑀳𑀼𑀮𑁆𑀮𑀸𑀬 𑀅𑀥𑀺𑀕𑀫𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀲𑀺 𑀉𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀺 𑀫𑀦𑀼𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀅𑀮𑀫𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑀯𑀺𑀲𑁂𑀲𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺?

991\. 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑁂, 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀯𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑁂 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀏𑀢𑀤𑀯𑁄𑀘𑀁—  ‘𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀚𑀸𑀦𑀸𑀣 𑀫𑁂 𑀦𑁄 𑀢𑀼𑀫𑁆𑀳𑁂, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀇𑀢𑁄 𑀧𑀼𑀩𑁆𑀩𑁂 𑀏𑀯𑀭𑀽𑀧𑀁 𑀧𑀪𑀸𑀯𑀺𑀢𑀫𑁂𑀢𑀦𑁆’𑀢𑀺? ‘𑀦𑁄 𑀳𑁂𑀢𑀁, 𑀪𑀦𑁆𑀢𑁂’. ‘𑀅𑀭𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀢𑀣𑀸𑀕𑀢𑁄 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀩𑀼𑀤𑁆𑀥𑁄. 𑀑𑀤𑀳𑀣, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀲𑁄𑀢𑀁, 𑀅𑀫𑀢𑀫𑀥𑀺𑀕𑀢𑀁, 𑀅𑀳𑀫𑀦𑀼𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀅𑀳𑀁 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀁 𑀤𑁂𑀲𑁂𑀫𑀺. 𑀬𑀣𑀸𑀦𑀼𑀲𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀢𑀣𑀸 𑀧𑀝𑀺𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀸 𑀦𑀘𑀺𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀲𑁂𑀯—  𑀬𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀸𑀬 𑀓𑀼𑀮𑀧𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀸 𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀤𑁂𑀯 𑀅𑀕𑀸𑀭𑀲𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀅𑀦𑀕𑀸𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀁 𑀧𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀚𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺, 𑀢𑀤𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁—  𑀩𑁆𑀭𑀳𑁆𑀫𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀬𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁄𑀲𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀤𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑁂𑀯 𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀲𑀬𑀁 𑀅𑀪𑀺𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸 𑀲𑀘𑁆𑀙𑀺𑀓𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀉𑀧𑀲𑀫𑁆𑀧𑀚𑁆𑀚 𑀯𑀺𑀳𑀭𑀺𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀣𑀸’𑀢𑀺.

992\. 𑀅𑀲𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀺𑀁 𑀔𑁄 𑀅𑀳𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀯𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑁂 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀲𑀜𑁆𑀜𑀸𑀧𑁂𑀢𑀼𑀁. 𑀤𑁆𑀯𑁂𑀧𑀺 𑀲𑀼𑀤𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀑𑀯𑀤𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀢𑀬𑁄 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀧𑀺𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀸𑀬 𑀘𑀭𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺. 𑀬𑀁 𑀢𑀬𑁄 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀧𑀺𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀸𑀬 𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀆𑀳𑀭𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺 𑀢𑁂𑀦 𑀙𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀬𑀸𑀧𑁂𑀫. 𑀢𑀬𑁄𑀧𑀺 𑀲𑀼𑀤𑀁, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀑𑀯𑀤𑀸𑀫𑀺, 𑀤𑁆𑀯𑁂 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀧𑀺𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀸𑀬 𑀘𑀭𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺. 𑀬𑀁 𑀤𑁆𑀯𑁂 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀧𑀺𑀡𑁆𑀟𑀸𑀬 𑀘𑀭𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀆𑀳𑀭𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺 𑀢𑁂𑀦 𑀙𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀬𑀸𑀧𑁂𑀫.

993\. 𑀅𑀣 𑀔𑁄, 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀯𑁂, 𑀧𑀜𑁆𑀘𑀯𑀕𑁆𑀕𑀺𑀬𑀸 𑀪𑀺𑀓𑁆𑀔𑀽 𑀫𑀬𑀸 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀑𑀯𑀤𑀺𑀬𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀸 𑀏𑀯𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀲𑀸𑀲𑀺𑀬𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀸 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀸 𑀅𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀕𑀫𑀁𑀲𑀼, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀸 𑀚𑀭𑀸𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀚𑀭𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀸 𑀅𑀚𑀭𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀕𑀫𑀁𑀲𑀼, 𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀩𑁆𑀬𑀸𑀥𑀺𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀸…𑀧𑁂…  𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀫𑀭𑀡𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀸…  𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑁄𑀓𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀸…  𑀅𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑀸 𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀸 𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑁂𑀲𑀥𑀫𑁆𑀫𑁂 𑀆𑀤𑀻𑀦𑀯𑀁 𑀯𑀺𑀤𑀺𑀢𑁆𑀯𑀸 𑀅𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀧𑀭𑀺𑀬𑁂𑀲𑀫𑀸𑀦𑀸 𑀅𑀲𑀁𑀓𑀺𑀮𑀺𑀝𑁆𑀞𑀁 𑀅𑀦𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀭𑀁 𑀬𑁄𑀕𑀓𑁆𑀔𑁂𑀫𑀁 𑀦𑀺𑀩𑁆𑀩𑀸𑀦𑀁 𑀅𑀚𑁆𑀛𑀕𑀫𑀁𑀲𑀼. 𑀜𑀸𑀡𑀜𑁆𑀘 𑀧𑀦 𑀦𑁂𑀲𑀁 𑀤𑀲𑁆𑀲𑀦𑀁 𑀉𑀤𑀧𑀸𑀤𑀺—  ‘𑀅𑀓𑀼𑀧𑁆𑀧𑀸 𑀦𑁄 𑀯𑀺𑀫𑀼𑀢𑁆𑀢𑀺, 𑀅𑀬𑀫𑀦𑁆𑀢𑀺𑀫𑀸 𑀚𑀸𑀢𑀺, 𑀦𑀢𑁆𑀣𑀺 𑀤𑀸𑀦𑀺 𑀧𑀼𑀦𑀩𑁆𑀪𑀯𑁄’𑀢𑀺.
</TabItem>

987.1 Atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhave, anupubbena cārikaṃ caramāno yena bārāṇasī isipatanaṃ migadāyo yena pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū tenupasaṅkamiṃ.  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚻⨀③(anupubba) 🚺⨀②(cārikā) 🚹⨀①🔽(māna)(caramāna) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚺⨀①(bārāṇasī) 🚻⨀①(isipatana) 🚹⨀①(migadāya) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ya) 🚹⨂①🆎(pañca+vaggiya) 🚹⨂①(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(ta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(upasaṅkamati)  
then | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | by stages | on a journey | wandering | by where | Bārāṇasī | Isipatana | the Deer Park | by where | of the group of five | bhikkhū | by there | approached  
Then, Bhikkhave, I, wandering by stages, arrived at Bārāṇasī, Isipatana, in the Deer Park, where the group of five bhikkhū were.

987.2 Addasaṃsu kho maṃ, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū dūrato āgacchantaṃ.  
🟢⨂🤟⏮(passati) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨂①🆎(pañca+vaggiya) 🚹⨂①(bhikkhu) 🚻⨀⑤(dūra) 🚹⨀②🔽(anta)(āgacchanta)  
saw | indeed | me | Bhikkhave | of the group of five | bhikkhū | from afar | coming  
And, Bhikkhave, the group of five bhikkhū saw me coming from afar.

987.3 Disvāna aññamaññaṃ saṇṭhapesuṃ—  ‘ayaṃ kho, āvuso, samaṇo gotamo āgacchati bāhulliko padhānavibbhanto āvatto bāhullāya.  
🔽(tvāna)(passati) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(añña) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(añña) 🟠⨂🤟⏮(saṇṭhapeti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ima) ⏏️(kho) ⏏️(āvuso) 🚹⨀①(samaṇa) 🚹⨀①(gotama) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(āgacchati) 🚹⨀①(bāhullika) 🚹⨀①🆎(padhāna+vibbhanta) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(āvatta) 🚻⨀④(bāhulla)  
having seen | one | another | agreed among themselves | this | indeed | Āvuso | wanderer | Gotama | is coming | luxurious | given up striving | reverted | to luxury  
Having seen me, they agreed among themselves: ‘Āvuso, this `samaṇa` (wanderer) Gotama is coming, who has become luxurious, given up striving, and reverted to luxury.

987.4 So neva abhivādetabbo, na paccuṭṭhātabbo;  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(ta) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀①🔽(tabba)(abhivādetabba) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨀①🔽(tabba)(paccuṭṭhātabba)  
he | neither | just | should be bowed to | nor | should be risen to meet  
He should neither be bowed to nor risen to meet;

987.5 nāssa pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahetabbaṃ.  
⏏️(na) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑥(ima) 🚻⨀①🆎(patta+cīvara) 🚻⨀①🔽(tabba)(paṭiggahetabba)  
nor | his | bowl and robe | should be received  
nor should his bowl and robe be received.

987.6 Api ca kho āsanaṃ ṭhapetabbaṃ, sace ākaṅkhissati nisīdissatī’ti.  
⏏️(api) ⏏️(ca) ⏏️(kho) 🚻⨀①(āsana) 🚻⨀①🔽(tabba)(ṭhapetabba) ⏏️(sace) 🟢⨀🤟⏭(ākaṅkhati) 🟢⨀🤟⏭(nisīdati) ⏏️(iti)  
but | rather | indeed | a seat | should be prepared | if | he wishes | he will sit down | (end quote)  
Nevertheless, a seat should be prepared, (so that) if he wishes, he may sit down.’

987.7 Yathā yathā kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, upasaṅkamiṃ tathā tathā pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū nāsakkhiṃsu sakāya katikāya saṇṭhātuṃ.  
⏏️(yathā) ⏏️(yathā) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🟢⨀👆⏮(upasaṅkamati) ⏏️(tathā) ⏏️(tathā) 🚹⨂①🆎(pañca+vaggiya) 🚹⨂①(bhikkhu) ⏏️(na) 🟢⨂🤟⏮(sakkoti) 🚺⨀⑦(saka) 🚺⨀⑦(katikā) 🔽(tuṃ)(saṇṭhāti)  
as | as | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | approached | so | so | of the group of five | bhikkhū | not | were able | in their own | agreement | to keep  
However, Bhikkhave, as I approached, the group of five bhikkhū were unable to keep their own agreement.

987.8 Appekacce maṃ paccuggantvā pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahesuṃ, appekacce āsanaṃ paññapesuṃ, appekacce pādodakaṃ upaṭṭhapesuṃ.  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨂①🆎(api+ekacca) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) 🔽(tvā)(paccuggacchati) 🚻⨀②🆎(patta+cīvara) 🟢⨂🤟⏮(paṭiggaṇhāti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①🆎(api+ekacca) 🚻⨀②(āsana) 🟠⨂🤟⏮(paññāpeti) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①🆎(api+ekacca) 🚹⨀②🆎(pāda+udaka) 🟠⨂🤟⏮(upaṭṭhapeti)  
some | me | having come forward to meet | bowl and robes | received | some | a seat | prepared | some | water for the feet | set out  
Some came forward to meet me, received my bowl and robe, some prepared a seat, some set out water for my feet.

987.9 Api ca kho maṃ nāmena ca āvusovādena ca samudācaranti.  
⏏️(api) ⏏️(ca) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) 🚻⨀③(nāma) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀③🆎(āvuso+vāda) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(samudācarati)  
but | rather | indeed | me | by name | and | with the address ‘āvuso’ | and | they address  
But they addressed me by name and with the address ‘āvuso’.

988.1 Evaṃ vutte, ahaṃ, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiye bhikkhū etadavocaṃ—  ‘mā, bhikkhave, tathāgataṃ nāmena ca āvusovādena ca samudācaratha.  
⏏️(evaṃ) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(vutta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨂②🆎(pañca+vaggiya) 🚹⨂②(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(vadati) ⏏️(mā) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀②🆎(tathā+gata) 🚻⨀③(nāma) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀③🆎(āvuso+vāda) ⏏️(ca) 🟢⨂🤘⏹(samudācarati)

When this was said, I, Bhikkhave, said this to the group of five bhikkhū: ‘Bhikkhave, do not address the Tathāgata by name or with the address ‘āvuso’.

988.2 Arahaṃ, bhikkhave, tathāgato sammāsambuddho.  
🚹⨀①(arahant) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀①🆎(tathā+gata) 🚹⨀①🆎(sammā+sambuddha)  
a liberated one | Bhikkhave | the Tathāgata | a perfectly awakened one  
The Tathāgata, Bhikkhave, is a liberated one, a perfectly awakened one.

988.3 Odahatha, bhikkhave, sotaṃ, amatamadhigataṃ, ahamanusāsāmi, ahaṃ dhammaṃ desemi.  
🟢⨂🤘⏹(odahati) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚻⨀②(sota) 🚻⨀①🔽(ta)🆎(na+mata) 🚻⨀①🔽(ta)(adhigata) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🟢⨀👆▶️(anusāsati) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨀②(dhamma) 🟠⨀👆▶️(deseti)  
lend | Bhikkhave | the ear | the deathless | has been attained | I | instruct | I | the Dhamma | teach  
Lend your ear, Bhikkhave! The deathless has been attained. I instruct, I teach the Dhamma.

988.4 Yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipajjamānā nacirasseva—  yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadeva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ—  brahmacariyapariyosānaṃ diṭṭheva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharissathā’ti.  
⏏️🆎(yathā+anusiṭṭha) ⏏️(tathā) 🚹⨂①🔽(māna)(paṭipajjamāna) ⏏️(na) ⏏️(cirassa) ⏏️(eva) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀⑥(ya) 🚹⨀④(attha) 🚹⨂①🆎(kula+putta) ⏏️(sammā) ⏏️(eva) 🚻⨀⑤(agāra) 🚻⨀②🆎(na+agāriya) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(pabbajati) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(ta) 🚻⨀②🆎(na+uttara) 🚻⨀②🆎(brahma+cariya+pariyosāna) 🚹⨀⑦🔽(ta)(diṭṭha) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀⑦(dhamma) ⏏️(sayaṃ) 🚺⨀③(abhiññā) 🔽(tvā)(sacchikaroti) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨂🤘⏭(viharati) ⏏️(iti)  
as instructed | so | practising | not | long | just | of which | for the sake | young men of good families | rightly | just | from household life | to homeless state | go forth | that | unsurpassed | culmination of the pure life | in the seen | very | life | for yourselves | through experiential understanding | having realised | entering | you will dwell | (end quote)  
Acting in accordance with the instruction, before long—for the sake of which young men of good families rightly go forth from the home life into homelessness, that unsurpassed culmination of the pure life—having realised it for yourselves through experiential understanding in this very life, you will enter and dwell in it.’

988.5 Evaṃ vutte, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū maṃ etadavocuṃ—  ‘tāyapi kho tvaṃ, āvuso gotama, iriyāya tāya paṭipadāya tāya dukkarakārikāya nājjhagamā uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisesaṃ, kiṃ pana tvaṃ etarahi bāhulliko padhānavibbhanto āvatto bāhullāya adhigamissasi uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisesan’ti?  
⏏️(evaṃ) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(vutta) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨂①🆎(pañca+vaggiya) 🚹⨂①(bhikkhu) *️⃣👆⚧⨀②(ahaṃ) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨂🤟⏮(vadati) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀③(ta) ⏏️(api) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) ⏏️(āvuso) 🚹⨀⓪(gotama) 🚺⨀③(iriyā) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀③(ta) 🚺⨀③(paṭipadā) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀③(ta) 🚺⨀③🆎(dukkara+kārikā) ⏏️(na) 🟢⨀🤘↩️(adhigacchati) ⏏️(uttari) 🚹⨀⑤🆎(manussa+dhamma) 🚹⨀②🆎(alaṃ+ariya+ñāṇa+dassana+visesa) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(kiṃ) ⏏️(pana) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(tvaṃ) ⏏️(etarahi) 🚹⨀①(bāhullika) 🚹⨀①🆎(padhāna+vibbhanta) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(āvatta) 🚻⨀④(bāhulla) 🟢⨀🤘⏭(adhigacchati) ⏏️(uttari) 🚹⨀⑤🆎(manussa+dhamma) 🚹⨀②🆎(alaṃ+ariya+ñāṇa+dassana+visesa) ⏏️(iti)

When this was said, Bhikkhave, the group of five bhikkhū said this to me: ‘Even with that posture, that practice, that difficult asceticism, Āvuso Gotama, you did not attain any superhuman state, any distinction in knowledge and vision worthy of the liberated. How then will you now, having become luxurious, given up striving, and reverted to luxury, attain any superhuman state, any distinction in knowledge and vision worthy of the liberated?’

988.6 Evaṃ vutte, ahaṃ, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiye bhikkhū etadavocaṃ—  ‘na, bhikkhave, tathāgato bāhulliko, na padhānavibbhanto, na āvatto bāhullāya.

When this was said, I, Bhikkhave, said this to the group of five bhikkhū: ‘Bhikkhave, the Tathāgata is not luxurious, nor has he given up striving, nor has he reverted to luxury.

988.7 Arahaṃ, bhikkhave, tathāgato sammāsambuddho.  
🚹⨀①(arahant) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀①🆎(tathā+gata) 🚹⨀①🆎(sammā+sambuddha)  
a liberated one | Bhikkhave | the Tathāgata | a perfectly awakened one  
The Tathāgata, Bhikkhave, is a liberated one, a perfectly awakened one.

988.8 Odahatha, bhikkhave, sotaṃ, amatamadhigataṃ, ahamanusāsāmi, ahaṃ dhammaṃ desemi.

Lend your ear, Bhikkhave! The deathless has been attained. I instruct, I teach the Dhamma.

988.9 Yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipajjamānā nacirasseva—  yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadeva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ—  brahmacariyapariyosānaṃ diṭṭheva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharissathā’ti.

Acting in accordance with the instruction, before long—for the sake of which young men of good families rightly go forth from the home life into homelessness, that unsurpassed culmination of the pure life—having realised it for yourselves through experiential understanding in this very life, you will enter and dwell in it.’

989.1 Dutiyampi kho, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū maṃ etadavocuṃ—  ‘tāyapi kho tvaṃ, āvuso gotama, iriyāya tāya paṭipadāya tāya dukkarakārikāya nājjhagamā uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisesaṃ, kiṃ pana tvaṃ etarahi bāhulliko padhānavibbhanto āvatto bāhullāya adhigamissasi uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisesan’ti?

A second time, Bhikkhave, the group of five bhikkhū said this to me: ‘Even with that posture, that practice, that difficult asceticism, Āvuso Gotama, you did not attain any superhuman state, any distinction in knowledge and vision worthy of the liberated. How then will you now, having become luxurious, given up striving, and reverted to luxury, attain any superhuman state, any distinction in knowledge and vision worthy of the liberated?’

989.2 Dutiyampi kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiye bhikkhū etadavocaṃ—  ‘na, bhikkhave, tathāgato bāhulliko…pe…  upasampajja viharissathā’ti.  
🚻⨀②(dutiya) ⏏️(api) ⏏️(kho) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨂②🆎(pañca+vaggiya) 🚹⨂②(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(vadati) ⏏️(na) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀①🆎(tathā+gata) 🚹⨀①(bāhullika) ⏏️(peyyāla) 🔽(ya)(upasampajjati) 🟢⨂🤘⏭(viharati) ⏏️(iti)  
a second time | too | indeed | I | Bhikkhave | of the group of five | bhikkhū | this | said | not | Bhikkhave | the Tathāgata | luxurious | (and so on) | entering | you will dwell | (end quote)  
A second time, I, Bhikkhave, said this to the group of five bhikkhū: ‘Bhikkhave, the Tathāgata is not luxurious, nor has he given up striving, nor has he reverted to luxury. The Tathāgata, Bhikkhave, is a liberated one, a perfectly awakened one. Lend your ear, Bhikkhave! The deathless has been attained. I instruct, I teach the Dhamma. Acting in accordance with the instruction, before long—for the sake of which young men of good families rightly go forth from the home life into homelessness, that unsurpassed culmination of the pure life—having realised it for yourselves through experiential understanding in this very life, you will enter and dwell in it.’

990.1 Tatiyampi kho, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū maṃ etadavocuṃ—  ‘tāyapi kho tvaṃ, āvuso gotama, iriyāya tāya paṭipadāya tāya dukkarakārikāya nājjhagamā uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisesaṃ, kiṃ pana tvaṃ etarahi bāhulliko padhānavibbhanto āvatto bāhullāya adhigamissasi uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisesan’ti?

A third time, Bhikkhave, the group of five bhikkhū said this to me: ‘Even with that posture, that practice, that difficult asceticism, Āvuso Gotama, you did not attain any superhuman state, any distinction in knowledge and vision worthy of the liberated. How then will you now, having become luxurious, given up striving, and reverted to luxury, attain any superhuman state, any distinction in knowledge and vision worthy of the liberated?’

991.1 Evaṃ vutte, ahaṃ, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiye bhikkhū etadavocaṃ—  ‘abhijānātha me no tumhe, bhikkhave, ito pubbe evarūpaṃ pabhāvitametan’ti?  
⏏️(evaṃ) 🚻⨀⑦🔽(ta)(vutta) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(ahaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨂②🆎(pañca+vaggiya) 🚹⨂②(bhikkhu) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) 🟢⨀👆⏮(vadati) 🟢⨂🤘▶️(abhijānāti) *️⃣👆⚧⨀⑥(ahaṃ) ⏏️(no) *️⃣🤘⚧⨂①(tvaṃ) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) ⏏️(ito) ⏏️(pubbe) 🚻⨀②🆎(evaṃ+rūpa) 🚻⨀②🔽(ta)(pabhāvita) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀②(eta) ⏏️(iti)

When this was said, I, Bhikkhave, said this to the group of five bhikkhū: ‘Do you recall, Bhikkhave, ever hearing me speak in such a way before this?’

991.2 ‘No hetaṃ, bhante’.

not | indeed | this | Bhante  
‘Of course not, Bhante.’

991.3 ‘Arahaṃ, bhikkhave, tathāgato sammāsambuddho.  
🚹⨀①(arahant) 🚹⨂⓪(bhikkhu) 🚹⨀①🆎(tathā+gata) 🚹⨀①🆎(sammā+sambuddha)  
a liberated one | Bhikkhave | the Tathāgata | a perfectly awakened one  
‘The Tathāgata, Bhikkhave, is a liberated one, a perfectly awakened one.

991.4 Odahatha, bhikkhave, sotaṃ, amatamadhigataṃ, ahamanusāsāmi, ahaṃ dhammaṃ desemi.

991.5 Yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipajjamānā nacirasseva—  yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadeva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ—  brahmacariyapariyosānaṃ diṭṭheva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā sacchikatvā upasampajja viharissathā’ti.

Acting in accordance with the instruction, before long—for the sake of which young men of good families rightly go forth from the home life into homelessness, that unsurpassed culmination of the pure life—having realised it for yourselves through experiential understanding in this very life, you will enter and dwell in it.’

992.1 Asakkhiṃ kho ahaṃ, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiye bhikkhū saññāpetuṃ.

993.1 Atha kho, bhikkhave, pañcavaggiyā bhikkhū mayā evaṃ ovadiyamānā evaṃ anusāsiyamānā attanā jātidhammā samānā jātidhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesamānā ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃsu, attanā jarādhammā samānā jarādhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesamānā ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃsu, attanā byādhidhammā samānā…pe…  attanā maraṇadhammā samānā…  attanā sokadhammā samānā…  attanā saṃkilesadhammā samānā saṃkilesadhamme ādīnavaṃ viditvā asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyesamānā asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yogakkhemaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃsu.
